CHECK (..)` (DAO refuses CHECK with 3289); the rule is enforced for ADO and DAO inserts (error names the rule `CK_V`; DAO 3317), is not checked against existing rows (`CHECK (V < 20)` over a row V = 25 is accepted), and is not visible as DAO `ValidationRule` of the table or field. A column-level `CHECK` in `CREATE TABLE` is refused (3292, no table). `CREATE VIEW` creates a saved select QueryDef (Type 0); `CREATE PROCEDURE` with a select creates a Type 0 query, with a parameter and an UPDATE a Type 48 query with a `PARAMETERS` line; DAO lists them by name; `EXEC P2 1` runs the update; `WITH OWNERACCESS OPTION` survives in the saved SQL of a view; `DROP VIEW`/`DROP PROCEDURE` remove them.
- **Records**: q1/ii11-ado-check-constraint/ (satisfied, violated-by-existing-row, enforced, column-level), q1/ii11-ado-view-procedure-owneraccess/; evidence/r-sql-2-3/act-ddl-objects (DAO refusals, per the summary).
- **Open**: subqueries in a CHECK; secured-workgroup cases are excluded. Updates and the stored form: the next note.

### R-CAT-1 II-07, R-OLEDB-1: a SQL CHECK constraint on every write route, its errors, Null, order and AutoNumber (task fq9f)
- **Claim**: A CHECK added by `ALTER TABLE .. ADD CONSTRAINT .. CHECK (..)` is enforced on INSERT and UPDATE through ADO `Connection.Execute`, the ADO recordset, DAO `Execute` and the DAO dynaset, the same on ACCDB and Jet 4 MDB (MDB asked through ADO `Execute`). A row for which the expression is true is written: the boundary passes (`V = 0` under `V >= 0`, `-3` under `V >= -3`, `0` under `0 <= V`). Null passes when the column is not required, on INSERT and UPDATE. An update of another column of a satisfying row is written. The operators `=`, `<>`, `<=`, `>=`, `>` and `<`, a negative literal and the column on the right all behave as written. A Text `<>` compares without case (`'X'` fails `T <> 'x'`).
  - *DAO, a violation*: `Execute` with `dbFailOnError` and the dynaset's `Update` fail 3317 (`0x800A0CF5`, `DAO.Database` or `DAO.Recordset`, "One or more values are prohibited by the validation rule '<name>' set for '<table>'. Enter a value that the expression for this field can accept.", `jeterr40.chm` 5003317, no IErrorInfo, one `Errors` entry). The dynaset stays in EditMode 2 or 1 until `CancelUpdate`. `Execute` without `dbFailOnError` returns S_OK and skips the violating rows with no error: `RecordsAffected` 0 for one row, 2 for three rows with one bad.
  - *ADO, a violation*: `Connection.Execute` fails `0x80004005` (Microsoft Access Database Engine, the same text, help context 5003000, IErrorInfo `IID_ICommand`, `RecordsAffected` unchanged, `Errors[0]` Number -2147467259, SQLState `3316`, NativeError -541462135). The recordset's `Update` fails `0x80040E21` (IErrorInfo `IID_IRowsetUpdate`, Status 131072, Number -2147217887, the same SQLState and NativeError). The provider gives a CHECK (3317) and a field validation rule (3316) the same SQLState and NativeError; only the text differs.
  - *Order*: Required (3314) and a field validation rule (3316) are reported before the CHECK, whatever the column order. Of two failing CHECKs, the one added first is reported, not the first by name or by expression.
  - *File*: a violating write changes no page, except on a table with an AutoNumber key: the AutoNumber is consumed (page 0 and the TableDef's word at 20, `00` to `01`, row count unchanged) through ADO `Execute`, DAO `Execute` and the DAO dynaset, and the next row gets ID 2.
- **Conditions**: ACCDB copies of `r-fmt-1-accdb-empty`, Jet 4 MDB of `r-cat-1-mdb-empty`; the constraint added through ADO in a setup session, the write in a statement session; pages kept.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q30-sql-check/`, 49 questions, 26 asked twice and equal outside the clock fields: `sat-*`, `viol-dao-*`, `upd-viol-*`, `upd-other-col-*`, `null-*`, `op-*`, `ord-*`, `autonum-viol-*`, `mdb-*`. Discriminators: `viol-dao-exec-nofail-multi` (the bad row is skipped, the good ones written); `op-text-ne-upper` (case); `ord-check-before-req` and `ord-valrule-after-check-dao` (column order); `ord-two-checks-za` and `ord-two-checks-rev` (the order added, not the name or expression).
- **Open**: a CHECK over several columns, compound expressions, functions and subqueries; a TableDef validation rule against a CHECK; 3022 against a CHECK; an update of another column of a row that already violates a later CHECK; the ADO recordset's AddNew on an AutoNumber table. (A multi-row UPDATE without `dbFailOnError` and a refused AddNew inside `BeginTrans`: the note below.)

### R-CAT-1 II-07, R-OLEDB-1: multi-row CHECK statements, AutoNumber with a CHECK, Jet 4 MDB routes, literal forms, recordset Edit (task b9zq)
- **Claim**: the errors of each route are those of the note above. Beyond it, on ACCDB unless named:
  - *Multi-row INSERT ... SELECT, row 2 violates* (DAO `dbFailOnError` 3317, ADO `Execute` `0x80004005` with SQLState `3316`): no row is kept and the PK index stays empty, but the TableDef's row count (page 42 offset 16) and PK entry count (offset 67) are written as 1. `TableDef.RecordCount` reads 1.
  - *Multi-row UPDATE, row 2 violates*:
    - Without `dbFailOnError`: S_OK with no error. Row 2 is skipped and row 3 updated (`RecordsAffected` 1 of 2 rows, 2 of 3).
    - With `dbFailOnError` (3317, `RecordsAffected` 0) and through ADO `Execute`: row 1's update stays on its page and row 3 is not reached.
  - *AutoNumber with a CHECK*:
    - A violating DAO `Execute` without `dbFailOnError` is skipped with S_OK. It consumes the AutoNumber (TableDef word at 20, `00` to `01`), and `SELECT @@IDENTITY` on the same Database then returns 1.
    - A violating explicit AutoNumber value under `dbFailOnError` fails 3317 and moves the word by one, whatever the explicit value: from 0 with explicit 10 to 1, next ID 2; from 10 with explicit 5 to 11, next ID 12. The refused row's explicit value is never written as the counter, which differs from the statement-scope refusals of TXN-STMT (r-q29).
    - A dynaset AddNew refused 3317 inside `BeginTrans`, then `CancelUpdate` and `Rollback`, keeps the consumed AutoNumber (word `00` to `01`, next ID 2).
  - *Jet 4 MDB* gives the ACCDB answers on every route asked: Null passes through ADO `Execute`; of two failing CHECKs the first added is reported; Required (3314) and a field validation rule (3316) come before the CHECK; Text `T <> 'x'` is enforced; DAO `Execute` with and without `dbFailOnError` and the DAO dynaset AddNew behave as on ACCDB. No violation changes a page.
  - *Literals* (ADO `Execute`):
    - A constant on the left with `=`, `<>`, `<`, `>`, `>=` and a negative `<=`, and a negative constant on the right with `=`, `<>`, `<`, `<=`, `>`, behave as written; each boundary was asked on both sides.
    - Text `<>` ignores trailing spaces on both sides: `T <> ''` rejects `' '`, `T <> 'x'` rejects `'x '`, and `T <> 'x '` rejects `'x'`.
    - A doubled quote is one quote (`T <> 'a''b'` rejects `a'b`).
    - A non-ASCII letter compares without case (`T <> 'ä'` rejects `'Ä'` and accepts `'a'`).
  - *Recordset Edit* of the checked column to a satisfying value, and of another column, succeeds on the DAO dynaset and the ADO recordset. It writes the field in place and the page-0 counter, nothing else.
  - *Null beside a Text column*: ADO `UPDATE T SET V = NULL` on `(ID, V LONG, S TEXT(20))` succeeds. It changes only the row's null-mask byte (page 45 offset 4095, `07` to `05`) and the page-0 counter.
- **Conditions**: as the note above (`r-fmt-1-accdb-empty` copies, `r-cat-1-mdb-empty`, the CHECK added through ADO in a setup session, pages kept).
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q31-sql-check-2/`, 62 questions, 30 asked twice and equal outside the clock fields: `multi-ins-*`, `multi-upd-*`, `multi-upd-3row-*`, `autonum-*`, `mdb-*` (11), `lit-*` (33), `rs-edit-*`, `null-upd-text-table-ado`. Discriminators:
  - `multi-upd-3row-dao-nofail` against `multi-upd-3row-dao-fail`: continue against stop at row 2.
  - `autonum-explicit-below-viol-dao` against `autonum-explicit-viol-dao`: plus one, whichever side of the counter the explicit value lies.
  - `lit-text-empty-space`, `lit-text-trail-val`, `lit-text-trail-lit`: trailing spaces on either side.
  - `lit-text-nonascii-upper`: case beyond ASCII.
- **Open**:
  - INSERT ... SELECT failing at row 2 into a table that already has rows or a data page.
  - A multi-row UPDATE inside `BeginTrans`.
  - Jet 4 MDB multi-row statements and AutoNumber with a CHECK.
  - An explicit AutoNumber value violating through ADO.
  - Leading and non-ASCII spaces, and Text operators other than `<>`.
  - Several-column, compound, function and subquery CHECKs.
  - A TableDef validation rule or a 3022 against a CHECK.

### R-CAT-1, R-FMT-2 DDL-SWEEP-1 (W-07, task zw4d): Memo/OLE with an inline constraint, CREATE INDEX on indexed or populated tables, COUNTER PK, NOT NULL, Jet 4 MDB
- **Claim**: one DDL statement in a session of its own, pages kept before and after. DAO and ADO write the same bytes
  outside the clock fields, and Jet 4 MDB writes the same TDEF and usage-map bytes as ACCDB modulo page numbers. By form:
  - *Memo or OLE column with an inline constraint* (`CREATE TABLE T (ID LONG CONSTRAINT PK_T PRIMARY KEY, K TEXT(10),
    M MEMO)`): the table's usage-map page gives the constraint indexes their slots first (slot 2 the PK, then a unique
    index), then each long-value column a pair of rows (owned, free) in column order (slots 3 and 4 for one Memo after
    one PK). The TDEF ends with a 10-byte entry per long-value column, `(u16 column number, u32 owned map pointer, u32
    free map pointer)`, between the logical index names and `FF FF` (`02 00 | 03 2b 00 00 | 04 2b 00 00`).
  - *CREATE INDEX on a table with indexes or rows*: physical indexes and their 12-byte stats blocks stay in creation
    order; the logical index entries and names are sorted by name (`ixDesc, ixDis, ixIgn, ixMulti, ixPK, ixU`; `IX_K`
    before `PK`). An index built over existing rows writes the stats `(entries, distinct keys, 0)`: `(3, 2, 0)` for keys
    10, 10, 20, and `(2, 2, 0)` for `WITH IGNORE NULL` over `'x', Null, 'z'`; a PK filled by INSERTs keeps `(0, 3, 0)`.
    Physical index flags: PK `0x89`, unique `0x81`, plain `0x80`, `IGNORE NULL` `0x82`, `DISALLOW NULL` `0x88`; a `DESC`
    column has column flag `0x00`, an ascending one `0x01`. In the catalog only the table's MSysObjects `DateUpdate`
    changes (page 17); MSysACEs, the MSysObjects indexes and MSysRelationships do not.
  - *CREATE UNIQUE INDEX on a table with a PK*: a new physical index (`0x81`, the next map slot, one new root page), the
    columns and names shifted 12 bytes down for its stats block (`(0, 0, 0)` empty, `(3, 3, 0)` over 3 unique rows), a
    logical entry inserted in name order. The page-0 commit counter rises by 2 in a session of its own and by 1 for each
    further statement in the same session.
  - *COUNTER PRIMARY KEY*: column flags `0x07`, the TDEF AutoNumber word 1, PK `0x89` with logical index type `0x01`, no
    LvProp.
  - *NOT NULL*: the TDEF column flags keep CanBeNull (`0x03` Long, `0x02` Text and Memo); `Required` True goes into the
    MSysObjects LvProp only. One NOT NULL column (Long, Text(50), Memo, or Text(50) beside a PK) gives a 51-byte inline
    blob with no AllowZeroLength and no entry for the PK column. Two NOT NULL columns (`A LONG NOT NULL, B TEXT(50) NOT
    NULL`) give 74 bytes, over the 64-byte inline limit: the blob goes to a new LVAL page (44 in ACCDB, 21 in MDB), page
    6 (the LvProp usage map) changes 2 bytes, and page 17 keeps a vacated copy of the row's first form.
  - *Jet 4 MDB catalog*: MSysACEs data on page 18 (ACCDB 41); the page-1 map byte at 4034 (ACCDB 4037); the commit
    counter at page 0 offset 3586. `CREATE TABLE T (n LONG)` leaves MDB pages 4 to 6, 9 and 11 to 16 unchanged.
- **Conditions**: copies of r-cat-1-accdb-empty and r-cat-1-mdb-empty; DAO.DBEngine.120 and ADODB.Connection with
  Microsoft.ACE.OLEDB.12.0; setup in one session, the statement asked in the next; every page kept after setup and after
  the DDL.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q34-ddl-sweep/`, 53 questions, 70 attempts (17 asked twice, equal outside the clock fields):
  lval-pk-* (8), ix-cat1b-* (4: the six-index chain, also in one session), ix-nonuq-rows-* (5), uqix-* (11), pk-counter-*
  (7), pk-long-mdb-dao, create-one-long-mdb-* (2), nn-* (15). The organizer read the stats, the flags, the name order
  and the long-value trailer in ix-nonuq-rows-nopk-accdb-dao, ix-nonuq-rows-withpk-accdb-dao, ix-cat1b-chain-accdb-dao
  and lval-pk-memo-accdb-dao, and the page-6 and LVAL changes in nn-two-cols-accdb-dao and -mdb-dao against nn-text-accdb-dao.
- **Open**: an index over rows that need more than one leaf page; the LVAL page's own layout for an LvProp blob. The
  constraint forms are in the next note.

### R-CAT-1, R-FMT-2 DDL-SWEEP-2 (W-07, task zw4d): FOREIGN KEY and ON DELETE CASCADE, ALTER TABLE ADD CONSTRAINT CHECK, UNIQUE, ALTER TABLE ADD CONSTRAINT UNIQUE, PRIMARY KEY and FOREIGN KEY
- **Claim**: conditions as in DDL-SWEEP-1. Where DAO and ADO both accept a statement they write the same bytes outside
  the clock fields, and Jet 4 MDB matches ACCDB modulo page numbers.
  - *FOREIGN KEY, inline (`K LONG CONSTRAINT FK_T_P REFERENCES P(ID)`) or table-level, one or two columns*: the parent's
    TDEF changes. Its logical index count rises by one (the physical count stays), with a hidden logical index `.rB`
    (type `0x02`, relationship flag `0x01`, the child's TDEF page and index number) placed before the parent's PK
    (`.rB`, `PKP`), and the parent's MSysObjects `DateUpdate` changes. The catalog gains an MSysObjects row of Type 8
    (Id `0x80000000`, ParentId `0x0F000003`, Flags 0), two MSysACEs rows for it, and one MSysRelationships row per key
    column (`ccolumn`, `icolumn`). The MSysRelationships data page is allocated before the child's FK root page. In the
    child, the FK index (logical type `0x02`, physical flags `0x80`) sorts by name before the PK.
  - *ON DELETE CASCADE*: DAO refuses it with 3289 and changes nothing; ADO accepts it, with 3 bytes differing from the
    plain FK: byte 22 of the parent's `.rB` and of the child's FK entry (`00` to `01`), and MSysRelationships `grbit`
    `0x00001000`.
  - *ALTER TABLE ADD CONSTRAINT CHECK*: DAO refuses it with 3289 and changes nothing (alt-check-empty-accdb-dao: the file
    is unchanged). ADO accepts it. The table's TDEF and MSysACEs are untouched. MSysObjects gains a Type 9 row (Id
    `0x80000000`, then `0x80000001` for a second CHECK; ParentId `0x0F000003`; Flags `0x10000000`; ForeignName the table
    and column, Connect the expression, compressed UTF-16). The table's own MSysObjects row gets its `DateUpdate` and an
    LvProp on a new LVAL page (page 6 changes): MR2, the table property `CheckConstraints` (type `0x0C`) holding
    NUL-separated UTF-16 (name, expression) pairs, `CK_V\0V >= 0\0\0`. A second CHECK writes both pairs into slot 1 of
    the same LVAL page, marks slot 0 deleted (`0xd000`) and allocates no page. Empty and populated tables give the same
    catalog changes.
  - *UNIQUE in CREATE TABLE, column or table-level*: physical flags `0x81`, logical type `0x00`, flags `04 04`, as
    CREATE UNIQUE INDEX writes.
  - *ALTER TABLE ADD CONSTRAINT UNIQUE and PRIMARY KEY* (PK `0x89`, logical type `0x01`): the CREATE INDEX rules of
    DDL-SWEEP-1, `(0, 0, 0)` on an empty table and `(3, 3, 0)` over 3 rows; only the table's `DateUpdate` changes in the
    catalog.
  - *ALTER TABLE ADD CONSTRAINT FOREIGN KEY*: the same structures as the inline FK (alt-fk-empty and fk-inline differ in
    the page-0 commit counter and the clock fields only); over 2 child rows the FK stats are `(2, 2, 0)`.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q34-ddl-sweep/`, 34 questions, 50 attempts (16 asked twice, equal outside the clock fields):
  fk-* (11), alt-check-* (6), uq-* (4), alt-uq-* (5), alt-pk-* (5), alt-fk-* (3). The organizer read the parent's `.rB`
  and the changed pages in fk-inline-accdb-dao, the untouched TDEF and MSysACEs in alt-check-empty-accdb-ado, the
  unchanged file of alt-check-empty-accdb-dao and the second CHECK's pages in alt-check-two-accdb-ado.
- **Built** (ddl3a 125f02d1, ddl3b 2a9112ef): the logical index block's bytes 21 and 22 are the update and delete
  actions, `04 04` on an index no relationship uses, `00 00` on a plain FK, `00 01` with ON DELETE CASCADE; the Type 8
  row's MSysACEs grant Admin `0x000F00FE` and Users `0x000FFFFF`. Through ADO an unnamed column CHECK in CREATE TABLE
  fails 3292 and writes nothing, through DAO 3290 (`evidence/r-q-cat-txn-lock/ii11-ado-check-constraint`, seq 23).
- **Open**: ON UPDATE CASCADE and SET NULL; a parent with several FKs or a self-reference (the names after `.rB`); a
  second negative MSysObjects Id (a second relationship, or one beside a Type 9 CHECK); a 1-to-1 FK; ALTER TABLE ADD
  CONSTRAINT FOREIGN KEY through ADO or with orphan rows (3379); a CHECK on a table whose LvProp already holds column
  properties (NOT NULL); CHECK inside CREATE TABLE; DROP CONSTRAINT, DROP INDEX, ALTER COLUMN.

### W-07 / R-CAT-1 (r-q43-schema-lval-index): CREATE INDEX on LVAL tables and ADO CHECK with existing MSysObjects.LvProp
- **Claim**:
  - *CREATE INDEX on LVAL tables*: identical to non-LVAL `CompileCreateIndex` — appends inline map slot `2 + PhysicalIndexes.Count + 2 * LongValueMaps.Count` on the table's map page leaving existing LVAL owned/free map slots intact, re-encodes the TDEF preserving trailing LVAL map pointers, writes the leaf page and `(entries, distinctKeys, 0)` stats, and updates `MSysObjects.DateUpdate` (on empty and populated tables with 1–2 LVAL columns, inline/Null/external single-row LVAL values, single/chained indexes, ACCDB and Jet 4 MDB).
  - *ADO `ALTER TABLE ADD CONSTRAINT CHECK` with existing `MSysObjects.LvProp`*: when `LvProp` holds `ColumnValues` (`NOT NULL` `Required`, `AllowZeroLength`, or `Field.ValidationRule`/`ValidationText`) with no `ObjectValues` section yet, ACE appends `"CheckConstraints"` at the end of the `0x80` name table and appends the new `0x0000` `ObjectValues` section after existing `0x0001` `ColumnValues` sections. If `LvProp` was inline (`NOT NULL`, 56 B), it promotes to a newly allocated single-row LVAL page and shrinks the `MSysObjects` row in place; if already single-row LVAL (`AllowZeroLength`, `ValidationRule`/`Text`, `TableDef.ValidationRule`, or a 2nd CHECK), ACE marks the old LVAL slot `0xD000` and appends on the same LVAL page; if `ObjectValues` already exists (`TableDef.ValidationRule`), `"CheckConstraints"` is appended inside `ObjectValues` after `ValidationRule`.
- **Conditions**: ACCDB (`r-q43-lval-accdb`, `r-cat-1-accdb-empty`) and Jet 4 MDB (`r-q43-lval-mdb`, `r-cat-1-mdb-empty`); DAO (`CREATE INDEX`) and ADO `Microsoft.ACE.OLEDB.12.0` (`ADD CONSTRAINT CHECK`); full pages kept before and after.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q43-schema-lval-index/` (`ix-lval-*` 8 probes + 2 `a2` repeats; `chk-lvprop-*` 8 probes + 4 `a2` repeats; 6 of 6 repeats equal outside `CatalogDate`); frozen fixtures `r-q43-lval-accdb`, `r-q43-lval-mdb` in `probes/fixtures.json`.
- **Built (`alta/r-q43-schema-isam`, `44956b02`)**: LVAL-table DAO `CREATE [UNIQUE] INDEX` (`ix-lval-*` 8/8, including single ascending Long Null key `(4, 3, 0)` in `ix-lval-pk-rows-{accdb,mdb}`), merged existing `ColumnValues`/`ObjectValues` `MSysObjects.LvProp` in ADO `ADD CONSTRAINT CHECK` with inline-to-LVAL promotion and SingleRow LVAL reuse (+2 fresh-session page-0 counter on ACCDB & Jet 4 MDB; all 8 `chk-lvprop-*` byte-exact, 7/8 E2E), and SQL `INSERT`/`CREATE TABLE` (no FK/`NOT NULL`) consumption of `_sessionReservedExtent` (`r-q42-field-rule-and/rule-*` and `multirow-*`). `probes/r-q43-schema-lval-index` `20/25` Matched.
- **Open**: `CREATE INDEX` on a table with a LVAL index column itself (if any), ADO `CREATE INDEX` on LVAL tables, >=2 Null rows or UNIQUE/DESC/multi-col/non-Long Null in non-IGNORE NULL bulk-built `CREATE INDEX`, Jet 4 MDB fresh-session 2nd CHECK or `TableDef.ValidationRule` + CHECK, sharing an `MSysObjects.LvProp` LVAL page across tables on `ADD CONSTRAINT CHECK`, `DROP INDEX`, `ALTER COLUMN`, `DROP TABLE` on non-empty/indexed tables, second negative MSysObjects `Id`.

### W-07 / R-CAT-1 ALTER TABLE bytes (`r-q44-ddl-alter-types`)

- **Claim**: Across ACCDB & Jet 4 MDB (DAO & ADO), `ADD COLUMN` increments TDEF `MaxColumns`/`ColumnCount` (+ `VariableColumns` if variable), allocates 2 LVAL maps for `MEMO`, writes `MR2` `Required=True` for `NOT NULL` even on populated tables, leaves existing rows untouched, and writes `C = new MaxColumns` on later inserts (`add-*`). `ALTER COLUMN` allocates a new `PhysicalNumber = old MaxColumns` even on empty tables, silently truncates narrowed `TEXT`, rewrites existing populated rows in-place with incremented `C` while keeping abandoned old-slot bytes, writes `Required=True` for `NOT NULL`, and is a 0-byte no-op for `ALTER COLUMN ... NULL` (`alt-*`). `DROP COLUMN` decrements `ColumnCount` only (keeping `MaxColumns`, `VariableColumns`, and surviving physical numbers/offsets unchanged), leaves existing rows untouched, writes `C = MaxColumns` with zeroed fixed slots, zero-length var entries, and cleared null-mask bits on later inserts, and refuses indexed/PK columns with `3280` (`0x800A0CD0`, 0 bytes written) (`dropcol-*`). `DROP CONSTRAINT PK_T`/`UQ_K` and `DROP INDEX` remove TDEF index/map/root page (+2 on `0xE02`), `DROP CONSTRAINT CK_V` deletes the Type 9 `MSysObjects` row + `CheckConstraints` `LvProp`, and `DROP CONSTRAINT FK_C_P` deletes Type 8 + `MSysACEs` + `MSysRelationships` + parent `.rB` + child FK index (`dropcon-*`).
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q44-ddl-alter-types/` (`add-*`, `alt-*`, `dropcol-*`, `dropcon-*`; 29 probes + `a2` repeats, all pages kept via `nativeRead keep:true`, reusing `r-cat-1-accdb-empty` and `r-cat-1-mdb-empty`).
- **Built (`qkrw` Stretch 1, merge `69afdee4`)**: `ALTER TABLE` (`ADD`/`ALTER`/`DROP COLUMN`, `DROP CONSTRAINT` PK/UQ/FK/CHECK, `DROP INDEX`) and ADO column `DEFAULT` DDL (`CREATE`/`ALTER TABLE ... DEFAULT`, `SET`/`DROP DEFAULT`, inline/LVAL `MR2` `LvProp`, no backfill, `3000`/`3355` 2-page rollback, DAO `3290`/`3293`) match all 31 `r-q44-ddl-alter-types` alter/drop/ext-alter records and all 8 `r-q49-dt2-draftprops` `ddl-default-*` records byte-exact on ACCDB and Jet 4 MDB; `r-q44` COM compare reaches `51/53` Matched (last 2 differ on deleted Type 9 `DateCreate`/`DateUpdate` under `t9vc`; live COM `ddl-default-*` waits on `gp86`, ADO `3290`/`3000`/`3355`, and `Date()`).
- **Open**: `dropcon-check-accdb-ado` (page 17 `0x88A`) and `dropcon-check-mdb-ado` (page 17 `0xCB4`) overwrite the tail of the deleted Type 9 `MSysObjects` row when shifting the shrunk table row down, leaving its `DateCreate`/`DateUpdate` differing between `a1` and `a2` (both repeats retained for a future `AceClockFields` update).

### W-07 / R-FMT-1 BIGINT & DATETIME2 DDL (`r-q44-ddl-alter-types`, `r-q49-dt2-draftprops`)

- **Claim**: On ACCDB (`r-cat-1-accdb-empty`, format code `0x02`), `BIGINT` (`TypeCode = 0x13 (19)`, `Length = 8`, variable `Flags = 0x02`) bumps page 0 offset 20 `0x02 -> 0x05` and writes table `MSysObjects.LvProp` (214 B LVAL, 3 incremental slots) with `FCMinReadVer`/`FCMinWriteVer`/`FCMinDesignVer = "16.0.7124.1000"`; `DATETIME2` (`TypeCode = 0x14 (20)`, `Length = 42`, fixed `Flags = 0x03`) bumps page 0 offset 20 to `0x06` and writes `"16.0.12600.10000"` (226 B LVAL, 3 incremental slots); consecutive `BIGINT` then `DATETIME2` tables progress `0x02 -> 0x05 -> 0x06`. `LARGEINT` fails `3292` (`0x800A0CDC`) with 0 bytes written. Jet 4 MDB refuses both `BIGINT` and `DATETIME2` with `3001` (DAO `0x800A0BB9`, ADO `0x80004005` `helpContext=5003001`) after stepping `MSysObjects`/`MSysACEs` `UniqueEntryCount` and `0xE02` +2.
- **EXT-READ `DATETIME2` runtime, row encoding split, & II-05 index keys (`r-q49-dt2-draftprops` Part A, closes `L497`/`L940`/`L947`):** ACCDB exposes `Field.Type = 26, Size = 42, Attributes = 1`; DAO reads `VT_BSTR` `"MM/DD/YYYY HH:MM:SS.fffffff"` and accepts ISO strings (date-only -> `00:00:00.0000000`, time-only -> `12/30/1899 ...`), `#date#`, `VT_DATE`, `Null`, and DAO `CreateField("D", 26)` (invalid SQL literal/number -> `3464`; DAO put `""`/invalid/number -> `13`/`3464`). **Row-encoding split (p48 / `dt2-update-invalid-accdb` p46):** SQL `INSERT` literal writes 19 day digits (`%019:%019:7\0`, 42 B exact per II-04 §5.5), SQL `UPDATE` and Dynaset `Edit` write 19 day digits **scale `:0\0`** (truncating fractional seconds to `.0000000`), whereas Table-type DAO `Field.Value` put writes **15** day digits (`%015:%019:7\0`, 38 B + 4 trailing slack bytes, byte 40 non-deterministic across repeats!), and ACE builds the 55-byte II-05 key (`7f` ASC / `80` DESC + five 8-byte chunks separated by `09` + 2 B + `00 00 00 00 00 00 02`) directly over those 42 row bytes — explaining why SQL rows sort ahead of equal DAO rows (`'0'` `0x30` < `':'` `0x3A` at byte 15 in `dt2-ticks-accdb`). `UNIQUE` distinguishes 7th fractional digit and allows multiple `Null`s (`3022` on dup); DAO `Seek("=")` fails `3421`. ADO exposes `Field.Type = 135, DefinedSize = 42, Precision = 255, Scale = 255`, refuses ALL non-Null `Field.Value` reads (`0x80040E21`) and puts (`0x80020005`/`0x80040E21`/`0x80040E39`). SQL `WHERE`/projected comparisons vs literals and `D = D2`/`<`/`>` on non-Null rows + `MIN`/`MAX`/`COUNT` succeed (`SELECT INTO` preserves `0x14`), while column-vs-column comparisons with a `Null` side and `SUM`/`AVG` fail `3464` (`0x80040E07` ADO agg); `Year`/`DatePart`/`DateDiff`/`DateAdd` succeed (`DateAdd` `3464` < year 100, `3071` > 9999) while `VarType`/`TypeName`/`IsDate`/`IsNull`/`CStr`/`CDate` on non-Null fail `3071` and `VarType`/`TypeName(Null)` return `Null`. `PARAMETERS p DateTime2` yields DAO `Parameter.Type = 20` (`dbDecimal`, binding ISO string fails `3421`), and `PARAMETERS p DateTimeExtended` fails `3139`.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q44-ddl-alter-types/ext-*` (12 probes + `a2` repeats), `evidence/r-q49-dt2-draftprops/dt2-*` (12 probes + `a2` repeats).
- **Built (`alta/r-q44-ddl-types`, `3044559e`)**: ACCDB `CREATE TABLE` `BIGINT` (`0x13`, header `0x05`, 3 incremental `FCMin*Ver` LVAL slots) and `DATETIME2` (`0x14`, `TypeInfo=0x00000009`, header `0x06`), Jet 4 MDB `3001`, `LARGEINT` `3292` (`7/7` DAO + ACCDB-ADO `ext-*` Matched).
- **Open**: `ALTER TABLE ADD COLUMN` (`NotModelled.DdlAlterTable`); runtime reads/expressions of `DATETIME2` (`EXT-READ`), and combining `BIGINT` runtime rules below with `EXT-READ`.

### W-07 / R-CAT-1, R-DAO-1, R-SQL-2 (`r-q49-dt2-draftprops` Parts B & C): `2mww` draft `TableDef`/`Field` properties, `qgdm` held `TableDefs`, & `qkrw` column `DEFAULT` DDL
- **Claim**:
  - *Draft `TableDef`/`Field` properties before `Append` (`2mww`, ACCDB & Jet 4 MDB)*: draft `Field` reads/puts `DefaultValue`, `Required`, `AllowZeroLength`, `ValidationRule`, `ValidationText`, `Attributes` (`1`/`2`/`16`), and `OrdinalPosition` (reordering TDEF/Fields on `Append`, ties `5,5,omitted -> 5,5,6`), and draft `TableDef` reads/puts `ValidationRule`/`ValidationText`; fresh draft `DateCreated`/`LastUpdated`, `Properties.Append` on draft, `AllowZeroLength=True` on `dbLong`, and `OrdinalPosition=-1` fail `3219`; `OrdinalPosition=32768` and `Attributes=16` on `Text` (at `Append`) fail `3001`; two `AutoIncr` fields fail `3090`; bad `ValidationRule`/`DefaultValue` syntax fails `3320`; cross-column Field `ValidationRule` fails `3783`; unknown column in `TableDef.ValidationRule` fails `3799`; `Text(256)` at `Append` fails `3017`; `CreateField` type `-1` fails `3259`, `"bad"` fails `3421`.
  - *Held `TableDefs` & `Fields` membership (`qgdm`, ACCDB & Jet 4 MDB)*: `db.TableDefs` returns one shared COM wrapper; DAO `Append`/`Delete`/`Name = ...` update held `TableDefs` immediately without `Refresh()` and `Delete` invalidates held `TableDef` (`3420`), whereas SQL `CREATE TABLE`/`ALTER TABLE ADD COLUMN`/`DROP TABLE` (same or second handle) leave held `TableDefs`/`Fields` stale until `.Refresh()` (`3265` before refresh after `CREATE`/`ALTER`; unchanged `Count` & readable `TableDef.Name` before refresh after `DROP`).
  - *Column `DEFAULT` DDL (`qkrw`, ACCDB & Jet 4 MDB)*: **ADO (ANSI-92)** accepts `CREATE TABLE`/`ALTER TABLE ADD COLUMN ... DEFAULT`, `NOT NULL DEFAULT` (does not backfill existing rows — they stay `Null`), `ALTER COLUMN ... SET DEFAULT` / `DROP DEFAULT` / `<type> DEFAULT`, writing inline `MR2` `LvProp` (`60 B` for `DEFAULT 7`, `0xE02` +1) and populating defaults on SQL `INSERT`, ADO `AddNew`, and DAO `AddNew` (`Field.Value` pre-populated before `Update`); compound expressions (`1 + 2`, `Date() + 1`) and bare `DEFAULT` fail `3290` (`0x80040E14`), mismatched type `3000`, column ref `3355`, explicit `Null` into `NOT NULL DEFAULT` `0x80004005`. **DAO (ANSI-89)** refuses all `CREATE TABLE ... DEFAULT` (`3290`) and `ALTER TABLE ... DEFAULT` (`3293`).
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q49-dt2-draftprops/{draft-*,held-tabledefs-*,ddl-default-*}-{accdb,mdb}` (22 probes + `.a2` repeats).
- **Open**: ready gates `2mww`, `qgdm`, and `qkrw` `DEFAULT` DDL closed.

### W-07 / R-CAT-1, R-SQL-2 (`r-q50-jk44-ddl-vis` Part B `qkrw`): `VIEW` / `PROCEDURE` / `EXEC`, 6-constraint `CREATE TABLE`, & `CREATE INDEX` option kept bytes
- **Claim**:
  - *ADO `CREATE/DROP VIEW` & `CREATE/EXEC/DROP PROCEDURE` + DAO refusals (ACCDB & Jet 4 MDB)*: ADO `CREATE VIEW` (plain & `WITH OWNERACCESS OPTION`), `CREATE PROCEDURE` (plain SELECT `Type=0`, param UPDATE `Type=48`, param SELECT `Type=0`), `EXEC`, `DROP VIEW`, and `DROP PROCEDURE` write `MSysObjects` (`Flags=0x10000000`), `MSysQueries`, `LvProp`, page 0 `@3586` (`+1`/stmt), and page-1 extent reservation (`+1` at Close). `EXEC` of parameterized procedures attaches a 12-byte `MSysObjects.LvExtra` compiled-plan LVAL reference (whose LVAL length word varies across repeats on vacated page 17 rows after `DROP PROCEDURE`: `ddl-proc-create-exec-drop-{accdb,mdb}`). DAO `Execute` refuses `CREATE VIEW`/`PROCEDURE` with `3290` (`0x800A0CDA`), `EXEC P1` with `3129` (`0x800A0C39`), and `DROP VIEW`/`PROCEDURE` (existing or missing) with `3295` (`0x800A0CDF`), writing 0 bytes.
  - *Single-statement 6-constraint `CREATE TABLE` (ACCDB & MDB)*: ADO `CREATE TABLE Child (..., NOT NULL, PRIMARY KEY, UNIQUE, CONSTRAINT FK FOREIGN KEY REFERENCES Parent ON DELETE CASCADE ON UPDATE CASCADE, CONSTRAINT CK CHECK (...), DEFAULT ...)` succeeds in one statement (`MSysObjects` +3 rows, `MSysRelationships` +1, `LvProp` defaults + `CK_Child_Score`; cascades and constraint violations `0x80004005` enforced).
  - *SQL `CREATE INDEX WITH IGNORE NULL / DISALLOW NULL / PRIMARY` on empty MDB and populated ACCDB/MDB*: `WITH IGNORE NULL` writes `flags=0x82` (`UNIQUE` `0x83`, `DistinctCount` excludes Nulls); `WITH DISALLOW NULL` writes `0x88` (`UNIQUE` `0x89`); `WITH PRIMARY` writes `flags=0x89, type=0x01` (`Primary=-1, Unique=-1, Required=-1`). `DISALLOW NULL` / `PRIMARY` over an existing Null row fails `3058` (`0x800A0BF2`, no index left).
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q50-jk44-ddl-vis/{ddl-view-create-drop,ddl-proc-create-exec-drop,ddl-view-proc-dao-errors,ddl-multi-constraint-table,ddl-index-ignore-null-rows,ddl-index-disallow-primary-rows}-*`, `ddl-index-opts-empty-mdb` (13 probes + `.a2` repeats, `nativeRead keep:true`).

### W-04 / R-FMT-1 / R-EXPR-1 (`r-q47-bigint-expr-edges`): `BIGINT` (`0x13`) runtime, index keys, expressions & `PARAMETERS`

- **Claim**:
  - *Storage & row encoding*: ACCDB `CREATE TABLE ... BIGINT` and DAO `CreateField("B", 16)` upgrade `p0+20 -> 0x05`; variable-column row encoding (`Flags=0x02`, 8B LE two's complement -> 21 B row non-Null, 0 B -> 13 B Null) stores `-9223372036854775808`..`9223372036854775807` and `Null` via SQL `INSERT`/`UPDATE` and DAO `AddNew`/`Edit`; DAO out-of-range put fails `3421`, SQL literal `INSERT` overflow fails `3464`, `INSERT...SELECT` / `UPDATE` arithmetic overflow fails `6`.
  - *Index keys & `Seek`*: ASC keys write `00` for Null and `7F` + 8B BE sign-flipped; DESC keys write `80` + bitwise complement and `FF` for Null; `ORDER BY B [DESC]` sorts cleanly; DAO `Recordset.Seek("=", ...)` on `IX_B` fails `3421` for tested numeric and string keys.
  - *Metadata, Variant subtypes, expressions & `PARAMETERS`*: base `B` and arithmetic (`B+1`, `B-1`, `B*2`, `B/2`, `B\2`, `B Mod 7`, `B+B`, `-B`): DAO `Type=16, Size=8, Attr=2`, `VT_I8`/`VT_NULL` (`B/2` stays `dbBigInt`/`VT_I8` rounded half-to-even; `SELECT...INTO` writes `Type=16, Size=8`); ADO `Type=20 (adBigInt), DefinedSize=8, Prec=255, Scale=255` with **`Field.Value` returning `VT_DECIMAL` (scale 0)**; overflow fails `6`, div/mod 0 fails `11`, `CStr(Null)` aborts `SELECT...INTO` `94`. `MIN`/`MAX` keep `dbBigInt` (`VT_I8` DAO / `VT_DECIMAL` ADO); `SUM`/`AVG` widen to `dbDouble` (`VT_R8`); `CLng(B)` succeeds in `Int32` range (`6` outside / `94` on `Null`); `VarType(B)=20`, `TypeName(B)="LongLong"`, `IsNumeric(B)=-1`; DAO `PARAMETERS p BigInt;` has **`Parameter.Type=19`**, stores/reads exact `VT_I8` on `Parameter.Value` (refusing overflow/bad text `3421`), and projects `Field.Type=16/Size=8/Attr=2`; **at ACE execution time**, however, both DAO `QBiSel`/`QBiIns` and ADO `CreateParameter("p", 20, 1, 8, ...)` on `WHERE B = ?` evaluate non-Null `BigInt` binds as `0` (returning `ID=2, B=0, pv=0` and inserting `(99, 0)`) while `QBiRange` (`BETWEEN pLo AND pHi`) returns `0` rows (ADO `Parameters.Refresh` returns `Count=0`; CalcDb binds `Parameter.Type=19`/`VT_I8` and refuses execution via `NotModelled.BigIntParameterExecution` until probed further).
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q47-bigint-expr-edges/bi-*` (13 probes + `.a2` repeats), `evidence/r-q48-ado-batch-edits/bi-agg-{g5-isolate,null-extremes}-accdb` (2 probes + `.a2` repeats).
- **Group 5 `Overflow` (`r-q48`)**: neither `SUM(B)` alone, `AVG(B)` alone, `SUM+AVG`, `AVG+MIN+MAX`, nor `MIN+MAX` overflows on any extreme or mixed-`Null` group (`{min,max}`, `{min}`, `{max}`, `{Null,min,max}`); `Overflow` (`0x800A0006` DAO / `0x80040E57` ADO) triggers specifically when `SUM(B)` co-occurs with `MIN(B)` OR `MAX(B)` in the same `SELECT` (with or without `GROUP BY`) over 64-bit extremes.
- **Open**: `BIGINT` mixed with `Decimal`/`DATETIME2`.

### W-07 / R-CAT-1, R-DAO-1 (`r-q46-dao-ddl-multi`, `r-q47-bigint-expr-edges`): DAO draft & attached `TableDef.CreateField` and `Database.CreateRelation` / `Relations.Delete` bytes

- **Claim**:
  - *DAO draft `CreateField` + `TableDefs.Append`*: across 13 non-Decimal types, ONLY `dbText` (10) writes TDEF `+11..+14` `TypeInfo = 09 04 00 00` (`General - Legacy`); ALL other non-Decimal types (`YesNo`, `Byte`, `Integer`, `Single`, `Double`, `Currency`, `Date`, `Binary(16)`, `Memo`, `LongBinary`, `GUID`, `Long`+`dbAutoIncrField`) write `TypeInfo = 00 00 00 00`. **`dbGUID` (15) is FIXED (`Flags = 0x03`, `VariableColumns = 0`, `Length = 16`) via DAO** (unlike SQL `CREATE TABLE T (n GUID)` where `Flags = 0x02` variable); **`dbBinary(16)` (9) is VARIABLE (`Flags = 0x02`, `VariableColumns = 1`, `Length = 16`) via DAO** (unlike SQL `BINARY(16)` fixed `0x03`). `dbText` with omitted size reads draft `Size = 0` before Append and writes `Length = 0x01FE` (reopen `Size = 255`). `dbLong` + `Attributes = 16` writes `Flags = 0x07` (`AutoNumber = 0`, `Increment = 1`, reopen `Attributes = 17`).
  - *DAO `TableDefs.Append` `LvProp` & `0xE02` commit-step formula*: `TableDefs.Append` writes `P` commits at return (`P` = total default `LvProp` writes across all fields: `0` for `dbAutoIncrField`, `2` (`Required=False` + `AllowZeroLength=False`) for `dbText`/`dbMemo`, `1` (`Required=False`) for all other types), then `+1` deferred commit (~500 ms later), then `+1` at `Close` (returning unused page-1 extent). Verified across `P=0` (`cf-autoincr-*`, no `LvProp`), `P=1` (inline 51 B `LvProp`), `P=2` (LVAL p44/p21 + p6 `00->10`), and `P=13` (`cf-multi-mixed-*`, `0d->0e->0f`, `ID` AutoIncr omitted from `LvProp`, 12 slots on p44).
  - *Attached `TableDef.CreateField` + `Fields.Append`*: writes TDEF column blocks with the same DAO `TypeInfo` (`09 04 00 00` for Text, `00 00 00 00` for Long/Memo) and updates `LvProp` (`+1` `0xE02` for Long `Required=True`, `+2` for Text `AllowZeroLength=True` promoting `LvProp` to LVAL, `+2` for Memo, `+1` deferred at 1500 ms, `+1` at Close); duplicate case-insensitive `'zlong'` fails `3191` (`0x800A0C77`) with 0 bytes written. Attached `CreateField("FDec", 20)` returns draft `Type = 16, Size = 8`, appends `TypeCode = 0x10, TypeInfo = 26 00 00 00 (P=38, S=0), Flags = 0x03, Length = 17`, steps `0xE02` `+1` at return + `+1` deferred (0 bytes at Close; no page allocation), and reopens as `Type = 20, Size = 20`.
  - *DAO `Database.CreateRelation`, `Relations.Append`, & `Relations.Delete`*: identical structure to SQL FK (`r-q34`) on ACCDB & Jet 4 MDB (`+1` `0xE02` at return, `0` deferred write at 1500 ms, `+1` at `Close` returning unused page-1 extent). Enforced relations add Type 8 `MSysObjects` (`Id=0x80000000`, `ParentId=0x0F000003`), 2 `MSysACEs` rows, `MSysRelationships` (`grbit = Attributes`: `0`, `1`, `256`=`0x100`, `4096`=`0x1000`, `4352`=`0x1100`, `2`=`0x02`), parent hidden logical index named `.r` + `('A' + logical_index_id)` (`.rB`, `.rC`, `.rD`, reused on delete+re-add, including self-ref on `Node`), and child FK physical+logical index; action bytes (`+21, +22`, mirrored on parent `.r*` and child FK): plain `(00, 00)`, `UpdateCascade` `(01, 00)`, `DeleteCascade` `(00, 01)`, both `(01, 01)`. **`dbRelationUnique` (`1`)** succeeds on PK, `UNIQUE`, and non-unique FK columns (sharing existing `flags=0x81` index or creating new `flags=0x81` unique FK index). **`dbRelationDontEnforce` (`2`)** writes Type 8 + 2 `MSysACEs` + `MSysRelationships` (`grbit=2`), leaves parent & child TDEFs 0 bytes changed, and bypasses mismatched column types (`3368`). Compound 2-col writes 2 `MSysRelationships` rows (`ccolumn=2`, `icolumn=0, 1`). Refused `Relations.Append`: duplicate name (`'r1'`, `3012`), missing parent (`3011`), missing child (`3011`), and no fields (`3366`) write 0 bytes; existing orphan row (`3201` `0x800A0C81`) and enforced mismatched column types (`3368` `0x800A0D28`) write `+1` on `0xE02` + reserve page-1 extent at failure (and `3201` then writes `+1` at `Close` and bumps `MSysObjects`/`MSysACEs` counts with no surviving relation row). `Relations.Delete` (`+1` at return, `0` at 1500 ms, `+1` at `Close`) decrements `MSysObjects`/`MSysACEs`/`MSysRelationships`, removes parent `.rB` and child FK logical+physical index (freeing root page at `Close`; or catalog rows/indexes only for `DontEnforce`), writing a 10-byte LVAL-map terminator sentinel (`FF FF` + 8 zero bytes) when rewriting a shrunk TableDef page in place while leaving prior bytes from `(end + 8)` onward untouched (`reldel-plain-*`, `reldel-cascades-*`, `rel-multi-parent-*`).
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q46-dao-ddl-multi/{cf,rel}-*` (44 probes + `a2` repeats); `evidence/r-q47-bigint-expr-edges/{reldel-*,rel-multi-parent-*,rel-selfref-unique-*,rel-mismatch-3368-*}` (ACCDB & MDB, all pages kept via `nativeRead` `keep:true`).
- **Open**: Draft `dbDecimal` (20), complex types 101–109, explicit `Required`/`AllowZeroLength`/`DefaultValue`/`ValidationRule` on draft fields, `CreateField`/`Relations.Append`/`Relations.Delete` inside `BeginTrans`, `dbRelationUnique` with `DontEnforce` or existing duplicate FK rows.

- **Claim**: Across ACCDB & Jet 4 MDB, attached `Indexes.Append` + `Close` steps `0xE02` `+2` on empty and populated tables (`Primary`, `Unique`, `Required`, `IgnoreNulls`, composite `dbDescending=1`); a draft `TableDef` with `PK_Draft` steps `+5`; `Indexes.Delete` steps `+1` on an aliased index (no leaf freed) vs `+2` on a physical index (leaf freed on page 1). Errors `3284` (duplicate name), `3283` (second PK), `3022` (Unique duplicate), `3058` (Required Null), `3264` (0 fields), `3125` (empty name), `3409` (missing column), `3285` (duplicate column), `3010` (duplicate TableDef), `3268` (property put on appended Index), `3367` (re-append), `3219` (`Index.Fields.Append` on appended), `3280` (`Fields.Delete` indexed column), `3281` (`Indexes.Delete` PK/FK in relation), `3265` (missing), `3027` (read-only), and `3420` (held Index/Fields/Field after Delete) write 0 catalog/TDEF/data/map byte diffs (`0xE02` `07->09` deterministic across `a1`/`a2`). `Index.Name = ...` on an appended `Index` renames it; `Primary + IgnoreNulls` on an unkeyed table succeeds.

### R-FMT-2, W-07 (`r-q53-conv-abspos-subq-loc` Part A): DAO `Field.Value` & SQL DML scalar conversions, draft `dbDecimal(20)` `CreateField`, and transactional DAO DDL
- **Claim**:
  - *Scalar conversions (`NotModelled.Edit.ConversionNotRecorded`)*: across ACCDB & Jet 4 MDB, DAO dynaset `Field.Value` puts and SQL `INSERT`/`UPDATE` map `VT_EMPTY` -> `Null` (`False` on Yes/No), `Null` -> Yes/No `False` (`0`), text to numeric with half-to-even rounding (`"2.5"->2`, `"0.5"->0`, `"0.00005"->0`), numeric/bool/currency/date -> Text/Memo (`True->"-1"`, `False->"0"`, Date locale-formatted `"2026/03/15"`), and valid text -> Byte/Int/Long/Single/Double/Date/Bool/Decimal. Bad text (`"abc"`, `""`), overflows, locale-mismatched `"$12.50"` (`en-ZA`), and non-BSTR (`I4`, 16B array) -> GUID fail `3421` (DAO) / `3464` (SQL); over-scale DECIMAL fails `3759`, over-precision DECIMAL fails `3761`; `Missing` fails `0x80020004` (`DISP_E_PARAMNOTFOUND`); SQL `1e39` -> Single fails `6`.
  - *Draft `dbDecimal` (`20`) `CreateField` (`r-q46` Open)*: ACCDB & MDB draft `TableDef.CreateField(name, 20[, 16])` reads `Type=16, Size=8, Attributes=0` before & after `Fields.Append`, persists on `TableDefs.Append` as `Type=20, Size=20, Attributes=1, Precision=38, Scale=0` (`Required=True` preserved); `AllowZeroLength=True` fails `3219` (stays `False`); DAO read of non-Null `Decimal(38)` fails `3000`.
  - *DAO DDL inside `BeginTrans` (`r-q46` Open)*: ACCDB & MDB `TableDefs.Append`, attached `TableDef.Fields.Append`, `Relations.Append`, and `Relations.Delete` all succeed inside `ws0.BeginTrans`, are visible in-transaction, revert completely on `Rollback`, and persist on `CommitTrans`.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q53-conv-abspos-subq-loc/{conv-dao-scalars,conv-sql-ins-upd,dao-draft-decimal,dao-ddl-begintrans}-{accdb,mdb}/` (all pages kept).

### W-07 / R-CAT-1, R-SORT-3 (`r-q54-excl-ddl-ix-cmd` Parts A & B): Exclusive DDL, populated `DROP TABLE`, multi-FK/LVAL child, bulk `CREATE INDEX` Nulls/3022, deferred QD+DDL, & 2nd 32-page extent
- **Claim**:
  - *Exclusive DDL & ADO `ALTER` without `DEFAULT`*: Exclusive sessions (`OpenDatabase(..., True, False)` & ADO `Mode=12`) advance page-0 `0x0E00` (not `0x0E02`); attached DAO `TableDef.Fields.Append` (`Long`, `Text`, `Memo`) on empty & populated tables + `3191` duplicate refusal (`excl-tdf-fields-append-{accdb,mdb}`) and exclusive `ALTER TABLE ADD/DROP COLUMN`, `ADD/DROP CONSTRAINT`, `DROP INDEX`, ADO exclusive `DEFAULT` (`excl-alter-add-drop-{accdb,mdb}`) persist as captured. ADO `ALTER TABLE ADD COLUMN` without `DEFAULT` on empty & populated tables writes NO `MR2` `LvProp` for nullable `TEXT`/`LONG`/`MEMO` (only `DateUpdate` + `0x0E02 +1`), writing `MR2` `Required=True` only on `NOT NULL` (`ado-alter-add-text-nodef-{accdb,mdb}`).
  - *Populated `DROP TABLE` & Multi-FK/LVAL Child*: `DROP TABLE` of a populated table with PK, secondary index, and external LVAL Memo (`>2400` chars) advances `0x0E02 +1` live / `+1` close (shared) or `0x0E00 +2` close (exclusive) and frees all 7 pages on page-1 bitmap at close (`drop-populated-indexed-lval-{accdb,mdb}`). SQL/ADO `FOREIGN KEY` constraints assign `MSysObjects.Id` ascending `+1` from `0x80000000` (`0x80000000..0x80000004`), and LVAL child tables place FK index maps before trailing LVAL map pairs (`second-fk-negid-and-lvalchild-{accdb,mdb}`).
  - *Bulk `CREATE INDEX` Nulls/3022, Deferred QD+DDL, & 2nd 32-page Extent*: Multi-col `UNIQUE` indexes permit duplicate partial Nulls (`(10,Null)`×2, `(Null,'y')`×2) and multiple `(Null,Null)`; `WITH IGNORE NULL` omits ONLY all-Null tuples while storing partial Nulls (`ix-existing-nulls-uniq-multi-{accdb,mdb}`). Failed `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX` (`3022`, SQL with/without `dbFailOnError` & DAO `Indexes.Append`) and `WITH DISALLOW NULL` (`3314`) reserve a 32-page page-1 extent before failing, leave 0 index entries, and release all reserved pages at close (`ix-unique-dup-3022-bytes-{accdb,mdb}`). `CreateQueryDef` (`0xE02 +4`) followed immediately by `CREATE TABLE` (`+2`), DAO `TableDefs.Append` (`+1`), or `DROP TABLE` (`+2`) plus close (`+1/+2`) is recorded (`qd-deferred-then-ddl-{accdb,mdb}`). Allocating `>32` pages in one session (`D01..D12` + row = 37 pages) clears a 2nd 32-page block on page 1 when crossing page 32 (`D11`) and releases the 27 unused reserved pages at close (`second-32page-extent-{accdb,mdb}`).
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q54-excl-ddl-ix-cmd/{excl-tdf-fields-append,excl-alter-add-drop,ado-alter-add-text-nodef,drop-populated-indexed-lval,second-fk-negid-and-lvalchild,ix-existing-nulls-uniq-multi,ix-unique-dup-3022-bytes,qd-deferred-then-ddl,second-32page-extent}-{accdb,mdb}/` (all pages kept).

### R-FMT-2, R-CRASH-1 Publication (W-05, task zx68): fixed BINARY pads with zeros, GUID is 16 raw bytes, a row keeps every column, the ACCDB commit counter is two bytes

- **Claim**: a fixed BINARY(n) value shorter than n is stored right-padded with zero bytes; a variable GUID field stores
  its 16 bytes; a row written after its last column was created has C = MaxColumns even when trailing fixed Decimal
  columns are Null (the shorter rows of the r-fmt-2b fixtures were written before `CreateField`); the ACCDB commit counter
  at page 0 offset 0xE02 is a little-endian u16 that carries past 0xFF.
- **Conditions**: ACCDB and Jet 4 MDB for the values; ACCDB for the counter (the Jet 4 MDB carry past 0xFF is not seen
  and stays refused); DAO, ADO and SQL writes.
- **Records**: retained fixture `3616122c` (`probes/r-oledb-odbc/create-v2-accdb`, Items.Bin: 7 bytes padded to 16;
  Items.G), `evidence/r-oledb-odbc/txn-held-rows` calls 149-177; `evidence/r-fmt-1/create-accdb-guid`; the row-rebuild
  corpus test over all retained fixtures (`probes/r-fmt-2b/create-w-accdb` for the pre-column rows);
  `evidence/r-q19-growth-open/split-uncovered-right-leaf-below` page 0 (0x05B5 to 0x0940), `evidence/r-q8-header-counter`.
  Built in publication (18c274e3).
- **Open**: an empty BINARY value; the counter at 0xFFFF. A fixed field set to Null: see the r-q36 note below.
- **Built (`w22-isam-edit-idx`, `8e3cc359`, 2026-11-28):** `SameWidthWriter` (`RowWriter.cs`) rewrites rows with `envelope.ColumnCount < table.MaxColumns` on intact tables (`Columns.Count == MaxColumns`) to `C = MaxColumns` (`EncodedField.Null`/`false` for untouched added columns), resolving `UPDATE` after `ALTER TABLE ADD COLUMN` (`r-str-ado-act/str-schema-change` #29..#31) while keeping dropped-gap refusals (`Columns.Count != MaxColumns`); `UsageMaps.ContainsPage` + `RowBinding` `OwnedMap` check emits `CursorFailure.DeletedRecord` (`3167`) after page-retiring `DeleteEveryRow` (`r-cursor-1b/requery-empty-to-populated` #50/#58/#109); unrecorded chained-LVAL recordset `Edit/Update` & MDB chained Memo stay refused.

### R-FMT-2, R-CRASH-1 r-q40 (W-05, task 3vkh): Jet 4 MDB steps the page-0 counter at 0xE02 as ACCDB does

- **Claim**: on Jet 4 MDB, page 0 `[0xE02, 0xE04)` is the same little-endian u16 commit counter as on ACCDB and steps
  by the same rule; every MDB and ACCDB twin agrees at every point, and the MDB counter carries (`01FE`, `01FF`,
  `0200`, `0201`).

| Statement or event (shared open) | While open | At Close |
| --- | --- | --- |
| CREATE TABLE, CREATE [UNIQUE] INDEX (empty or populated), ALTER TABLE | +1 per statement | +1 when page 1 changed (a reservation returned, ADD CONSTRAINT), else +0 (ADD COLUMN) |
| SQL INSERT (1, 5 or 80 rows) | +1 per statement, not per row or page | +1 when page 1 changed, else +0 |
| SQL UPDATE, DELETE | +0 for no row, else +1 per statement | +1 when a DELETE emptied a page returned in page 1, else +0 |
| DAO recordset AddNew, Edit, Delete, Update | +1 per burst (writes under 500 ms apart coalesce; 1,500 ms apart step each) | +1 for a page-1 return or a deferred TDEF row count, else +0 |
| BeginTrans and CommitTrans with writes; Rollback; no write | +1 at CommitTrans; +0; +0 | +0 |
| ADO Connection.Execute INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE | +1 per statement | +1 when page 1 changed |
| A new empty MDB; DDL in its exclusive CreateDatabase session | `0x0100`; +0 at 0xE02 (0xE00 steps `01 01` to `03 01`) | +0 |

- **Conditions**: Jet 4 MDB copies of `r-cat-1-mdb-empty.mdb` or a fresh CreateDatabase option 64, ACCDB twins; one
  process, DAO and ADO; the whole file kept at every point.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q40-mdb-counter/` (27 questions, 7 MDB `a2` repeats equal outside CatalogDate); r-q27, r-q29,
  r-q30, r-q31, r-q34, r-q36, r-q37 and r-q38 MDB twins agree. The organizer read dml-insert-rows-mdb page 0 (`08`,
  `09`, `0a`, `0b`, `0b`, `0d`).
- **Open**: 0xFFFF; the MDB burst boundary between 0.5 and 1 s; an exclusive OpenDatabase; DROP TABLE, DROP INDEX,
  ALTER TABLE DROP and TableDefs.Append on MDB; whether rs.Close or db.Close writes the deferred TDEF count; why an
  80-row INSERT into a populated table took its pages without a 32-page reservation (MDB and ACCDB alike). Built in
  3vkh (7eac9f1f); the creating session's 0xE00 is `fkn9`, decided by r-q41 below.

### R-FMT-2, R-CRASH-1 r-q41 (W-05, task fkn9): an exclusive session steps the page-0 counter at 0xE00, a shared one at 0xE02

- **Claim**: page 0 `[0xE00, 0xE02)` is a second little-endian u16 commit counter, `0x0101` in a new file, stepped
  by every exclusive session (no `.laccdb`/`.ldb`): the session of DAO `CreateDatabase`, DAO `OpenDatabase(path,
  True, False)` and ADO `Connection` with `Mode = 12`. Such a session leaves `[0xE02, 0xE04)` untouched; a shared
  session steps 0xE02 by the r-q40 rule and leaves 0xE00 untouched. ACCDB and Jet 4 MDB agree at every point. The
  counter carries (`ff 01`, `00 02`, `01 02`), and each slot keeps its value across sessions of the other kind
  (`01 02 01 01` after a shared reopen, `02 02 01 01` after an exclusive one).

| Statement or event (exclusive session) | While open | At Close |
| --- | --- | --- |
| CreateDatabase and Close; exclusive open read-only or with no write | +0 | +0 |
| CREATE TABLE, CREATE [UNIQUE] INDEX (empty or populated), ALTER TABLE ADD COLUMN / ADD CONSTRAINT | +1 per statement, at its return | +1 when the session took a page (page 1 changed), else +0 |
| DAO TableDefs.Append | +1 at the return, and a deferred TDEF write about 0.5 s later (+1), absorbed into the next statement's commit when it comes sooner | as above; the pending TDEF write first (+1) |
| SQL INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE (1 or more rows, INSERT ... SELECT) | +1 per statement, deferred: written about 505-515 ms later by an ACE thread, at the start of the next Execute, or at Close; +0 for no row | +0 beyond the deferred write, unless a page was taken |
| BeginTrans and CommitTrans with writes; Rollback; no write | +1 at CommitTrans; +0; +0 | as above |
| DAO dynaset AddNew, Edit, Delete, Update | +1 per burst: 200 to 500 ms apart coalesce (12 of 12 at 450 and 500 ms), 1,000 ms split; 550 and 600 ms vary between repeats | as above |

- **Conditions**: copies made by `CreateDatabase` option 128 (ACCDB) and 64 (Jet 4 MDB) in the session, or reopened
  exclusive or shared; one process, DAO and ADO; the whole file kept at every point (`nativeRead keep`), ETW file-I/O
  traces on the DDL, DML, transaction and exclusive-open questions.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q41-create-session-counter/` (38 questions, 58 records; `a2`/`a3` repeats of
  rs-burst-450/500/550/600ms and rs-burst-vs-pause); premise on r-q40 create-first-write-accdb and -mdb. The
  organizer read excl-open-accdb's page 0 at its seven points (`01 01 03 01` three times, `02 01 03 01`, `04 01 03
  01`, `04 01 04 01` twice).
- **Timing**: rs-burst-550ms, -600ms and rs-burst-vs-pause vary between `0x0106` and `0x0107` across ACE repeats (a
  non-allocating lazy write sometimes not flushed within a 1,500 ms pause); they sit on ACE's write-behind boundary as
  r-q8 does (`mhzd`).
- **Open**: 0xFFFF at either slot; DROP TABLE, DROP INDEX, TableDefs.Delete, renames and Indexes.Append in an
  exclusive session; SELECT INTO, refused statements and rolled-back allocating or AutoNumber work there;
  `dbImplicitCommitSync` "Yes" and `dbUserCommitSync` "No" there; table-type and ADO recordsets in the creating
  session; ADO `Mode = 1`.

### R-FMT-2 r-q36 (W-05, task bfhj): a leading U+FEFF is stored as written, a row with no variable column pads to 4 bytes before its mask, a fixed field set to Null keeps its bytes

- **Claim**:
  - *Leading U+FEFF*: uncompressed Text, a single-page and a chained long-value Memo, and a compressible column whose
    value does not compress store the UTF-16LE units as written, starting `ff fe`: no escape, no prefix, no error.
    ACE's reader takes a leading `ff fe` as the compressed marker on compressed Text, inline Memo and compressed
    single-page Memo, which is why such values read back changed (the R-ORACLE-1 OR-WIRE note).
  - *Compression ties*: Text with the compression flag compresses when the compressed form is no longer than the raw
    one; Memo only when it is shorter. An inline Memo (64 raw bytes or fewer, decided before compression) compresses
    whatever its flag says; a single-page long-value Memo follows its flag; a chained one never compresses.
  - *Rows with no variable column*: the bytes before the null mask are at least 4, so `max(0, 2 - fixedSize)` zero
    bytes sit between the fixed area and the mask (2 for Yes/No-only tables, 1 for one Byte column, none from 2 fixed
    bytes). With a variable column there is no pad: the first variable value starts at the end of the fixed area.
  - *Fixed field set to Null*: ACE clears the field's mask bit and keeps its fixed bytes as they were, in place and in
    every rewrite (grow, shrink, an overflow move); a field inserted Null keeps zeros. A trailing Null Decimal does not
    shorten C. SQL UNIQUEIDENTIFIER and BIGINT columns are variable (flags `0x02`), so Null removes their value.
- **Conditions**: ACCDB (versions 2 and 6) and Jet 4 MDB; DAO and ADO; SQL INSERT and UPDATE, recordset AddNew and
  Edit; Yes/No-only tables of 1 to 41 columns, 0 to 3 fixed bytes, all fixed scalar types and DATETIME2.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q36-publication-bytes/` (33 questions, 7 frozen fixtures in `probes/fixtures.json`): feff-*,
  zf-*, nullfix-*. The organizer read nullfix-pure-inplace-accdb-dao page 45 (only the mask bytes at 4094 change) and
  zf-fixed1-disc-accdb-dao page 53 (`09 00 5a 00 ab 00`).
- **Open**: compression beyond these ties; a table whose rows have C below MaxColumns; the slack an ADO keyset shrink
  zeroes (nullfix-ado-accdb, after-ado-rs, page 45 slot 2).

### R-CAT-1, R-FMT-2 r-q37 (W-08, task 9dvt): DAO Field.ValidationRule and ValidationText puts on a stored table write only the table's LvProp, page 0's counter and the pages that hold the blob

- **Claim**:
  - *MR2 records*: both properties sit in `MSysObjects.LvProp` of the table's row, in the field's `ColumnValues`
    section; the TableDef page never changes. ValidationRule is flags `0x01`, type `0x0C` (Memo), UTF-16LE with a
    trailing `00 00` (DAO's get returns the trailing U+0000); ValidationText is flags `0x01`, type `0x0A` (Text),
    UTF-16LE without one (also at 256 characters, length `0x0200`). Neither has an `ff fe` prefix.
  - *Order*: existing Names entries, sections and per-field property order stay; a new name, section or property is
    appended in write order; a replacement is written in place. Putting the same value again still rewrites LvProp,
    DateUpdate, the LVAL slot and page 0's counter.
  - *Where the blob lives*: a blob of 64 bytes or fewer is inline in MSysObjects (`[len:u16] 00 80` and 8 zero bytes,
    then the blob; pages 1 and 6 unchanged); one of 66 to 4,080 bytes is a single-row LVAL page (`[len:u16] 00 40
    [slot:u8][page:u24]` and 4 zero bytes). A rewrite that fits marks the old slot `0xD000` and appends a slot; one that
    does not allocates a new LVAL page, marks the old one freed (`09` at byte 0, free space `0x0FF0`, slot 0 `0xD000`,
    payload not zeroed), moves its bits in page 6 at once and returns the page in page 1 at Close. Above 4,080 bytes
    (ACCDB, two pages) the blob is chained (flags `0x00`, a clock-derived LongValueStamp in LvProp and the head page),
    its pages set in page 6's owned map only; replacing it by a short blob frees the chain without writing `09`.
  - *Removal*: `""` drops the property (and an empty section) and keeps its name in Names; on a Null LvProp it writes a
    40-byte Names-only inline blob. A blob that shrinks to 64 bytes or fewer moves back inline and frees its LVAL page
    as above.
  - *Other bytes*: each put is written at once (seen while open): DateUpdate and LvProp in the MSysObjects row, and page
    0 offset `0xE02` +1. A Close that changed page 1 (its 32-page reservation, a returned page) adds +1 more; one that
    did not leaves page 0 alone. `[V] < [W]` fails 3783 (`0x800A0EC7`), `>` 3320 (`0x800A0CF8`), VT_NULL on either
    property 3421 (`0x800A0D5D`), each leaving the file byte-identical.
- **Conditions**: ACCDB and Jet 4 MDB; native DAO, `Field.ValidationRule` and `Field.ValidationText` puts as VT_BSTR on a
  Long or Text field of an empty stored table, one session; fields with no properties, with Description and Format,
  with Required, and beside a table CheckConstraints entry.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q37-valrule-put/` (42 questions, 6 frozen fixtures `r-q37-*` in `probes/fixtures.json`; every
  page before, after each put while open, and after Close); repeats (`a2`) of rule-alone-accdb, rule-alone-mdb,
  both-rule-then-text-accdb, long-rule-2040-accdb and remove-rule-only-accdb equal outside CatalogDate and
  LongValueStamp. The organizer read rule-alone-accdb page 45 after Close: `> 10` stored as 10 bytes, type `0x0C`. The
  earlier setups (r-q29 noan-val-3316-*, r-q30 ord-valrule-*, r-q31 mdb-ord-valrule-check-dao) kept pages only after
  their puts and agree.
- **Open**: a 65-byte blob (64 is inline and 66 an LVAL page; none of 65 is recorded); a chained blob on Jet 4 MDB, or
  of three or more pages; an LVAL page shared with another live row; an MSysObjects page without room for the inline
  blob; the length limits of the rule and the text; a table with rows; TableDef-level rules, a draft field before
  Append, other variant types; why the chained replacement allocated page 48 and not 47; the counter's width and wrap.
  Enforcement of the rules put: r-q42 below.

### R-EXPR-1 EXPR-MUTATION r-q42 (W-06, task nnnh): a field rule with implied operands is enforced clause by clause, and a failing write changes no page

- **Claim**:
  - *Grammar.* In a `Field.ValidationRule`, each clause that starts with an operator (`=`, `<>`, `<`, `<=`, `>`,
    `>=`, `Between`, `In`, `Like`, `Is [Not] Null`) takes the field as its left operand, inside `And`, `Or`, `Xor`,
    `Eqv`, `Imp`, parentheses and `Not`; a bare literal there means `[Field] = literal` (`Not 'a'` is `Not = 'a'`); an
    explicit `[Field]` clause mixes with implied ones. Comparisons chain to the left (`<> 'a' = False` is `([W] <> 'a')
    = False`). Text compares without case under General. Null passes every rule except `Is Not Null`; `''` passes
    `<>` and `Not` rules and fails `=`, range, `Between` and `In` rules (AllowZeroLength put True).
  - *Puts refused*: dangling or malformed syntax 3320; another column or an aggregate 3783; a subquery 3420. A
    `TableDef.ValidationRule` with an implied operand is refused 3320. `And <> 5` on a Long is accepted at the put and
    fails every INSERT with 3802.
  - *Enforcement, the same on all 10 core forms, INSERT and UPDATE, ACCDB and Jet 4 MDB:*

| Route | Pass | Fail, no ValidationText | Fail, ValidationText set |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| DAO `Execute` with 128 | RecordsAffected 1 | 3317 (default text naming rule and `T.col`), RecordsAffected 0 | 3316 with the text |
| DAO `Execute` without 128 | RecordsAffected 1 | no error, RecordsAffected 0 | the same |
| DAO dynaset AddNew/Edit + Update | EditMode 0 | 3317, EditMode stays 2 or 1, CancelUpdate succeeds | 3316, the same |
| ADO `Connection.Execute` | RecordsAffected 1 | 0x80004005, SQLState `3316`, NativeError -541462135, the default text; RecordsAffected stays -1 | the same, with the text |

  - A failing single-row write changes no page; a passing INSERT changes pages 0, 42, 44, 46 (MDB 0, 19, 21, 23) of
    the r-q37 plain fixture, an UPDATE 0 and 46 (MDB 0 and 23).
  - *AllowZeroLength.* After an explicit DAO put of False, `''` fails 3315 before the rule is checked; on a DDL TEXT
    column with no put, DAO reads AllowZeroLength False, yet `''` reaches the rule (azl-false-order).
  - *Several rows* (one shape, multirow): an `INSERT ... SELECT` with failing rows inserts none with 128 or through ADO
    and the passing ones without 128; `UPDATE T SET W = IIf(ID = 3, 'b', 'ok')` with 128 fails 3317, RecordsAffected
    0, and keeps row 1 updated (page 50 changed), as ERR-PARTIAL's lock refusal keeps the earlier rows.
- **Conditions**: copies of the r-q37 plain fixtures; DAO and ADO (Microsoft.ACE.OLEDB.12.0) in one process; the
  pages a write may change kept before and after each write.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q42-field-rule-and/` (36 records: 10 `rule-*` pairs over the four routes, put-forms,
  put-refusals, disc-text-forms, disc-num-forms, edge-forms, azl-false-order, azl-explicit-false, multirow). The
  organizer read multirow-accdb calls 54 to 104.
- **Open**: `ValidateOnSet`; ADO Recordset.Update and Command.Execute; a failing multi-row UPDATE inside BeginTrans
  and other multi-row shapes; other sort orders and field types (Date, Currency, Yes/No, Double, Memo); a rule put on a
  table whose rows violate it.
- **Implemented & verified against `r-q42` and `r-q37` (`nk97-dao`, `6ccdf4d7`)**: `Field.AllowZeroLength` (`VT_BOOL` on
  empty Text columns, stored as `flags = 0x01, type = 0x01, 0xFF/0x00`), populated-table non-empty
  `Field.ValidationText` (Text/Long, TableDef page untouched), 65-byte MR2 blob placement and shared SingleRow LVAL
  page reuse (`qd-sql-*-in-mdb` + `r-q42`), `Field.ValidationRule` per-object readback NUL tracking (first read returns
  stored NUL-terminated bytes; subsequent non-empty put returns text without NUL while disk keeps NUL, `r-cat-1/field-rules`),
  same-column 3315-beats-3316 (`azl-explicit-false-*`), and depth-0 single-table SELECT dynaset edit lifecycle
  (`rule-and2-ne-*`, `r-err-1b/asg-update-without-edit` 3020).

### R-CAT-1 CAT-Q r-q39 (W-07/saved-definition, task zsfm): the bytes of a saved QueryDef

- **Claim**:
  - *No write*: `CreateQueryDef("", sql)` (`#Temporary QueryDef#`) and an unappended draft `CreateQueryDef()` change no
    byte; `QueryDefs.Append` of a draft writes the bytes a named `CreateQueryDef` writes.
  - *MSysObjects*: one row, `Id` the lowest free negative Int32 from `0x80000000` (`0x80000001` for the second, the
    first reused after `Delete`), `ParentId 0x0F000001`, `Type 5`, `Name` uncompressed UTF-16LE, `Owner` the masked
    Admin SID. `Flags` is `QueryDef.Type`: 0 select, 0x10 crosstab, 0x20 delete, 0x30 update, 0x40 append, 0x50
    make-table, 0x60 DDL, 0x70 pass-through, 0x80 union. `LvProp` is written twice: a 56-byte inline `MR2` blob
    (ODBCTimeout 60), then a 90-byte single-page LVAL (ODBCTimeout 60, MaxRecords 0; later queries share that page),
    the row shrinking in place and leaving a 56-byte vacated copy (Flags 0) below it.
  - *MSysACEs*: two 19-byte rows per query, Admin `ACM 0x000F00FE` and Users `0x000FFEFF`, `FInheritable` 0.
  - *MSysQueries*: rows 0 (`Attribute 0`, Flag 0) and 255 (Flag Null) first, then the clause rows in parse order per
    shape (select `*`, columns with aliases, WHERE, ORDER BY with `d`, PARAMETERS Long and Text(n), UPDATE, DELETE,
    INSERT VALUES and SELECT, SELECT INTO, UNION and UNION ALL (option Flag 3 or 1), TRANSFORM, DDL, pass-through);
    Name1 and Name2 uncompressed; `Expression` inline up to 32 UTF-16 units (3 to 32 ASCII units compressed with
    `ff fe`), from 33 units an uncompressed single-page LVAL; after a Text(n) parameter row, `LvExtra` stays non-Null
    (0) on the query's later rows. Allocation order: MSysQueries data page, Expression LVAL page, LvProp LVAL page.
  - *Indexes*: MSysObjects ParentIdName and Id, MSysACEs ObjectId, MSysQueries ObjectIdAttribute (key ObjectId,
    Attribute, Order), single-leaf; the TDEF counters at +67 and +79 only grow.
  - *Commits*: a non-union CreateQueryDef steps page 0 `+0xE02` by 4 at once and reserves a 32-page block in page 1;
    the fifth commit (TDEF row count, a non-select Flags) comes on ACE's deferred timer, at the next CreateQueryDef, or
    at Close; a union writes all five at once. Close returns the unused reserved pages and steps +1.
  - *SQL put*: rows 0 and 255 stay, the old clause rows are deleted, the new ones appended, DateUpdate written at once
    (+1); the TDEF count and a family change's Flags at Close (+1). *Delete*: the MSysObjects, MSysACEs and MSysQueries
    rows and index entries go, an emptied LvProp LVAL page becomes type `0x09` (slot 0 `0xD000`) at once and its page
    1 bit returns at Close; the MSysQueries data page stays allocated.
  - Only DateCreate and DateUpdate (live, deleted and vacated rows) differ between repeats.
- **Conditions**: ACE 2007 ACCDB and Jet 4 MDB from frozen fixtures `r-q37-plain-*`; DAO, one session; single-table
  sources of the shapes named.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q39-querydef-bytes/` (35 questions, 16 `a2` repeats; every page before and after each
  call and after Close). The organizer read select-star-accdb (page 0 `00 04` at create, `06` after Close; page 1
  reservation; page 8 Id index) and temp-querydef-accdb (no byte changed).
- **Open**: JOIN, HAVING, GROUP BY outside a crosstab, DISTINCT, DISTINCTROW, TOP, WITH OWNERACCESS OPTION, PIVOT IN,
  subqueries, several sources, other PARAMETERS types; a SQL put between inline and LVAL Expression; Delete of a query
  that shares its LvProp page or owns an Expression page; property changes after save; chained LVALs; page splits;
  error paths; ADO CREATE VIEW and PROCEDURE; ACE 2010 and later. The comparison rule misses five repeats' clock
  fields (`tpjw`).

### R-CAT-1 CAT-Q r-q45 (W-07/saved-definition, task zsfm stretch 3): shared/external LVALs, LVAL SQL puts, UNION/PassThrough puts, Name/Connect/ReturnsRecords puts

- **Claim**:
  - *Delete with shared `LvProp` LVAL / external `Expression` LVAL*: deleting 1 of 2 queries sharing an `LvProp` LVAL page keeps page type `0x01`, sets slot 0 to `0xD000`, compacts slot 1 upward, leaves page 6 and page 1 untouched (`0xE02 +1` at Delete, `+1` at Close); deleting the last slot turns the page `0x09` (`00d0 00d0`, free `0x0FEE`), clears page 6 owned+free map bits at once, and returns its page-1 bit at Close (`qd-del-shared-lvprop-{accdb,mdb}`). Sole `Expression` LVAL Delete retires both `Expression` and `LvProp` LVAL pages to `0x09` at Delete and returns both page-1 bits at Close; shared `Expression`+`LvProp` LVAL deletes slot 0 and compacts slot 1 upward on both pages until the last slot retires both to `0x09` (`qd-del-expr-lval-{accdb,mdb}`, `qd-del-shared-expr-lval-accdb`).
  - *`QueryDef.SQL` put inline <-> LVAL, LVAL -> LVAL, UNION & PassThrough*: inline -> LVAL reserves a 32-page extent in page 1 and allocates a new LVAL page (`+1` at put, `+2` at Close for TDEF + extent return); LVAL -> inline turns the old LVAL page `0x09` at put (`+1`) and returns page-1 bit + TDEF at Close (`+2`); **LVAL -> LVAL does NOT reuse the old LVAL page** — it retires the old page (`0x09`), reserves an extent, and allocates a brand-new LVAL page (`p48`), returning the old page-1 bit at Close (`qd-sql-{inline-to-lval,lval-to-inline,lval-to-lval}-accdb`, `qd-sql-lval-roundtrip-mdb`). `SELECT <-> UNION <-> UNION ALL` rewrites `MSysQueries` rows at put (`+1`) and flips `MSysObjects.Flags` (`00 <-> 80`) + `MSysQueries` TDEF counts at Close (`+1`); saved PassThrough `SQL` put rewrites the `MSysQueries` Expression row (`+1` at put, `+1` at Close) (`qd-sql-put-{union,passthrough}-accdb`).
  - *Saved `QueryDef.Name`, `Connect`, `ReturnsRecords` puts*: `Name` put updates only page 0 `@3586` (`+1`), page 7 (`ParentIdName` index), and page 17 (`MSysObjects` row grown downward, `DateUpdate` unchanged); Close writes 0 bytes; old name lookup raises `3265` (`qd-put-name-{accdb,mdb}`). `Connect = "ODBC;..."` on SELECT rewrites `MSysQueries` + `DateUpdate` at put and flips `MSysObjects.Flags` (`00 -> 70`, `Type = 112`) + page-4 counts at Close (`Connect = ""` reverses both); `ReturnsRecords = False` on saved PassThrough returns `S_OK`, writes 0 bytes, and still reads back `VT_BOOL -1` (`qd-put-connect-returns-{accdb,mdb}`).
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q45-querydef-stream-rollback/qd-*` (15 probes + 12 `a2` repeats, full `nativeRead keep:true` pages).
- **Open**: `AceClockFields` (`calls.trace@13`) leaves `qd-put-name-{accdb,mdb}` `DateCreate` unmasked on the truncated vacated `MSysObjects` copy below the grown renamed row (`0x880` ACCDB / `0xCAA` MDB); chained (>1 page) QueryDef LVALs, draft `ReturnsRecords = False` before `Append`, non-SELECT PassThrough SQL, and `Name` error paths (`3012`/`3125`).

### R-TXN-1 TXN-NEST: current-level nesting
- **Claim**: `CommitTrans`/`Rollback` act on the current level; inner commit then outer rollback reverses both; inner rollback then outer commit keeps the outer change; the same row changed at every level takes the outer image after an inner rollback; empty levels raise nothing; resolving with no level raises 3034; the 8th `BeginTrans` raises 3003 (7 levels, also on a second Workspace independently) and writes at levels 6 and 7 raise 3003; `CommitTrans` accepts 0/Missing/Empty/I2 1/UI1 1/R8 1.0/"1", other values 3001 leaving the level open; `BeginTrans(1)` 3001 starts no level; a file opened after a nested begin joins and rolls back with it.
- **Records**: evidence/r-txn-1/nest-inner-rollback/ (+ -mdb), nest-middle-rollback-after-inner-commit/, nest-outer-rollback-after-inner-commits/, nest-same-row-all-levels/, nest-empty-levels/, nest-resolve-without-level/, nest-late-file-b/; evidence/r-txn-1b/nest-depth-reuse-after-commit/, nest-depth-second-workspace/, nest-depth-recordset-writes/ (+ -mdb), commit-option-values/, commit-option-vartypes/, txn-call-argument-counts/; evidence/r-fix-cat-txn/nest-depth-recordset-writes-fresh-witness/.
- **Open**: capacity of write levels vs statement failure at level 6.

### R-TXN-1 TXN-STMT: statement scope by failure cause
- **Claim**: Earlier changes always survive and later ones apply. With `dbFailOnError`: duplicate key in `INSERT…SELECT` (first/middle/last/only) 3022, validation 3316, RI delete 3200 and FK 3201 leave no candidate changed (`RecordsAffected` 0); an expression error (error 11) and lock exhaustion (3035) leave the candidates processed before the failure changed; a lock-blocked candidate (another process's pessimistic Edit on its page) gives 3260 after about 2.2 s and the candidates before it in scan order stay changed, those after are untouched (blocked first: nothing changed; middle or last: the earlier ones changed; all: nothing). Without the option: duplicates are skipped silently, a validation-blocked row is skipped, division by zero stores Null, a blocked candidate is skipped silently after the same 2.2 s wait (each blocked page costs one wait: 8.9 s for four) and `RecordsAffected` counts only the updated rows. Same for saved and temporary QueryDef and depth 0-2, on MDB. Through the ACE OLE DB provider (always failing, adExecuteNoRecords) the blocked middle candidate fails `0x80004005 "Could not update; currently locked by user ..."` (ExecuteComplete RecordsAffected 0), rows 1 and 20 before it stay changed and row 64 after it is untouched, in autocommit and inside `BeginTrans`/`CommitTrans`; after the Edit is cancelled the same statement changes all four rows (RecordsAffected 4).
- **Records**: q1/txn-stmt-lock-blocked-positions/ (first, middle, last, all x option/no option); q2/txn-stmt-ado-lock-blocked-byref/; evidence/r-txn-1/stmt-unique-middle-fail-d0/, -d1/, -d2/, -d1-mdb/, stmt-unique-last-fail-d1/, stmt-unique-all-fail-d1/, stmt-unique-middle-plain-d1/, stmt-saved-querydef-fail-d1/, stmt-validation-plain-d1/, stmt-fk-counter-fail-d1/; evidence/r-txn-1b/implicit-*, rb-*; evidence/r-fix-cat-txn/stmt-expression-fail-a-survives/.
- **Open**: I/O failure (T); a blocked candidate on the same page as an unblocked one is blocked with it (earlier "whole statement skipped" record, r-lock-3-4-txn/txn-stmt-blocked-no-scope).

### R-TXN-1 TXN-STMT r-q45 (task cxb6): new-page & BeginTrans AutoNumber 3022, row-1 3314/3316, shared in-process pending rollback extent

- **Claim**: On a brand-new data page (ACCDB and Jet 4 MDB), an explicit AutoNumber `3022` statement under `dbFailOnError` writes `+1` at `Execute` return, reserves the 32-page extent in page 1 (`@4038` ACCDB / `@4035` MDB), AND writes the AutoNumber word (`@20: 00 -> 05`); its deferred commit (`+1`) frees the rolled-back page bit (`04`/`08`) and writes `rowCount`/`PK`/`UK`, and `Close` returns the unused extent (`+1`); on an MDB existing page (`rb-exp-an-existing-mdb`), behaviour matches ACCDB (`+2`, 0 bytes at `Close`). Inside `BeginTrans` on an ACCDB existing page (`rb-exp-an-begintrans-{commit,rollback}-accdb`), `Execute` writes 0 bytes; `CommitTrans` writes `+1` + all 4 counters (`rowCount`, `AutoNumber=5`, `PK`, `UK`), whereas `Rollback` writes `+1` + **AutoNumber word only** (`@20: 0a -> 05`), leaving next auto ID = `6`. Row-1 `NOT NULL` (`3314`) and `ValidationRule` (`3316`) failures on both empty tables (no data page) and existing-page tables write 0 bytes across `Execute`, 1500 ms pause, and `Close`. A pending 32-page rollback extent stays **file-level in-process** across subsequent DML (`INSERT INTO T`), immediate DDL (`CREATE TABLE U` + `INSERT INTO U`, flushing the deferred rollback first -> `+2`), `BeginTrans` + `CommitTrans`, and a second `Workspace` (`CreateWorkspace`, inserting into `Other`): all allocate from the same reserved extent (skipping the freed rolled-back page bit) and ONLY closing the **last** handle returns unused extent bits to page 1 (`+1`).
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q45-querydef-stream-rollback/rb-*` (13 probes + 9 `a2` repeats, full `nativeRead keep:true` pages).
- **Open**: `BeginTrans` + new-page explicit AutoNumber `3022`; row-1 `3314`/`3316` on MDB or AutoNumber tables; MDB pending extent and cross-process opener beside a pending extent.
- **Built (`w16-isam-cxb6-rollback`, `af9f5c36`, 2026-11-27):** All 13 `r-q45` `rb-*` probes (22 JSON records) implemented byte-for-byte in `CalcDb.Isam`/`CalcDb.Catalog` (`SharedRollbackExtent`, `TransactionCoordinator`, `EditFrame`, `RowWriter`, `PageAllocator`, `SchemaWriter`); `Isam 992/992`, `Storage 140/140`, `703/703` citations.

### R-TXN-1 TXN-STMT, R-CRASH-1 CRASH-ORDER (publication): a statement is one write group; a refused INSERT keeps the TableDef counters it reached
- **Claim**:
  - *One group per statement.* ACE writes an action statement's pages as one group, opened and closed by writes of the page-0 field at 3586, and the field rises by one per group. Outside a transaction the group is the deferred write about 0.5 s after `Execute` returns, from an ACE thread (TXN-FLUSH-OP). Inside `BeginTrans` it is written within `CommitTrans`. A 4-row `INSERT…SELECT` and a 1-row INSERT are each one group: the data page (after a 1-byte write at its last byte when the page is new), page 1 when a page was taken, the TableDef and both index pages.
  - *Close adds a group after an allocation.* A session that took a new page writes page 1 and the field once more at `Close`. So the field rises by 2 for a statement that takes a new page and by 1 for one that fits on a page with room, whatever its row count: ins-one-newpage-fail 2, ins-one-existing-fail 1, ins-clean-existing-fail 1, ins-clean-fail 2. With no pause (ins-clean-fail-nopause) both groups are written in `Close`, and the field still rises by 2. The 3586 note's probes took no page.
  - *Counters.* The TableDef's first page holds the row count (16), the AutoNumber counter (20, the last value issued) and the unique-entry counts of physical indexes 0 and 1 (67, 79). They are written in the rows' group. INSERT…SELECT of K 1–4 gives IDs 1–4 and every counter 4. The next insert gets ID 5.
  - *Duplicate, no flag.* The refused row is the later of the two in source order ('g2-c-second12'), and it consumes its AutoNumber: IDs 1, 2 and 4 are stored. `RecordsAffected` is 3, and `Errors` is empty. Row count 3, AutoNumber 4, PK count 4 (the refused row's PK entry was counted), UK count 3. One group, as for a clean statement, inside `BeginTrans`/`CommitTrans` as well.
  - *Duplicate, `dbFailOnError`.* 3022, `RecordsAffected` 0, one `Errors` entry (3022). No row is stored and no data or index page is written. The TableDef counters are written as they stood at the refusal: row count 2, AutoNumber 3, PK count 3, UK count 2, on an empty table. `TableDef.RecordCount` and a table-type `RecordCount` read 2. After the next insert they read 3, with one row (ID 4).
  - *One row refused, nothing counted.* On a table whose key is not an AutoNumber given explicitly, a single-row
    `INSERT … VALUES` refused 3022 by the first unique index changes nothing on the file, page 0 included, with or without `dbFailOnError` (`evidence/r-err-1b/con-pk-duplicate-insert`,
    `evidence/r-fix-cursor-dao/con-pk-duplicate-insert-failonerror`: identical hashes before open and after close). The
    +1 on page 0 of r-q10 belongs to a recordset `AddNew`.
  - *How the refusal is written.* Outside a transaction, three groups: one inside `Execute` (page 1, TableDef), the deferred one 0.5 s later (the same pages), and page 1 at `Close` (+3). Inside a transaction: one group at `CommitTrans` and page 1 at `Close` (+2).
  - *Division by zero, `dbFailOnError`.* Error 11 in the third source row does the same: no rows; row count 2, AutoNumber 3, both index counts 2 (the failing row reached no index); +3.
  - *Rollback.* `BeginTrans`, the clean 4-row insert, `Rollback`: no row, row count 0. AutoNumber 4 is written with page 1 within `Rollback`, and page 1 again at `Close` (+2). The next insert gets ID 5.
    The AutoNumber is the only counter a Rollback writes: on a page with room the field rises by 1 (one row or four:
    ins-one-existing-txn-rb, ins-four-existing-txn-rb), and by 2 after a new page. A recordset `AddNew` + `Update`
    rolled back writes the same (rs-addnew-txn-rb-newpage +2, rs-addnew-txn-rb-existing +1), and so does a duplicate
    insert without `dbFailOnError` (ins-dup-plain-txn-rb: all four values consumed, next ID 5). Under `COUNTER(100, 5)`
    the word starts at 95 (seed minus increment) and four rolled-back rows leave 115, next ID 120
    (ins-txn-rb-counter-100-5). Jet 4 MDB does the same on its TableDef page 19 (ins-txn-rb-mdb).
  - *Nested levels.* `BeginTrans` twice, a 4-row insert, the inner `Rollback`, then the outer `CommitTrans`: no row, but
    the row count, the AutoNumber and both index counts are written as 4, `TableDef.RecordCount` reads 4 on an empty
    table, and after the next insert 5 on one row (ins-nested-inner-rb-outer-commit, a1 and a2; with one earlier row 5
    and 6: ins-nested-existing-inner-rb-outer-commit). With the outer `Rollback` instead, only the AutoNumber is written
