- **Claim.** Without a workgroup file ACE runs as default Admin with a blank password: `CreateWorkspace` with a named user, or Admin with a wrong password, gives 3028; `Users` and `Groups` are empty on ACCDB and MDB (Count 0, an empty enumeration; `Item("Admin")` 3265); `CreateUser` succeeds without changing the file bytes; `Permissions`/`AllPermissions` refuse 3358 until a put has been tried (DAO-META); `Container`/`Document` metadata reads work.
- **Conditions.** ACCDB and a native MDB made by `CreateDatabase`; no workgroup file (`SystemDB` "system.mdb" is reported but not present).
- **Records.** d1e/sec-default-admin-accdb, sec-default-admin-mdb, sec-logon-defaults; d1b/coll-item-top-level; q/meta-document-permissions-repeat; q2/meta-permissions-put-then-read, enum-dao-top-level-collections.
- **Open.** An MDB that needs only the default workgroup, a write that would change protected security metadata (CalcDb-side NotModelled refusal: N; workgroup success excluded by the card).

### R-DAO-1 II-05 index slice: metadata, flags, DistinctCount (Null counts), nonindexable types, refusal precedence

- **Claim.** `PK_*` indexes are Primary, Unique and Required; Foreign indexes carry the Foreign flag; index order in `Indexes` is not creation order. `DistinctCount` counts distinct index entries and Null is one entry in an ordinary index: Q = 1,2,2,3,Null gives 4, and 4 for one Null among 5 rows with IgnoreNulls (the Null row is not in that index). A held Index keeps its value after an INSERT until `Indexes.Refresh` (4 to 5); a fresh `TableDefs.Refresh`/`Item` shows 5 at once; the value reads 5 after `Rollback` of a later insert, after `Refresh`, and after reopen. A newly appended index reads 0 until `Indexes.Refresh`, then 1. DAO flag errors: second primary 3283, duplicate name 3284, no fields 3264, missing field 3409, `Required` over a column holding Null 3058, duplicate keys under a unique index 3022. An OLE (LONGBINARY) field cannot be indexed (3409 "Invalid field definition", by SQL and by DAO `Indexes.Append`, the index is not created) while a MEMO field is indexed without error by both routes (Indexes count 1 to 2 to 3). Construction refusal precedence: a `UNIQUE ... WITH DISALLOW NULL` index on (A,B) gives 3058 (Null) on tables holding (1,1),(1,1),(9,Null) and the same rows with the Null inserted first, where in key order the Null row sorts AFTER the duplicate; on (7,1),(7,1),(2,Null) (Null sorting first) in both insertion orders it also gives 3058; unique only gives 3022, DISALLOW NULL only gives 3058, and no index is left behind. So the Null refusal precedes duplicate detection regardless of key order and insertion order. `Index='PK_T'` then Seek works; a wrong index name 3800 (current row unchanged); Seek with 13 keys gives 3250, with 14 keys 3001.
- **Conditions.** ACCDB.
- **Records.** q2/index-null-sorts-after-duplicate (discriminating: Null after the duplicate in key order, two insertion orders, single-defect controls); q/index-distinctcount-dml, index-nonindexable-types, index-build-order-failure; fix/index-metadata-seek-pk-name; d1b/index-members-each, index-create-readback; r-cat-1b/index-dao-create-flags, index-members-ddl; c1/id-index-switch; fix/err-index-over-duplicates-two-pass.
- **Open.** (T) Raw statistics words, Calculated and Attachment fields (no DAO DDL).
- **Built (`w23-com-dao-ado-near`, `369144e0`, 2026-11-28):** `IndexObject.DistinctCount()` returns cached `_physical.UniqueEntryCount` across DML/rollback until `Indexes.Refresh()` (`r-q-cursor-dao/index-distinctcount-dml`); `Database.OpenLocalTableRecordset` forwards `Database.Collation` and maps `NotModelled.DynasetOrder*` to `Failure.NotModelled`.
- **Built (`w23-cat-sql-idx-ddl`, `a33d5b53`, 2026-11-28):** `SchemaWriter.BuildIndexLeaf` prioritizes Null refusal (`3058`) on `PRIMARY`/`DISALLOW NULL` over duplicate keys (`3022`) regardless of row/key order (`r-q-cursor-dao/index-build-order-failure`, `r-q2-cursor-dao/index-null-sorts-after-duplicate`), admits `NativeTypes.Text` in single-column ascending `UNIQUE` Null counts (`r-fix-fmt-sort/sort3b-existing-nulls-isolated` `E_uk` `DistinctCount=5`), and emits `DAO.Indexes` `3409` for DAO `Indexes.Append` on `Ole` (`r-q-cursor-dao/index-nonindexable-types`).

### R-DAO-1 DAO-LIFE: explicit Close makes held children give 3420; clones and derived recordsets outlive their parent; the last Release frees the file

- **Claim.** After `Database.Close` every held child (TableDefs, TableDef, Field, Indexes, QueryDef, Parameters, Recordset, Property, Containers) gives 3420 on every member including `Name` and a second `Close`; after `Workspace.Close` its Databases and children give 3420 and the appended workspace leaves the collection; after `Recordset.Close` every member and held Field gives 3420 while its clone, child and derived recordsets keep working; a recordset opened from a QueryDef survives `qd.Close()` and `qd.SQL` reads; a pending Edit is discarded by `Database.Close`; `db.Close()` with an open recordset gives no error; a live enumerator's `Next` then returns 0x800A0D5C. Releasing the last reference of a Database that was opened exclusive read-write, WITHOUT `Close` (wrapper count 0), frees the file: another process opens it exclusive read-write at once with no error and reads, whether the engine object was also released or not; the lock file is no longer listed afterwards.
- **Conditions.** ACCDB, default Workspace; the release case with two processes (participant b's answer is the evidence; both records ReturnedSuccess).
- **Records.** q/life-release-without-close-database, life-release-without-close-database-and-engine; q2/enum-close-during-walk; d1e/life-database-workspace-close, life-recordset-querydef-release; def/recordset-closed-member-inventory; c1/life-close-held; c1b/clone-chain-close-orders.
- **Open.** Concurrent/reentrant Close (DAO-CLOSE-OWN, T). Probe defect: the q release probes did not record file state while A still held the database, so the lock file's presence before the release is inferred from earlier records.

### R-ERR-1 ERR-BASE: DBEngine.Errors is one collection per engine; ADO Errors is per Connection; a failed or aggregated activation leaves no entry

- **Claim.** `DBEngine.Errors` is a single collection for the engine (all Databases and Workspaces): empty before any failure (56 probes), one entry after a failure whose Number, Description, Source, HelpContext and HelpFile equal the failing call's EXCEPINFO; a later failure replaces it (3078 then 3061: Count 1, the later description); a later success of any kind leaves the old entry; `Errors.Refresh` changes nothing; a second `CoCreate` in the same process returns the same engine object and Errors; a second process (own engine) starts with Count 0 and its failures do not appear in the first. Activation failures leave nothing in Errors: `CoCreateInstance` of DAO.DBEngine.120 with CLSCTX_LOCAL_SERVER fails 0x80040154, with in-process aggregation CLASS_E_NOAGGREGATION 0x80040110 (E_UNEXPECTED 0x8000FFFF when the process already holds an engine, ops record), and a following in-process activation succeeds with `Errors.Count` 0; the first member failure (`OpenDatabase` of a missing file, 3024) then gives Count 1. ADO: each Connection has its own `Errors`: a provider failure on c1 (Count 1, SQLState 3000, NativeError) leaves c2 at 0; a different failure on c2 leaves c1's entry unchanged; a successful `Execute` on c1 clears c1 only (c2 keeps its entry).
- **Conditions.** DAO raw lane ACCDB; ADO read-only connections to one file.
- **Records.** q2/errbase-activation-failure-then-member (discriminating: activation failure separated from the first member failure); q/errbase-two-ado-connections (two live Connections, three steps); ops/activation-routes; e1b/proto-fail-success-no-read, proto-two-fails-no-read, proto-success-kinds, proto-errors-item-range; fix/proto-two-fails-different-numbers, err-base-scope-second-process (truly independent engine); d1e/err-base-scope.
- **Open.** The two aggregation answers differ between a fresh process (0x80040110, q2) and one that already holds an engine (0x8000FFFF, ops): one record each, order-dependent, outside this card's decision.

### R-ERR-1 ERR-BIND: positional, named and by-reference arguments

- **Claim.** Positional: arity errors on a DAO method give 3001 from the DAO source; an unknown member name fails in `GetIDsOfNames` with 0x80020006 (no member entry); a property called as a method or a put-ref on a get-only member gives 3251; for `OpenRecordset("T", Type, 0, 3)` a Type cell of Missing or Empty gives 3001 and Null 3421; "4" and R8 4.5 succeed as a snapshot (type 4); BOOL -1 gives 3001; swapped arguments give 3421; an out-of-range type (999) or `Execute` option 99999 gives 3001. Named: one `GetIDsOfNames` resolves the names and the call binds them by DISPID: `OpenRecordset` Name=0, Type=1, Options=2, LockEdit=3; `Execute` Query=0, Options=1; `Move` Rows=0; names are case-insensitive (`type` binds Type); any order works (`Type:=2, Name:='T'`); a name given both positionally and by name, or the same name twice, gives 3001; a wrong-typed named value gives the positional error (Type:='abc' 3421, Null 3421, Name:=Null 3421); a name the member does not have (`Execute` Name, `MoveNext` Rows, a property get with Index) fails in `GetIDsOfNames` with DISP_E_UNKNOWNNAME 0x80020006 and never enters the member. By reference: a DAO input parameter accepts a by-reference Variant and a typed by-reference I4 or BSTR (`OpenRecordset("T", ByRef 4)`, `(ByRef "T", 4)`, `Move(ByRef 2)`, `GetRows`, `FindFirst`, `Execute(ByRef sql, ByRef 128)`, `Seek(ByRef "=", ByRef 3)`) with the same result as by value and the value after the call unchanged; ByRef Empty gives 3001 and ByRef Null 3421 as by value. ADO writes back: `Connection.Execute(sql, RecordsAffected, options)` writes RecordsAffected into a by-reference variant or typed I4 (2 after an UPDATE of two rows, 0 after an UPDATE of none) and leaves a by-value argument alone.
- **Conditions.** Late-bound; DAO and ADO; the worker's by-reference and named-argument operations.
- **Records.** q2/named-dao-order-variants, named-dao-refusals, byref-dao-openrecordset, byref-dao-move-seek; ops/named-dao-openrecordset, byref-ado-execute-records-affected; d1e/err-bind-arity-member, err-bind-optional-cells, err-bind-types; e1b/arg-execute-options-99999, arg-openrs-type-999, arg-openrs-opentable-on-sql; d1b/eng-members-each; q/errinfo-presence-by-kind.
- **Open.** (T) Typed vtable and Access/VBA lanes; the managed lanes (ERR-MANAGED).

### R-ERR-1 ERR-DAO-ORDER: with several Errors entries the EXCEPINFO equals the last entry

- **Claim.** In every one of nine captures with more than one Errors entry the EXCEPINFO `scode` of the failing call equals the LAST entry: `Item(Object)` on TableDefs, QueryDefs, Relations, Fields, Indexes and Properties each gives [3001, 3421] with EXCEPINFO 3421, where entry 0 carries the fixed source `DAO.TableDefs` for all six and entry 1 the actual collection class (also the EXCEPINFO source); `Indexes.Append` on a held TableDef of a table dropped by SQL gives [3011, 3078] with EXCEPINFO 3078; an ODBC connection failure gives [0 (source `ODBC.Workspace`), 3146] with EXCEPINFO 3146 (retained record, not to be repeated). Entries run general first; `Errors.Refresh` changes nothing; each case follows a marker failure that left one entry; an enumerator over the collection returns the entries in the same order (q2/enum-errors-partial-consume).
- **Conditions.** DAO raw lane; the eight DAO-only captures are the qualifying ones.
- **Records.** q/errorder-dispatch-ordinal-by-collection (discriminating: six collections against a marker, DAO only); q2/enum-errors-partial-consume; d1e/err-dao-order, coll-tabledefs-index-variants; fix/deleted-tabledef-held-calls.
- **Open.** (T) Comparison with the VBA `Err` object (needs Access/VBA).

### R-ERR-1 ERR-ADO: which calls clear a Connection's Errors; Clear; provider versus runtime failures; no provider warning

- **Claim.** After a provider failure (Count 1, Number -2147217900 SQLState 3000, or -2147217865 SQLState 3078): a successful `Connection.Execute` clears `Errors` (Count 0); a property get (`State`) and `Recordset.MoveNext` on an open recordset leave it unchanged (Count 1); an ADO runtime failure (`Properties.Item` of a missing name, 3265-class) leaves Count 0; `Errors.Clear` empties it. Errors is scoped per Connection (ERR-BASE). A held Errors collection is live (Count follows the Connection) and a held Error object keeps its Number, Description and SQLState after `Clear` and after a later failure, while `Item(0)` afterwards returns a new object with the new failure. Requesting a recordset with a cursor and lock type ACE may downgrade (dynamic/optimistic, static/batch, forward-only/pessimistic) returns a keyset cursor (CursorType 1 in all three, LockType as requested), `Errors.Count` stays 0, and a recording sink on ConnectionEvents sees no InfoMessage (dispid 0 never raised); the events seen are WillConnect (6), ConnectComplete (7), WillExecute (4), ExecuteComplete (5) and Disconnect (8).
- **Conditions.** ADO, Provider Microsoft.ACE.OLEDB.12.0, read-only and read-write connection; event sink on the calling thread.
- **Records.** q/errado-clear-by-operation (discriminating: one call kind varied after the same failure), errado-held-after-clear, errbase-two-ado-connections; q2/errado-warning-infomessage; fix/ado-connection-errors-clear-with-entries; d1e/ado-connection-errors, ado-recordset-warning-runtime; ops/ado-connection-events.
- **Open.** (T) A successful provider warning has still not been observed (three downgrades, no InfoMessage, no Errors entry). Probe defect: in q/errado-clear-by-operation the runtime case clears Count while `cn.Properties()` (a get) alone does not, but the clearing call (`Item` failure versus the failed `Properties` object call) is not separated further.

### R-ERR-1 ERR-SNAPSHOT: a held Error is an immutable snapshot; the Errors collection and its enumerator are live

- **Claim.** DAO: a held `Error` object (e0 from failure A, 3078) keeps its Number and Description after failure B (3061), after a successful call, after `Errors.Refresh`, after A repeated, after `Database.Close` and after a failure of the Errors collection itself (`Item(1)`, 3265); the held `Errors` collection reads Count 1 throughout while a fresh `Item(0)` always shows the newest failure (3061, 3078, 3265); `eng.Errors()` returns the same collection wrapper. An `IEnumVARIANT` over Errors that is half consumed is live: with a two-entry collection [3001, 3421] and the first `Next` returned (3001), a new failure replaced the collection by one entry (3376); the next `Next` returned S_FALSE (the position was already past the single entry), `Reset` then `Next` returned the new entry, and the item already returned kept its payload. ADO: see ERR-ADO (held Error survives `Clear`).
- **Conditions.** DAO raw lane and ADO; ACCDB.
- **Records.** q2/enum-errors-partial-consume (discriminating: the collection replaced between two `Next` calls); q/errsnap-held-variants (six later events between two reads of one held object), errado-held-after-clear; d1e/err-snapshot-held.
- **Open.** None for the reference build; the book's "no universal snapshot semantics" remains: the policy is per family (Error immutable, Errors and its enumerator positional over the live collection).

### R-ERR-1 ERR-EXCEP: inline EXCEPINFO shape and which failures also publish an IErrorInfo

- **Claim.** Every failing DAO member returned `hr=0x80020009` with `wCode=0`, `scode=0x800A<number>`, `deferredFill=False`, `source` the class of the failing object, `helpFile` `jeterr40.chm` and `helpContext` 5000000+Number; VBA runtime errors (11, 5, 6) carry `helpFile` "-" and `helpContext` 4294967295, which the Error object reads as "" and -1. The public `Error.Number` is the scode low word. An `IErrorInfo` (guid zero, same source, description, help context) is published only for argument-count failures of Database and Recordset members (`OpenRecordset` with seven arguments or none: 3001; `MoveNext(1)`: 3001) and for `Field.Value` failures (put of "abc" into a Long: 3421; get of `1/0`: 11); it is absent for engine-service and collection failures (`Execute` on a missing table 3078, `TableDefs.Item('Nope')` 3265, `Item(Object)` 3421, `Recordset.Update` without Edit 3020, `DROP TABLE` on a missing table 3376) and for 3001 raised by an out-of-range VALUE of a correct-arity call (e1b/arg-execute-options-99999, arg-openrs-type-999). ADO failures carry EXCEPINFO `scode=0x80040E14`/`0x80040E37`/`0x80040E21` with source "Microsoft Access Database Engine" or `ADODB.*` and a populated IErrorInfo.
- **Conditions.** DAO raw lane, ACCDB; ADO provider routes.
- **Records.** q/errinfo-presence-by-kind (discriminating with e1b/arg-*: 3001 by arity has it, 3001 by value does not); e1b/* (56 probes, e.g. asg-byte-300, div0-select-read, fn-sqr-negative, missing-file); d1e/err-bind-arity-member; q2/errnest-ado-connection-event-error.
- **Open.** (T) A controlled deferred-fill object. Probe defect: the last case of q/errinfo-presence-by-kind (a member call on a closed recordset) was never run: the helper released the recordset, so the call hit an unbound target.

### R-ERR-1 ERR-NEST (ADO event part): completion events carry pError and adStatus and fire before the failing call returns

- **Claim.** On the calling thread, for `Connection.Execute`: WillExecute (dispid 4: source text, adStatus 1) then ExecuteComplete (dispid 5: RecordsAffected, pError, adStatus, pCommand, pRecordset, pConnection) then the call returns. For a successful Execute pError is null and adStatus 1; for a failing Execute (`SELEC ...` 0x80040E14, `SELECT * FROM NoSuchTable` 0x80040E37, also inside a transaction) pError is a non-null Error object and adStatus 2 (adStatusErrorsOccurred), the event fires before the call returns its failure, and `Connection.Errors` then holds the entry. `BeginTrans` raises dispid 1 (BeginTransComplete, level 1), `RollbackTrans` dispid 2 (the name of the event raised for a rollback is not resolved by the sink), `Close` dispid 8 (Disconnect). For a Recordset (keyset, optimistic): WillMove/MoveComplete (dispids 15/16) around Open and every move with adReason 10 (move) or 13/15; `AddNew` raises WillChangeRecord/RecordChangeComplete (11/12, reason 1), setting a field WillChangeField/FieldChangeComplete (9/10); a failing `Update` (duplicate primary key 0x80040E21) raises WillChangeRecord (reason 3) and RecordChangeComplete with a non-null pError and adStatus 2, `CancelUpdate` raises them again with reason 5, moves past the end raise EndOfRecordset (17), and the ADO error 3021-class (0x800A0BCD) follows on the extra `MoveNext`.
- **Conditions.** ADO, Provider Microsoft.ACE.OLEDB.12.0; the worker's recording sink (it records arguments and the thread, it cannot act inside a handler).
- **Records.** q2/errnest-ado-connection-event-error, errnest-ado-recordset-event-error, errado-warning-infomessage; ops/ado-connection-events.
- **Open.** (T) A handler that creates another error, or reenters ACE, while the retained pError of an outer completion event is alive (the sink cannot make calls); ERR-NEST's native callback cases need R-UDF-1.

### R-ERR-1 ERR-CLOSE: duplicate Close and held-child calls

- **Claim.** `Close` on an already closed Database or Recordset, a member call on a closed object, and calls on a held child after the parent closed give 3420 ("Object invalid or no longer set", source the object's class) and one entry in `DBEngine.Errors`; a second Database on the same file keeps working after the first closes; a held Error keeps its payload after `Database.Close` (ERR-SNAPSHOT); an enumerator over a collection of the closed Database returns 3420 from `Next`.
- **Conditions.** DAO raw lane, ACCDB.
- **Records.** e1b/closed-db-openrs, closed-rs-close-twice, closed-rs-movenext; d1e/life-database-workspace-close, err-snapshot-held; def/recordset-closed-member-inventory; q/errsnap-held-variants; q2/enum-close-during-walk.
- **Open.** (T) Close from a callback, reentry into an ancestor, cleanup failure after a primary error.

### R-ERR-1 ERR-PARTIAL: constraint refusals under dbFailOnError store no rows (the TableDef counters still move, corrected 2026-11-21); lock refusals are page-granular and keep earlier updates; without the flag violating rows are skipped silently

- **Claim.** Constraint refusals: `Database.Execute` of an INSERT with `dbFailOnError` (128) that violates a primary key or unique index (3022), a required field (3314), a zero-length required Text (3315), a relationship (3201), a field or table rule (3316) or a type conversion (3464) leaves the table unchanged, `RecordsAffected` 0, one Errors entry; an `INSERT ... SELECT` with a duplicate in the first, middle or last row gives 3022 and adds no rows, but it is not "unchanged": the TableDef's row count, AutoNumber counter and index counts are written as they stood at the refusal, so `RecordCount` reads 2 on a table with no rows (corrected 2026-11-21 by `evidence/r-q18-statements/ins-dup-fail`, `-fail-count`, `-fail-txn`; see the r-q18 statement-publication note); without the flag the same statement inserts the other rows and raises no error. `DELETE FROM PA` (some parent has a child) with 128 gives 3200, RecordsAffected 0 and PA unchanged, whichever parent (first, third, fifth of five) carries the child; without 128 it raises no error, RecordsAffected is 4 and the row with the child is the only one left. `UPDATE T SET ID = ID + 1` against a relationship gives 3200 with no row changed. A byte value of 300 inserted into a Byte column is accepted and stored as 44. Lock refusals (another Workspace holds a pessimistic edit): the lock covers the page of the edited row (about 18 rows of the 2048-row test table). On the 8-row, one-page table an `UPDATE` gives 3188 with 128 and changes no row, and changes no row, raises no error and reports 0 without the flag. On the 2048-row table, `UPDATE Big SET V = 'u'` with a lock on ID 1, 1000 or 2048: without the flag rows of the other pages are updated and the locked page's rows are skipped, no error, RecordsAffected 2030, 2030 and 2034 (18, 18 and 14 rows skipped; MIN updated ID 19 for the lock on ID 1, MAX 2034 for the lock on ID 2048); WITH 128 the statement fails 3188 with RecordsAffected 0, stops at the first row on the locked page and KEEPS the earlier updates in key order: 0 rows for the lock on ID 1, IDs 1-990 for the lock on ID 1000, IDs 1-2034 for the lock on ID 2048. Recordset `Update` violations keep EditMode and the buffer (CUR-EDIT).
- **Conditions.** ACCDB, one Database (second Workspace for the lock cases); table read back through a snapshot; the lock statements update in key order.
- **Records.** q2/errpart-lock-multipage-failonerror, errpart-lock-multipage-plain (discriminating: lock on the first, a middle and the last page, flag on and off; settles page granularity and the missing rollback); q/errpart-delete-ri-position-failonerror, errpart-delete-ri-position-plain, errpart-lock-position; fix/con-pk-duplicate-insert-failonerror, con-unique-index-insert-failonerror, con-required-omitted-failonerror, con-zero-length-required-failonerror, con-fk-orphan-insert-failonerror, con-field-validation-insert-failonerror, con-table-validation-insert-failonerror, sql-insert-text-into-number-failonerror, ovf-insert-byte-failonerror, err-multi-violation-sql-failonerror, proto-txn-fail-rollback-failonerror; d1e/err-partial-constraint (first/middle/last, with and without 128); e1b/con-fk-parent-delete.
- **Open.** (T) I/O, flush and delivery faults, killed process (R-CRASH-1 and fault injection; r-sample-rec shows that an explicit transaction writes new data pages first and pages 0, 1 and 45-48 later, and that an implicit INSERT is written after it returned, but records no failure). The file hash changed after a last-row duplicate rollback with no row change (d1e) is unexplained.

## R-EXPR-1, R-SQL-1 to R-SQL-3 and R-PAR-1

### R-EXPR-1 Branch A: Null propagation and parameter input adaptation
**Claim.** In a SELECT expression `Null + x`, `-Null`, `Not Null`, `Null = x` and `Null <> x` are Null, while `&` treats Null as an empty string (`Null & 'x'` = "x", `Null & Null` is Null). `IsNull('')` is 0 and `Len(Null)` is Null. A declared Long parameter takes Null as Null, Empty as 0 and the VT_ERROR "missing" marker as Null; a declared or undeclared Text parameter reads Null for Null, Empty, the marker and an empty BSTR. An undeclared parameter reads back as Text whatever its input tag (I4 7 gives "7", R8 2.5 gives "2.5", DATE 0.25 gives "06:00:00"). A BSTR "q" or "" put into a declared Long parameter fails at the put (3421).
**Conditions.** DAO QueryDef.Parameters, snapshot, ACCDB; declared types Long and Text and undeclared names.
**Records.** `evidence/r-expr-1/a-null-empty-error-literals/r-expr-1.a-null-empty-error-literals.f2882098b8.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1/a-parameter-inputs/r-expr-1.a-parameter-inputs.d375f6c653.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1/b-plus-versus-concat/r-expr-1.b-plus-versus-concat.963f485c1c.a1.json`; independent repeat: `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/nz-free-forms/r-fix-expr-sql.nz-free-forms.4105e37dd6.a1.json` (Long p Null gives Null, Empty gives 0), `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/parameters-values-set/r-fix-expr-sql.parameters-values-set.55c1458fde.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/params-null-and-unset/r-sql-13b.params-null-and-unset.71e0e47568.a1.json`.

**Claim (follow-up, optional arguments).** Fed through a declared Long parameter p, Null into an optional argument is refused and Empty behaves as an explicit 0: `Round(2.5, p)` and `Left('abcd', p)` with p Null fail 3464 at OpenRecordset, `Mid('abcd', 2, p)` and `InStr(p, 'abcd', 'c')` with p Null fail 94 "Invalid use of Null"; with p Empty (read back as Long 0) `Round(2.5, p)` is 2, `Mid('abcd', 2, p)` and `Left('abcd', p)` are "" and `InStr(p, 'abcd', 'c')` fails 5 "Invalid procedure call", the same as p = 0. Omission differs: `Round(2.5)` is 2, `Mid('abcd', 2)` is "bcd", `InStr('abcd', 'c')` is 3.
**Records (follow-up).** `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/a-optarg-null-empty-params/r-q-expr-sql.a-optarg-null-empty-params.822ae8f57e.a1.json`.
**Open.** No Error-valued input exists (CVErr is undefined, see the availability note); Error values, arrays and objects need an Access/VBA host (status T). An explicit Error marker as optional argument was not asked.

### R-EXPR-1 Branch A, EXPR-INVENTORY, EXPR-DOMAIN, EXPR-EVAL: function availability in the standalone expression service
**Corrected (2026-11-20, W-06/parser-binder-scan replay; r-q3-sql/iserror-forms).** `IsError` is available, against the claim below: `IsError(1)`, `IsError(Null)`, `IsError('x')` and `IsError(#2024-01-01#)` answer 0 and `VarType(IsError(1))` is 2 (`evidence/r-expr-1/a-null-empty-error-literals/`, `evidence/r-q3-sql/iserror-forms/`); `IsError(1/0)` fails 11 at open and `IsError(CVErr(5))` 3085, CVErr being the unavailable one.
**Claim.** In standalone DAO/ACE, `Nz`, `CVErr`, `IsError`, `IsArray`, `Eval`, `Environ`, `CurDir`, `Dir`, `FileLen`, `Command`, `Join`, `Split`, `Filter`, `UBound`, `Randomize`, `Log10`, `CurrentUser()`, `CurrentDb()`, `CLngLng`, `CLngPtr`, the file-handle functions and one-argument `CDec` are not available: calls fail with 0x800A0C0D (3085 "Undefined function") or 0x800A0C03 for CDec. `DLookup`, `DSum`, `DCount`, `DMax`, `DMin`, `DFirst`, `DLast`, `DAvg` work and return their result to DAO as Text for DMax/DMin/DFirst/DLast/DLookup. A context capability result, not a defect: Access-hosted values cannot be created here.
**Conditions.** DAO SELECT/Filter/Sort expressions on ACCDB and MDB (`expr-clnglng-mdb`).
**Records.** `evidence/r-expr-1b/sandbox-vba-library/r-expr-1b.sandbox-vba-library.55bf0b9648.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/sandbox-system-and-file-functions/r-expr-1b.sandbox-system-and-file-functions.ae73124ef1.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/sandbox-access-hosted-names/r-expr-1b.sandbox-access-hosted-names.8ac7ffb162.a1.json`, `evidence/r-def-expr-sql/expr-filehandle-functions-unopened/r-def-expr-sql.expr-filehandle-functions-unopened.de799c3d70.a1.json`, `evidence/r-def-expr-sql/expr-clnglng-clngptr/r-def-expr-sql.expr-clnglng-clngptr.07c24b48b6.a1.json`, `evidence/r-def-expr-sql/expr-clnglng-mdb/r-def-expr-sql.expr-clnglng-mdb.8f2eb5233a.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/cdec-argument-forms/r-fix-expr-sql.cdec-argument-forms.952cd70a13.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/array-functions-split-only/r-fix-expr-sql.array-functions-split-only.0b22e64004.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1/host-routes-domain-eval/r-expr-1.host-routes-domain-eval.2274c94294.a1.json`, `evidence/r-def-expr-sql/expr-domain-aggregate-values/r-def-expr-sql.expr-domain-aggregate-values.1f10ca6730.a1.json`.
**Open.** `Array(...)` crashes ACE and is never asked; `CDec(1,2)` is admitted and returns Long 0 (unexplained). Access-hosted routes are out of scope here.

### R-EXPR-1 Branch B: text against number comparison
**Claim.** A comparison of a number with text that is not a number (`7 < 'q'`, `7 = '7'`, `'10' < 7`, a stored Long against a stored Text) opens, and fails with 0x800A0D88 on the Value read; comparing two declared parameters of Long and Text fails at OpenRecordset. Two Text values compare as text (`'7' < '10'` is 0). Undeclared parameters carry text, so `[ua] = [ub]` compares as text ((7,"7") equal, (10,"9") less). `#date# = 'text'` returns 0 with no error, but a stored Date/Time field against a text literal fails 0x800A0D88 (Find `D = '1/15/2020'`, `evidence/r-cursor-1b/find-date-forms/r-cursor-1b.find-date-forms.ee7428cf5c.a1.json`). `WHERE T = N` over stored columns fails at open, per row for the projection form.
**Conditions.** DAO snapshot, ACCDB.
**Records.** `evidence/r-expr-1/b-numeric-text-compare/r-expr-1.b-numeric-text-compare.4560e822e0.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/cmp-cross-types/r-expr-1b.cmp-cross-types.e898adce72.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/cmp-stored-lanes/r-expr-1b.cmp-stored-lanes.269e15de0b.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/stored-where-per-row/r-fix-expr-sql.stored-where-per-row.cf6792983b.a1.json`.
**Open.** Date/Time Extended; the direct VBA lane.

### R-EXPR-1 Branch B: addition against concatenation
**Claim.** `+` with a number and numeric text adds (`7 + '7'` is Double 14); `+` of two texts concatenates (`'7' + '7'` = "77"); `7 + 'q'` fails with 0x800A0D88 on Value. `+` with Null or Empty is Null; `&` always concatenates, treats Null as empty and formats a Boolean as "-1" and a date as "2024/01/01". `N + T` over stored 7 and '7' is Double 14 (but `N = T` fails).
**Conditions.** DAO snapshot, ACCDB, literals, stored columns and undeclared parameters.
**Records.** `evidence/r-expr-1/b-plus-versus-concat/r-expr-1.b-plus-versus-concat.963f485c1c.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/op-plus-amp-precedence-and-types/r-expr-1b.op-plus-amp-precedence-and-types.674d84115d.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/op-concat-number-forms/r-expr-1b.op-concat-number-forms.94303d4918.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/cmp-stored-lanes/r-expr-1b.cmp-stored-lanes.269e15de0b.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/stored-null-iif/r-fix-expr-sql.stored-null-iif.2f97406e25.a1.json` (`T & 'x'` "x", `T + 'x'` Null for a stored Null).

### R-EXPR-1 Branch B: integer widening and overflow
**Claim.** Byte and Integer arithmetic widens: `B + CByte(1)` is VT_I4 256, `CInt(100) * CInt(400)` is 40000, `CByte(200) + CByte(100)` is 300. Long arithmetic does not widen: `L + CLng(1)` at the maximum, `L * CLng(2)` and `-CLng(-2147483648)` fail with 0x800A0006 on the Value read; integer literals widen to Double (`2147483647 + 1` is 2147483648). BIGINT (ACCDB only) adds like Long: `B + 1`, `B * 2` at the extremes overflow on Value, `B \ 2` stays BIGINT, `B / 2` is a Double, `B + 1.5` fails 3464 on rows at the maximum.
**Conditions.** ACCDB stored columns and literals; BIGINT is refused in MDB (3001).
**Records.** `evidence/r-expr-1/b-integer-widening/r-expr-1.b-integer-widening.52ba244d05.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/arith-overflow-promotion/r-expr-1b.arith-overflow-promotion.8e33d497ff.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/bigint-stored-arith/r-fix-expr-sql.bigint-stored-arith.39720af343.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/bigint-stored-compare-convert/r-fix-expr-sql.bigint-stored-compare-convert.30231878df.a1.json`.
**Open.** `B+1.5` on ID 3 returning I8 6 is unexplained.

### R-EXPR-1 Branch B: rounding in integer conversion, `\` and `Mod`
**Claim.** CInt, CLng and CByte round half to even: 2.5 gives 2, 3.5 gives 4, -2.5 gives -2, 0.5 gives 0, 100.5 gives 100, 101.5 gives 102; 2.5000000000000004 gives 3. They fail with 0x800A0006 outside the range (`CInt(32767.5)`, `CByte(255.5)`, `CByte(-0.6)`). `\` and `Mod` round their operands first (`7.5 \ 2` is 4, `7 \ 2.5` is 3, `7 \ 0.4` fails 0x800A000B) and `/` always gives Double; `1 / 0` fails 0x800A000B on Value, `0 / 0` fails 0x800A0006. CCur rounds half to even at four decimals (0.00015 and 0.00025 give 0.0002, 0.00005 gives 0); `Round(0.125,2)` is 0.12.
**Correction.** "`/` always gives Double" holds for the DAO Field.Type (7), not for the inner value: `VarType(CSng(1) / CSng(3))` is 4 (Single), every other `/` asked is 5 (`evidence/r-expr-1b/arith-overflow-promotion/r-expr-1b.arith-overflow-promotion.8e33d497ff.a1.json`). The inner types are in the note "inner result types follow oleaut32" below.
**Conditions.** DAO snapshot, ACCDB, literals and stored Double.
**Records.** `evidence/r-expr-1/b-integer-conversion/r-expr-1.b-integer-conversion.4a417fa48a.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/integer-conversion-alias/r-fix-expr-sql.integer-conversion-alias.60068f498b.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1/b-intdiv-mod/r-expr-1.b-intdiv-mod.60456b370a.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/conv-half-rounding-large/r-expr-1b.conv-half-rounding-large.11d003a414.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/conv-range-edges/r-expr-1b.conv-range-edges.01b85c3b66.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/arith-intdiv-mod-types/r-expr-1b.arith-intdiv-mod-types.dee0e58c4e.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/conv-round-function-digits/r-expr-1b.conv-round-function-digits.8f79dca0d3.a1.json`.
**Open.** Text boundary forms are in the text-to-number note.

### R-EXPR-1 Branch B: mixed-type result types and Currency/Decimal fidelity
**Claim.** Currency + Double and Double + Currency are Currency; Currency * Double is Double; Single with Long or Double is Double; Currency + Long is Currency; Date + number is Date; Date - Date is Double. A decimal-point literal (`1.5`) is Decimal (VarType 14) although the DAO Field.Type of an expression can differ from the inner VarType (`Abs(-2.5)` is Field type 7 with VarType 14; `Abs(CInt(-32768))` is R8 with VarType 3). A stored Decimal(28,4) with magnitude 2^64+5 survives identity, unary minus, `+0`, `*1`, `/1`: -1844674407370955.1621; Decimal + Currency, + Long, * Long stay Decimal; Decimal + Double is Field type 7 with VarType 14; CCur and `\ 1` of it overflow (6) on Value; CDbl gives R8. Currency maximum and minimum round-trip exactly through a parameter and overflow on one-unit neighbours at OpenRecordset.
**Conditions.** ACCDB; the Decimal column is made by ADO DDL with explicit precision and scale.
**Records.** `evidence/r-expr-1/b-mixed-precedence/r-expr-1.b-mixed-precedence.b8da1929d2.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/dec-mixed-precedence/r-fix-expr-sql.dec-mixed-precedence.ec3865446f.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/dec-fixed-point-fidelity/r-fix-expr-sql.dec-fixed-point-fidelity.71d4fc7f5f.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/meta-conversion-field-types/r-expr-1b.meta-conversion-field-types.bef199cacc.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/info-result-vartype-numeric/r-expr-1b.info-result-vartype-numeric.2ca10df6b5.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1/b-fixed-point-fidelity/r-expr-1.b-fixed-point-fidelity.43044a79c9.a1.json`.

**Claim (follow-up, Decimal scale).** A stored Decimal keeps its own scale on read: Decimal(28,4) 1.5000, Decimal(28,20) 1.50000000000000000000, Decimal(28,28) 0.0000000000000000000000000001 and 0.5; VarType 14 for all. Equal values of different scale compare equal (`a = b` is -1 for 1.5000 and 1.5 at scale 20; `WHERE a = b` selects all three rows) and `WHERE c = 0.5` selects the right row. `c + c` stays Decimal at scale 28 (1E-28 + 1E-28 gives 2E-28, 0.5 + 0.5 gives 1.0000000000000000000000000000); CStr(c) is exact and CDbl(c) is 1.0000000000000001E-28; Sum(c) is Decimal 0.5000000000000000000000000003 and Sum(a) Decimal 3.5001. Arithmetic between Decimal columns of different scale (`a + b`, `a * b`, `a - b`) returns Double (3, 2.25, 0), and `c * 1` returns Double, whereas `M * 1` on the Decimal(28,4) column of the first fixture stayed Decimal: whether a Decimal result survives depends on the scales involved, and the rule is not determined.
**Records (follow-up).** `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/dec-scale-compare/r-q-expr-sql.dec-scale-compare.3496819056.a1.json`.
**Open.** Why mixed-scale arithmetic and `c * 1` go Double (same-scale `a + a`, `a * a` were not asked); Decimal typed parameters (the builder has no VT_DECIMAL argument).

### R-EXPR-1 Branch B: floating-point comparison fidelity
**Claim.** Doubles compare exactly: 0.1 and 0.10000000000000002 as parameters are unequal and their difference is -1.3877787807814457E-17; Single 1.1 widened equals Double 1.1 only in the Single value (difference 2.3841857821338408E-08); `0.1 + 0.2 = 0.3` is -1. Non-finite and signed-zero parameter values arrive as text ("nan", "inf", "-0") and the non-finite ones fail 0x800A0D88 at OpenRecordset.
**Conditions.** DAO parameters (declared Double and undeclared), literals, ACCDB.
**Records.** `evidence/r-expr-1/b-floating-fidelity/r-expr-1.b-floating-fidelity.c580cdca94.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-1/eq-cross-type/r-sql-1.eq-cross-type.6be1a0daf9.a1.json`.

### R-EXPR-1 Branch C: DATE payloads
**Claim.** A DATE parameter and a Double parameter with the same payload behave identically in comparison and CDbl (raw payloads -1.25, -1.5, -0.25 are preserved: `CDbl(DateSerial(1899,12,29))` is -1); comparison, BETWEEN and ORDER BY follow the numeric payload (BETWEEN #12/29/1899# AND #12/31/1899# selects the same rows as the numeric range). Component functions read negative payloads as time-of-day plus day offset (`Hour(-0.25)` 6, `Day(-0.25)` 30). `CDate('1/2/2003')` is 37623 in en-ZA, impossible dates (`'2/30/2003'`) and out-of-range numbers fail 0x800A0D88; `#d# + 0.5` is Decimal.
**Conditions.** DAO, ACCDB, en-ZA locale (parse results are locale-bound).
**Records.** `evidence/r-expr-1/c-date-payloads/r-expr-1.c-date-payloads.28a1b72090.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1/c-date-stored-predicates/r-expr-1.c-date-stored-predicates.b2dd8fd9cd.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1/c-date-text-and-literals/r-expr-1.c-date-text-and-literals.0cd9f9bf25.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/conv-cdate-forms/r-expr-1b.conv-cdate-forms.d5a3fec393.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/date-serial-edges/r-expr-1b.date-serial-edges.98fc78ab88.a1.json`, `evidence/r-def-expr-sql/expr-literals-date-forms/r-def-expr-sql.expr-literals-date-forms.0073d8d417.a1.json`.

**Claim (follow-up, index).** An index does not change the result. Over the payloads -1.5, -1.25, -0.25, 0, 0.25, 1.25, 45000.5, `D = CDate(-0.25)` selects the -0.25 row; `D = #12/30/1899 06:00#` selects the +0.25 row (that literal is +0.25); `D BETWEEN #12/29/1899# AND #12/31/1899#` selects -0.25, 0 and 0.25; `D < 0`, `D > -1.3`, `CDbl(D) = -0.25` (residual only) and `ORDER BY D` give the same ids and the numeric order on a table without an index and on one with an index on D built before the insert.
**Records (follow-up).** `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/c-date-index-vs-residual/r-q-expr-sql.c-date-index-vs-residual.c8a5048ad5.a1.json`.
**Open.** A second locale; Date/Time Extended; undeclared DATE parameters read back as text, so DATE-parameter arithmetic needs a declared DateTime parameter.

### R-EXPR-1 Branch D: logical and bitwise operators and their consumers
**Claim.** And/Or/Xor/Eqv/Imp/Not over True/False/Null follow Kleene-style rules (And: T,F,N,F,F,F,N,F,N in the grid order TT,TF,TN,FT,FF,FN,NT,NF,NN; `Null And 0` is 0, `Null Or -1` is -1); on non-Boolean integers they are logical, not bitwise: `6 And 3`, `6 Or 3`, `6 Eqv 3`, `6 Imp 3` and `5 And 3` are VT_I2 -1 (a bitwise And would be 2), `6 Xor 3`, `5 Xor 3` and `Not 6` are 0. A WHERE predicate selects the row for any non-Null non-zero numeric (1, -1, 2, 2.5), for '' and for the texts '1', 'q', 'True'; it rejects Null, 0 and '0'. DAO FindFirst accepts only a comparison criterion: on a fresh dynaset `Null` gives NoMatch True without error, and the bare values 0, 1, -1, 2, 2.5, '', '1', '0', 'q', 'True' each fail with 3001 "Invalid argument"; `CVErr(5)` fails 3072.
**Conditions.** DAO snapshot/dynaset, ACCDB; FindFirst asked on a fresh recordset per criterion.
**Records.** `evidence/r-expr-1/d-logical-bitwise/r-expr-1.d-logical-bitwise.952f06510f.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1/d-predicate-consumers/r-expr-1.d-predicate-consumers.cac6cb9e52.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/findfirst-fresh-recordset/r-fix-expr-sql.findfirst-fresh-recordset.eae4a4c21a.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/cmp-between-in/r-expr-1b.cmp-between-in.059527a48b.a1.json`.

**Claim (follow-up, validation).** And and Or as a field validation rule follow the three-valued tables, and the row is admitted when the rule is True or Null: `[V] > 0 And [V] < 10` admits 5 and Null and rejects -1, 15 and -7; `[V] > 0 Or [V] < -5` admits 5, 15, -7 and Null and rejects -1; `([V] > 0) And Null` admits 5, 15 and Null (True And Null is Null) and rejects -1 and -7 (False And Null is False). A rejection is 3317 (no ValidationText set) with EditMode 2.
**Records (follow-up).** `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/d-validation-andor/r-q-expr-sql.d-validation-andor.14034a8da7.a1.json`.
**Open.** And/Or in default expressions; Error-valued predicates need a host route.

### R-EXPR-1 EXPR-MUTATION, Branch D: validation rules and Required
**Claim.** A field validation rule that evaluates False rejects the Update with 3316 (EditMode stays 2); a rule that evaluates Null admits the row, so an omitted or Null value passes `[V] > 0`; Required rejects Null independently (3314); an evaluation error rejects with 3802 "Error evaluating CHECK constraint" at Update. A table rule `[V] < [R]` rejects (2,1) with 3316 and admits (1,NULL); a table rule `Null` admits every row. A bare-literal field rule reads as an equality against the field: rule `0` admits only V = 0, `2` only V = 2, `True` only V = -1, `False` only V = 0; `''`, `'1'`, `'q'` admit no non-Null value; the rule text reads back with a trailing space.
**Conditions.** DAO table-type AddNew/Update, ACCDB; fresh recordset and fresh table per case in the repaired probe.
**Records.** `evidence/r-expr-1/mut-validation-rules/r-expr-1.mut-validation-rules.9d6f3c59df.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/validation-rule-literals/r-fix-expr-sql.validation-rule-literals.e7c3545cec.a1.json`.

**Correction.** The number of a rule rejection depends on ValidationText: 3316 when the field's ValidationText is set (the message is that text), 3317 ("One or more values are prohibited by the validation rule '...'") when it is not (`evidence/r-q-expr-sql/mut-declaration-order/r-q-expr-sql.mut-declaration-order.9f1e2cbf5f.a1.json` against `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/d-validation-andor/r-q-expr-sql.d-validation-andor.14034a8da7.a1.json` and `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/validation-rule-literals/r-fix-expr-sql.validation-rule-literals.e7c3545cec.a1.json`). The "3316" in the Claim above is therefore the with-text number; the records of r-expr-1 were not re-checked for which form they used. Both numbers are the same rejection.
**Claim (follow-up).** With two invalid fields the message reported is that of the field declared first, whichever field is assigned first: MA declares A, B and reports "A must be positive" for assignment orders A,B and B,A; MB declares B, A and reports "B must be positive" in both orders. A rule that names another field is refused at field level: setting `[V] < [R]` as the V field rule fails at the property put with 3783 "cannot use multiple columns in a column-level CHECK constraint" (so (2,1), (1,2) and (1,Null) are all accepted); the same text as TableDef.ValidationRule is accepted, rejects (2,1) with 3317 and admits (1,2) and (1,Null). A rejected insert still consumes an AutoNumber (ID 4 is missing).
**Records (follow-up).** `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/mut-declaration-order/r-q-expr-sql.mut-declaration-order.9f1e2cbf5f.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/mut-field-rule-other-field/r-q-expr-sql.mut-field-rule-other-field.47e42cc479.a1.json`.
**Claim (follow-up, the implied operand).** A field rule that starts with an operator reads with its field as the left operand: U's rule `<>'bad'` (stored with a trailing NUL, as every rule here) rejects 'bad' with 3316 "U must not be bad" and admits 'u9' and 'u1'. With the bare-literal equality of the Claim above and a rule that names its field (`[V] > 0`), read as written, these are the three forms of field rule the records show.
**Records (implied operand).** `evidence/r-cursor-1/edit-validation-order/r-cursor-1.edit-validation-order.51cceca2df.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-cursor-dao/edit-validation-errors-per-failure/r-fix-cursor-dao.edit-validation-errors-per-failure.16d8e3339f.a1.json` (fixture r-cursor-1-edit).
**Open.** AllowZeroLength as a separate variable, and its default for a column that stores none; restricted-UDF rules need a host route; a complete field rule that neither names its field nor is a bare literal (`1 < 2`, `Len('a') = 1`): read as a predicate or as an equality with the field; a rule result that is a number other than -1 and 0.

### R-EXPR-1 EXPR-MUTATION, EXPR-STAGE: ValidateOnSet timing and defaults
**Claim.** With ValidateOnSet True an invalid assignment fails at the Value put (3316, source DAO.Field), the field keeps its value and EditMode is 1; with False the put succeeds (the invalid value reads back) and Update fails (3316, source DAO.Recordset); CancelUpdate restores the old value. With two invalid fields the V message is returned in both assignment orders. A DefaultValue `2+3` is visible after AddNew as VT_I4 5; `1/0` fails at the property set (0x800A000B) and `'q'` on a Long field fails 0x800A000D; an explicit Null assignment persists as Null and SQL INSERT omitting the column stores the default.
**Conditions.** DAO table recordsets, ACCDB.
**Records.** `evidence/r-expr-1/stage-validate-on-set/r-expr-1.stage-validate-on-set.6a9e23dbb6.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1/mut-default-values/r-expr-1.mut-default-values.bf7d388dd2.a1.json`.

**Claim (follow-up, clock and random defaults).** A DefaultValue of `Now()` or `Rnd()` is sampled when AddNew is called and held until Update: T and R read the same 2.1 s after AddNew, the stored row equals the AddNew values, a second AddNew samples again (new T, Rnd 0.5795 against 0.5334), and a further AddNew after CancelUpdate samples again (Rnd 0.2896; T equals the cancelled AddNew's because the clock second had not advanced). Reading T and R after Update fails (no current record).
**Records (follow-up).** `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/mut-default-now-rnd/r-q-expr-sql.mut-default-now-rnd.39f98e5c75.a1.json`.
**Open.** Transaction and multi-process interplay belongs to R-CURSOR-1/R-TXN-1; restricted-UDF defaults need a host route.

### R-EXPR-1 Branch E: conditional functions and selection rules
**Claim.** IIf evaluates only the selected arm for division by zero and text conversion failures: `IIf(True,1,1/0)` is R8 1, `IIf(False,1/0,2)` is 2, `IIf(True,1,CInt('q'))` is 1, also with row-dependent `1/[Denominator]` at Denominator 0. Switch and Choose fail at OpenRecordset when any constant arm fails (`Switch(True,1,True,1/0)`, `Choose(1,'a',1/0)` 0x800A000B). In `1/0 + CInt('q')` the first operand's error is reported and reversing reports the other. IIf: Null condition selects the false arm, `IIf('',1,2)` is 1, `IIf(True,1,'a')` is Text "1"; `IIf(True)` fails 0x800A0C03. Switch with no true pair is Null; `Switch(True)` and `Switch(True,1,False)` fail 0x800A0C03; Choose with index 0, 3, 0.5, -0.5 is Null, 1.5 gives arm 1, 2.5 arm 2 (round half to even), Null index fails 0x800A0D88. Nesting 10 IIf deep works; 20 fails "too complex".
**Conditions.** DAO, constants and one row-dependent IIf; ACCDB.
**Records.** `evidence/r-expr-1/e-eager-failures/r-expr-1.e-eager-failures.c9c723c692.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1/e-conditional-selection/r-expr-1.e-conditional-selection.a85f932a4b.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1/e-failure-phase/r-expr-1.e-failure-phase.6279e4757f.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/cond-iif-forms/r-expr-1b.cond-iif-forms.697f48b067.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/cond-switch-choose-forms/r-expr-1b.cond-switch-choose-forms.393479eddd.a1.json`.

**Claim (follow-up, rows).** With Ev (Id, Denominator, TextValue) = (1,5,'1'), (2,0,'x'), (3,2,'7'): `Switch(True,1,True,1/[Denominator])` and `Choose(1,'a',1/[Denominator])` fail on the Value read of row 2 although the first arm is selected, and succeed on rows 1 and 3, so Switch and Choose evaluate every value argument per row and a division by zero in an unselected arm surfaces. The error reported is the leftmost failing argument's, in Switch, Choose and `+`: `Switch(Id = 2, CInt([TextValue]), Id = 2, 1/[Denominator])` reports 3464 and the reversed order reports 11; the same for `Choose(1, ...)` and `CInt(...) + 1/...`. `IIf(Id = 2, X, Y)` reports the selected arm's failure (1/0 gives 11, CInt('x') gives 3464). With `WHERE Id = 999` Switch and Choose open with no rows and no error.
**Records (follow-up).** `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/e-row-arms/r-q-expr-sql.e-row-arms.583c2c52e5.a1.json`.
**Open.** IIf with an unselected failing arm in a row rests on the earlier record only (`IIf(True,1,1/[Denominator])` on Denominator 0 gives 1); marker UDF and object default-member order need a host route; the nesting limit between 10 and 20 is not bracketed.

### R-EXPR-1 EXPR-STAGE, R-SQL-1 SQL-DEMAND, R-PAR-1 PAR-ERROR: where a row error surfaces
**Reopened and qualified (2026-11-20, W-06/parser-binder-scan replay; r-q3-sql/constant-phase, constant-phase-2).** "A constant failing expression fails at OpenRecordset" holds only for a constant expression that contains a function call. Such an expression is evaluated once at OpenRecordset with oleaut32's operator rules: a failure there (division by zero, a type mismatch, a function's domain error, a conversion's overflow) fails the open (`CDbl(1)/0`, `1/CDbl(0)`, `Abs(1)/0`, `1/0+Abs(1)`, `CDbl(1/0)`, `'q'+CInt(7)`, `CStr('q'+7)`, `CByte(300)`, `Sqr(-1)`, `Left('ab', -1)`); its Long overflow widens to Double there (`Abs(CLng(2147483647)+CLng(1))` reads 2147483648), and a folded value that does not fit the column's static type fails at the Value read with 6 (`CLng(2147483647)+1`, `CLng(2147483647)*CLng(2)`), while a conversion around it fails at open (`CLng(CLng(2147483647)+CLng(1))`). An expression of operators over literals alone is evaluated at the Value read (`1/0`, `1\0`, `1 Mod 0`, `0/0`, `(1+0)/0`, `'q'+7`, `-'q'`), so its failure is the read's. This also reads the kernel's rule (1) of the note "inner result types follow oleaut32" (a Long overflow widens only between literals) as the static-type conversion at the read. Open: a function-bearing expression over a field (not constant), and Date and Currency folds.
**Claim (qualified 2026-11-20, R-EXPR-1 Branch D (In); r-q3-sql/in-phase).** An In whose operands are constant is evaluated at OpenRecordset, as a function-bearing constant is, wherever its failing part sits; Between with the same operands is evaluated at the read: `('q'+7) In (1, 2)`, `'x' In (1, 2)`, `1 In ('1', 2)`, `5 In (1, '5')` fail 3464 and `1 In (1/0, 2)`, `(1/0) In (1, 2)` fail 11 from DAO.Database, while `('q'+7) Between 1 And 2`, `1 Between 1/0 And 2`, `(1/0) Between 1 And 2`, `5 Between '1' And '9'` fail at the Value read from DAO.Field. Over a field the failure is the read's for both (`K In (1, 2)` 3464, `(ID/0) In (1, 2)` 11, `ID Between 'x' And 'y'` 3464). The comparisons run in list order and stop at the first True: `1 In (1, 'x')` and `ID In (1, 'x')` are -1, the mismatching element never compared. A comparison with a Null element counts as False: `1 In (Null, 2)` is 0, `3 In (1, 2)` 0, `Null In (1, 2)` Null; so `NOT IN (1, Null)` keeps the rows that are neither 1 nor Null (`evidence/r-sql-13b/sub-in-list-null-forms/`). **Records.** `evidence/r-q3-sql/in-phase/`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/cmp-between-in/`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/sub-in-list-null-forms/`. Discriminating: the failing part in the left operand, a list element and a bound; constants against fields; In against Between with the same operands; a match before and after a mismatching element. **Open.** A widened Long overflow under an operator (`CLng(2147483647)+CLng(1)+1`), named `NotModelled.FoldWidening`; In with a subquery (W-06).
**Claim.** Argument-count and parse errors, an undefined function, a failing expression that every aggregate, GROUP BY, DISTINCT, UNION or ORDER BY key must read, and a constant failing expression (`1/0`, `CByte(300)`) fail at OpenRecordset. A failing projected scalar on one row fails only the Value read of that field (1/[Denominator], `CInt([TextValue])`, `1/zm`): OpenRecordset, MoveNext, MoveLast, EOF and RecordCount succeed and neighbouring rows read normally; re-reading the row or moving back onto it raises again. A WHERE that matches no row opens with no rows. A failing WHERE term makes MoveNext fail (3071) at the bad row and the row is absent. A failing projection beyond a TOP cutoff raises nothing. GetRows stops silently before a bad projected row (a short batch, no error). The behaviour is identical for snapshot, dynaset and forward-only recordsets and on MDB.
**Conditions.** DAO snapshot, dynaset and forward-only; ACCDB and MDB (`err-snapshot-mid-bad-row-step-mdb`); ER fixture of 240 rows, bad rows at ids 3, 60, 80, 100, 150, 240.
**Records.** `evidence/r-expr-1/e-failure-phase/r-expr-1.e-failure-phase.6279e4757f.a1.json`, `evidence/r-par-1/err-snapshot-mid-bad-row-step/r-par-1.err-snapshot-mid-bad-row-step.75174593c2.a1.json`, `evidence/r-par-1/err-snapshot-mid-bad-row-step-mdb/r-par-1.err-snapshot-mid-bad-row-step-mdb.a8915d290f.a1.json`, `evidence/r-par-1/err-dynaset-mid-bad-row-step/r-par-1.err-dynaset-mid-bad-row-step.f8bad47fee.a1.json`, `evidence/r-par-1/err-forwardonly-mid-bad-row-step/r-par-1.err-forwardonly-mid-bad-row-step.ba94fa4faa.a1.json`, `evidence/r-par-1/err-revisit-bad-row/r-par-1.err-revisit-bad-row.3ddc406403.a1.json`, `evidence/r-par-1/err-getrows-chunks/r-par-1.err-getrows-chunks.c24e55f2b8.a1.json`, `evidence/r-par-1/err-two-errors-two-rows/r-par-1.err-two-errors-two-rows.8d5129947d.a1.json`, `evidence/r-par-1/err-expression-kinds-phase/r-par-1.err-expression-kinds-phase.0bfd412e3f.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/err-last-bad-row-bounded/r-fix-expr-sql.err-last-bad-row-bounded.0ef74b606a.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/err-aggregate-attributed/r-fix-expr-sql.err-aggregate-attributed.7a37b263e1.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/err-where-order-attributed/r-fix-expr-sql.err-where-order-attributed.c876ba4187.a1.json`, `evidence/r-par-1/err-top-stops-before-bad-row/r-par-1.err-top-stops-before-bad-row.db1df3b225.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-1/demand-conversion/r-sql-1.demand-conversion.a4bbf6a923.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-2-3/top-demand/r-sql-2-3.top-demand.a543dfd216.a1.json`.

**Claim (follow-up, saved QueryDefs).** The phases hold through a saved QueryDef opened with QueryDef.OpenRecordset (Bad: z = 0 at id 3 of 5). A projected `1/z` opens, fails only on the Value read of row 3, MoveLast and RecordCount (5) succeed, rows 4 and 5 read; with ORDER BY id DESC the same. `WHERE 1/z > 0` opens, the MoveNext onto the bad row fails and the row is absent (RecordCount 4). `Sum(1/z)` and a crosstab with `PIVOT 1/z` fail at open. `TOP 2 ... ORDER BY id` (cutoff before the bad row) raises nothing; `TOP 4` fails on the Value read of row 3.
**Records (follow-up).** `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/demand-saved-querydefs/r-q-expr-sql.demand-saved-querydefs.03c2aee28d.a1.json`.
**Open.** Requery after an error (crashes ACE), an unread speculative page error and aggregate-before-callback order stay T. The Errors reads after MoveLast in that record show stale entries: a successful call neither clears nor adds Errors.

### R-EXPR-1 EXPR-INVENTORY: text to number conversion grammar
**Claim.** CInt/CLng/CByte accept `' 12 '`, `'1e2'`(100), `'&H10'`(16), `'&O17'`(15), `'+5'`, `'1.5E+2'`(150) and `'1 2'`(12); `'(12)'` converts to -12; `'&HFFFF'` is -1 for CInt and 65535 for CLng; `'1,234'`, `'12abc'`, `''`, `'$12'` and full-width digits fail 0x800A0D88; CDbl of `'1E+400'` overflows (6), `'1,5'`, `'NaN'`, `'Infinity'` fail 0x800A0D88. `Val('1d3')` is 1000, `Val('&HFFFF')` is -1, `Val(Null)` fails 0x800A0D88. Null arguments: CInt(Null) and `Mid('a',1,Null)` fail 0x800A005E; `Abs/Int/Fix/Round/Trim/UCase/Hex/Oct(Null)` and `InStr(Null,'a')` return Null.
**Conditions.** DAO, ACCDB, en-ZA, one expression per snapshot.
**Records.** `evidence/r-expr-1b/conv-text-to-integer/r-expr-1b.conv-text-to-integer.b0637e2413.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/conv-text-to-floating/r-expr-1b.conv-text-to-floating.3213e9b55c.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/conv-val-forms/r-expr-1b.conv-val-forms.614ad20e3c.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/null-propagation-functions/r-expr-1b.null-propagation-functions.b2d04aed9f.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1/a-optional-arguments/r-expr-1.a-optional-arguments.7b1e2e3cf6.a1.json`.
**Open.** Locale-bound results (en-ZA only); which locale decides them is the next note. This is a sample of the function manifest, not the manifest.

### R-EXPR-1 EXPR-LOCALE (5zh9): ACE's text conversions take the user locale, not the thread locale or the invoke LCID
**Claim.** A DAO `Field.Value` put of text and the expression service's conversions (CDbl, CCur, CDate, CStr, CBool,
Format, `'1,5' + 0`) give the same answer whatever the caller's thread locale and the LCID of its `IDispatch::Invoke` are.
Seven variants gave identical results, call by call: thread locale 1031 or 1033 set before `DAO.DBEngine.120` is created,
1031 set after it, invoke LCID 1031, 1033 or 7177, and invoke 1031 with thread 1033. The answers are the user locale's
answers with the user's own settings (en-ZA, `sDecimal` ".", `sShortDate` yyyy/MM/dd):
- `"1.5"` puts into Double, Currency and Bit; `"1,5"`, `"1.234,5"`, `"1,234.5"` and `"1 234,5"` are 3421 from
  DAO.Field (with an IErrorInfo) for Double, Currency and Bit, also under German.
- `"Yes"`, `"True"` and `"Wahr"` are 3421 into Bit; `"-1"` puts.
- Into Date/Time, `"1,5"` puts as 2026-01-05 (a date of the current year), `"3/4/2020"` and `"2020/03/04"` as
  2020-03-04, and `"13/4/2020"` as 2020-04-13; `"4.3.2020"` is 3421.
- `CStr(1.5)` and `Format(1.5)` are `"1.5"`, and `CStr(#2020-03-04 13:30#)` is `"2020/03/04 13:30:00"` (the user's short
  date). `CDbl('1,5')`, `CCur('1,5')` and `CBool('Wahr')` are 3464 at OpenRecordset, from DAO.Database; `'1,5' + 0`
  is 3464 at the value read, from DAO.Field; `Val('1,5')` is 1.
- In FindFirst criteria a text literal against a Double or Date/Time field, and a number against a Text field, are
  3464 "Data type mismatch in criteria expression." from DAO.Recordset; `S = '1,5'` finds no row.
So CalcDb's rule is one locale per process: the user default locale with its overrides (`LOCALE_USER_DEFAULT`, the
locale that oleaut32's `VariantChangeType` uses). The caller's thread locale and invoke LCID are not consulted.
**Conditions.** DAO, ACCDB created with `;LANGID=0x0409;CP=1252;COUNTRY=0`, STA, native IDispatch caller; user locale
en-ZA with the settings above; one process per variant.
**Records.** `evidence/r-expr-locale/` (thread-1031, thread-1033, thread-1031-late, invoke-1031, invoke-1033, invoke-7177,
invoke-1031-thread-1033).
**Open.** Whether ACE reads the user's overrides or the locale's defaults: deciding it needs another user locale or
other settings for the run's account, which is an owner setup step. Whether the database's LANGID or a QueryDef's
locale changes conversions has not been asked.

### R-EXPR-1 Part C / EXPR-LOCALE (`r-q47-bigint-expr-edges`): `$` / `Format` / `Date*` / `StrConv` metadata matrix & `LANGID=0x0407` locale edges
- **Claim.**
  - *Metadata matrix & `Null` precedence*: across ACCDB & Jet 4 MDB, `String$`, `Space$`, `LTrim$`, `RTrim$`, `Right$`, `Mid$`, `Left$`, `Chr$`, `Format$`, `Format`, `FormatNumber`, `WeekdayName`, and `StrConv` report **DAO** `Type=10, Size=0`, **ADO** `Type=203 (adLongVarWChar), DefinedSize=536870910, Precision=255, NumericScale=255`, and **`SELECT...INTO`** `TableDef.Field.Type=10, Size=255` (even `String$(300,'a')`); `DateAdd` reports DAO `8/8`, ADO `7/8/255/255`; `DateDiff`/`DatePart` report DAO `4/4`, ADO `3/4/10/255`; `Weekday`/`Year` report DAO `3/2`, ADO `2/2/5/255`. `Null` into any of the 8 `$` functions or `WeekdayName(Null)` fails `3464` (`0x800A0D88`) at `OpenRecordset` (literal), `Value` (stored Null), or `Execute` (`SELECT...INTO`), and `0x80040E07` in ADO; on an ad-hoc ADO `SELECT`, row-level expression `3464` (`meta-dollar-str-dao-ado-accdb` `#340`: `SELECT Left$(Mm, 5) FROM Lg WHERE ID = 2`) returns `VT_EMPTY` on ADO `Field.Value` (vs `3464` on DAO `Field.Value`). `DateAdd`, `DateDiff`, `DatePart`, `Weekday`, `Year`, and `StrConv` propagate `Null` (`VT_NULL`) with identical static schema, whereas `Format$`, `Format`, `FormatNumber`, and `FormatDateTime` do **not** propagate `VT_NULL`: they return `VT_BSTR ""` (or the 4th format section for `Format$`, per `meta-fmt-date-strconv-dao-ado-accdb` `#474`/`#481`/`#1005`, `r-expr-1b/null-propagation-functions`, and `r-expr-1b/sandbox-vba-library` `#85`/`#282`), while `Format(1, Null)` fails `94`.
  - *German `LANGID=0x0407`, `StrConv`, `FormatNumber`, & `Date*` edges*: `ThreadLocale(1031)+InvokeLcid=1031`, `1033`, AND German DB `CollatingOrder=1031` (`;LANGID=0x0407`) produce identical user-default (`7177` en-ZA) answers (`R1 234.56`, `"5 March 2024"`, `"Sunday"`, `CDbl('1,5')` `3464`, `CDate('4.3.2020')` `3464`, `StrConv('straße', 1)="STRAßE"`). `StrConv` with `LCID=1055` maps Turkish dotless/dotted I (`"IIİı"`/`"iiİı"`); combined flags (`5,6,7,9,10,17,20,40,65,129,192`) fail `5` (`0x800A0005`); `vbFromUnicode(128)`+`vbUnicode(64)` round-trips via ANSI CP. `FormatNumber` accepts TriState `-2/-1/0` and fails `5` on `NumDigits=-2` or TriState `1`/`-3`; `Date*` fails `3464` on Null non-date args (`interval`, `number`, `fdow`, `fwoy`, `abbreviate`), and `DateAdd('w', ±5)` steps calendar days (`±5`).
- **Records.** `evidence/r-q47-bigint-expr-edges/{meta-*,locale-*,fmt-formatnumber-tristate-edges-accdb,date-system-week-null-edges-accdb}` (10 probes).
- **Open.** Non-en-ZA OS user locale (requires owner account setup); `FormatCurrency(Null)`/`FormatPercent(Null)` unrecorded.

### R-EXPR-1 Branch B, EXPR-RUNTIME: inner result types follow oleaut32's Var* rules, with eight ACE rules above them
**Claim.** The inner value of an operator or numeric function (what `VarType` sees) is what oleaut32 computes: `VarAdd`, `VarSub`, `VarMul`, `VarDiv`, `VarIdiv`, `VarMod`, `VarPow`, `VarNeg`, `VarAbs`, `VarInt`, `VarFix`, `VarRound` and `VariantChangeTypeEx` under LCID 7177 give ACE's types, values and text forms (Byte + Byte overflow is Integer, Integer overflow is Long, Single / Single is Single, Currency + Double is Currency, Date + Decimal is Decimal, text + text concatenates, `Abs(CInt(-32768))` is Long; the conversion grammar of `CInt('(12)')`, `CDbl('1,5')`, `CBool('FALSE')` and the text of `CStr(1/3)` or `#2024-03-05# & ''`). ACE adds these rules: (1) a Long result that overflows widens to Double only when both operands are literals (`2147483647 + 1`, `65536 * 65536`, `-2147483648 - 1`); with a typed source (`CLng(…)`, a stored Long) it fails 6, where `VarAdd` widens; (2) unary minus overflowing Integer or Long fails 6 (`-CInt(-32768)`, `-CLng(-2147483648)`), where `VarNeg` widens; (3) `^` fails 440 where `VarPow` answers E_INVALIDARG (`0 ^ -1`, `(-2) ^ 0.5`); (4) `Round` with negative digits fails 5 and with Null digits 3464; (5) `CByte` checks Byte's range and returns an Integer (`VarType(CVar(CByte(1)))` is 2), and `CBool`, `True`, comparisons, `IsNull` and the logical operators return VT_I2 -1/0, never VT_BOOL; (6) `And`, `Or`, `Xor`, `Eqv`, `Imp`, `Not` are logical with Kleene tables, not bitwise; (7) a number against text fails 3464, a date against text is less than any text with no conversion, and a Decimal against a Single compares equal where `VarCmp` does not (`0.1 = CSng(0.1)` is -1); (8) `CDate` of a numeric text is that number of days (`CDate('45000')`), which `VarDateFromStr` refuses. The mapping of oleaut32 failures is fixed: DISP_E_TYPEMISMATCH 3464, DISP_E_OVERFLOW 6 (`0 / 0` included), DISP_E_DIVBYZERO 11; a Null into a value conversion is 94.
**Conditions.** DAO SELECT over literals and conversion functions, ACCDB, en-ZA (LCID 7177); oleaut32 of the same Windows build, called in process by the kernel (W-02/shared-scalar-services, `src/CalcDb.Values/Scalar/ScalarKernel.cs`).
**Records.** The 27 records of `tests/CalcDb.Values.Tests/Scalar/ExpressionCatalog.cs`, among them `evidence/r-expr-1b/arith-overflow-promotion/r-expr-1b.arith-overflow-promotion.8e33d497ff.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/arith-intdiv-mod-types/r-expr-1b.arith-intdiv-mod-types.dee0e58c4e.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/info-result-vartype-numeric/r-expr-1b.info-result-vartype-numeric.2ca10df6b5.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/num-power-operator/r-expr-1b.num-power-operator.0c7a57c1f3.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/conv-round-function-digits/r-expr-1b.conv-round-function-digits.8f79dca0d3.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/conv-cbool-cvar/r-expr-1b.conv-cbool-cvar.6ee08b62c8.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/cmp-cross-types/r-expr-1b.cmp-cross-types.e898adce72.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/conv-cdate-forms/r-expr-1b.conv-cdate-forms.d5a3fec393.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1/d-logical-bitwise/r-expr-1.d-logical-bitwise.952f06510f.a1.json`. The kernel matches 747 of their answers, value and inner VarType, and names 21 as not modelled (below). Discriminating: `CLng(2147483647) + CLng(1)` against `2147483647 + 1` (origin, not tag, decides rule 1); `VarType(CVar(CByte(1)))` (rule 5); `CSng(0.1) < 0.1` and `> 0.1` both 0 (rule 7 is equality, not an ordering artefact).
**Open.** Each is a named `NotModelledCase` in the kernel: Database text compare needs the sort order's collation (W-04/weight-tables-per-sort-order, `DatabaseCollationMissing`: `'a' = 'A'`, `'a' = 'a '`, `'æ' = 'ae'` are -1, `'ä' = 'a'` 0); Text compare mode (StrComp 1) flags (`TextCompareMode`); a Long overflow whose operand is an operator's result, e.g. `(2147483647 + 0) + 1` (`LongOverflowOfExpression`); stored Decimal arithmetic (`StoredDecimalArithmetic`, dec-scale-compare); BIGINT with Decimal (`BigIntWithDecimal`); whether a Decimal–Single compare widens the Single or narrows the Decimal, decided by `CSng(0.1) = 0.1000000015` (`FloatingAgainstDecimalCompare`); text '' or '0' and a date as logical operands, decided by `'' And True`, `'0' And True`, `#12/30/1899# And True` (`TextTruthValue`, `DateTruthValue`); `CStr(Null)`, `Sgn(Null)` (`NullArgument`); one-argument `Nz` (`NzOmittedReplacement`, Access host); a VT_BOOL operand of `&`, CStr or Len (`BooleanToText`, needs a stored Yes/No field). The literal typing of `-0.0` (VarType Long in `Abs(-0.0)`) is the parser's question (W-06).

### R-EXPR-1 Branch B (nk97, r-q43-schema-lval-index): Boolean comparisons by operand origin and operator
- **Claim.** A Boolean compared with a non-Boolean depends on both the operator and the Boolean operand's origin (not runtime `VarType` alone):
  1. **Stored `YESNO` column (`Y`)**: compares strictly numerically as `-1`/`0` against numbers (`Y = 2` → 0, `Y = -1` → -1, `Y < 1` → -1), and always fails **3464** against text (literal or column, all six operators; at `Field.Value` in `SELECT` and at `OpenRecordset` in `WHERE`).
  2. **`True`/`False` literal with `=` / `<>`**: coerces the other non-Null operand by SQL predicate truthiness (`0`, `#12/30/1899#`, and `'0'` are False; all other numbers, dates, and strings including `''`, `'False'`, `'x'`, `' '` are True), symmetric in operand order; `True`/`False <> Null` → -1 (right-literal Null binder rule), `= Null` / `Null op B` → `Null`.
  3. **`True`/`False` literal with `<`, `<=`, `>`, `>=`**: compares numerically against Boolean/number/date (`True = -1`, `False = 0`); against text (literal or column), **`True` fails 3464** in both orders while **`False` compares as `"0"` in Database (General) collation** (`'' < False` → -1, `False > '!'` → -1, `False <= '0'` & `>= '0'` → -1, `False < 'a'` → -1).
  4. **Computed `VT_I2` Boolean expression (`(1=1)`, `(S <> 'a')`, `CBool(...)`)**: compares numerically `-1`/`0` against numbers; against text (literal or column, all six operators) stringifies to `"-1"` / `"0"` and compares in Database (General) collation (plain integer literals `0 = '0'`, `-1 < 'a'` still fail 3464).
- **Conditions.** DAO `SELECT` and `WHERE`, ACCDB and Jet 4 MDB.
- **Records.** `evidence/r-q43-schema-lval-index/bool-cmp-eq-ne-{accdb,mdb}/`, `bool-cmp-order-{accdb,mdb}/`, `bool-cmp-origin-accdb/`, `bool-cmp-text-disc-accdb/`; `evidence/r-expr-1b/cmp-cross-types/`; `evidence/r-q42-field-rule-and/edge-forms-{accdb,mdb}/`.
- **Built (`alta/nk97-bool-roddl`, `6827a95c`)**: lifted `NotModelledCase.BooleanComparison` across `CalcDb.Values`/`Expressions`/`Sql` for all 4 origins (plus `OperandContract.CIntFunctionResult` `-1`/`0` equality vs text and `OperandOrigin.Derived` keeping derived/UNION Boolean columns refused by name); scoped single-table ordered `WHERE` 3464-at-Open to stored `YESNO` comparisons (`r-expr-1b` `3->7/62`).
- **Open.** Other collations than General; stored `YESNO` field vs Date/Time; derived/UNION Boolean columns, Boolean parameter vs non-Boolean, `CInt` outside `-1`/`0` equality, zero-like spellings other than `'0'`.

**Claim (qualified 2026-11-20, R-EXPR-1 conversions; r-q3-sql/cdate-bounds).** `CDate` accepts a day from -657434 to 2958465 with any fraction (`CDate(-657434)`, `CDate(-657434.5)`, `CDate(2958465)`, `CDate(2958465.999999999)` read as those Date values) and fails 3464 beyond it, where `VariantChangeType` overflows (6): `CDate(-657435)`, `CDate(2958466)`, `CDate(1E+300)`, the same with a Double, Currency or Long operand, the numeric texts `'2958466'` and `'-657435'`, and `CVDate(2958466)`; as a call over constants it fails at OpenRecordset. **Records.** `evidence/r-q3-sql/cdate-bounds/`, `evidence/r-expr-1/c-date-text-and-literals/` (both records), `evidence/r-wire/expr-conversion-tags/`. **Open.** The range of a date text in date form. (CVDate is built since nxk2, 30866884: CVDate(Null) is Null where CDate(Null) fails 3464, `evidence/r-expr-1b/sandbox-vba-library` #335, #342.)

**Observation (W-06 binder).** `1 <> Null` is -1 and `Null <> Null` is 0, while `Null <> 1` is Null (`evidence/r-expr-1b/cmp-cross-types/r-expr-1b.cmp-cross-types.e898adce72.a1.json` against `evidence/r-expr-1/a-null-empty-error-literals/r-expr-1.a-null-empty-error-literals.f2882098b8.a1.json`): with a literal Null on the right, `<>` reads as `Is Not Null`, a binding rule, not the operator's. Over a stored field the same holds: Find `Q <> Null` finds exactly the rows where Q is not Null, and `Q = Null` finds none (`evidence/r-cursor-1b/find-null-forms/r-cursor-1b.find-null-forms.8ba762ea70.a1.json`). Open: `1 = Null` (Null or 0: a Find cannot tell), and `Null <> x` with a field.

### R-EXPR-1 EXPR-RUNTIME: Rnd and Now sampling in queries
**Claim.** In one query execution `Rnd()` returns the same value in every row; different executions give different values; `Rnd(Id)` differs per row; `Rnd(0)` repeats the preceding `Rnd()` in the same row; `Now()` is identical across rows and columns. `Rnd(Null)` fails 0x800A005E.
**Conditions.** DAO, ACCDB, no `Randomize`.
**Records.** `evidence/r-expr-1/runtime-rnd-now/r-expr-1.runtime-rnd-now.de5f5b536a.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/rnd-seeded-sequences/r-expr-1b.rnd-seeded-sequences.4cc9f2ea4b.a1.json`.

**Records (follow-up).** Field defaults of Now() and Rnd() are in the ValidateOnSet and defaults note (`evidence/r-q-expr-sql/mut-default-now-rnd/r-q-expr-sql.mut-default-now-rnd.39f98e5c75.a1.json`).
**Open.** VBA interleaving and a forced physical replan need a host route.

### R-EXPR-1 EXPR-INVENTORY: length and start arguments of the text functions
**Claim.** `Left`, `Right` and `Left$` refuse a length above 1073741823 (2^30 − 1) or below 0 with 5 "Invalid procedure call" (1073741823 answers the whole text, 1073741824 and 2147483647 fail; 2147483648, beyond Long, fails 6). The bound is on the argument, not the text: `Left('', 2147483647)` fails too, while `Left(Null, 2147483647)` is Null. `Mid`'s length has the same bound (`Mid('abcd', 2, 1073741823)` is "bcd", `2147483646` fails 5); its start may be at most 1073741824 (2^30): `Mid('abcd', 1073741824)` and `Mid('abcd', 1073741824, 1)` are "", and 1073741825, 1610612736, 2147483646 and 2147483647 fail 5 with or without a length (also a length 0) and on an empty text; 2147483648 fails 6. A start of 0 or a length of −1 fail 5. The byte forms `LeftB`, `RightB` and `MidB` accept 2147483647. `InStr`'s start 1073741824 answers 0 and 2147483647 fails 5. With every operand a literal the failure comes at OpenRecordset; with the length computed from a field (`Left('abcd', ID * 1073741824)`, `Left('abcd', 1073741823 + ID)`) the recordset opens and the Value read fails.
**Conditions.** DAO SELECT over one row, ACCDB, en-ZA (LCID 7177).
**Records.** `evidence/r-q4-values/left-bounds/`, `evidence/r-q4-values/bounds-2/`, `evidence/r-q4-values/bounds-3/` (Mid's start, bisected after the earlier text of this note contradicted left-bounds #102, #106), `evidence/r-expr-1b/str-mid-left-right-edges/` (#88, #290). Discriminators: lengths at each power of two from 2^15 to 2^31 with the text fixed; an empty and a Null text at the failing length; Right, Mid, the $ and B forms at the same bound; a literal against a field operand.
**Open.** `InStr`'s start bound between 1073741824 and 2147483647; `Space` and `String` at the bound (a result that large cannot be made, so the order of the checks is not asked).

### R-EXPR-1 PAT-01: LIKE wildcard grammar by route
**Claim.** DAO SQL `LIKE` uses the Access grammar: `*` and `?` are wildcards, `%` and `_` are literal (`LIKE 'Q*'` matches all 7 subjects, `LIKE 'Q%'` only "Q%"). `ALIKE` through DAO and `LIKE` through ADO use the ANSI grammar (`%`, `_`). The grammar is selected by the operator/route, not by the pattern.
**Conditions.** DAO and ADO (Microsoft.ACE.OLEDB.12.0), ACCDB, default sort.
**Records.** `evidence/r-expr-1/pat-01-wildcard-modes/r-expr-1.pat-01-wildcard-modes.8a80744779.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-1/b-ado/r-sql-1.b-ado.7c2ddbebd8.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/like-wildcard-forms/r-sql-13b.like-wildcard-forms.72ed92cece.a1.json`.
**Open.** The database ANSI-query option and module Option Compare need Access (see status T).

### R-EXPR-1 PAT-02, PAT-04, PAT-05: bracket classes and malformed patterns
**Claim.** `LIKE` and `ALIKE` agree on classes: `[]` matches only the empty string, `[[]` matches "[", `[[]]` "[]", `[!a]` excludes, while `[!]` and `[^]` are literal (`[^a-c]` is the same set as `[a-c]`; `^` is not a negation marker). `[C-A]` and the malformed `[`, `[a`, `[a-`, `a[`, `[z-a]`, `[*`, `[#` fail with "Invalid pattern string" (0x800A005D / error 93) but only for rows where the engine reaches the fault: `q[` fails for subjects "qx" and "qabc" and not for "x", "q", "" or "yq"; `xq[` fails only for "xqx"; the constant `'x' LIKE 'q['` returns 0.
**Conditions.** DAO, ACCDB.
**Records.** `evidence/r-expr-1/pat-02-bracket-classes/r-expr-1.pat-02-bracket-classes.1040bfba7a.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1/pat-03-ranges/r-expr-1.pat-03-ranges.c90e050e13.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1/pat-04-05-structural-diagnostics/r-expr-1.pat-04-05-structural-diagnostics.7cb5b6d540.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/like-bracket-classes/r-sql-13b.like-bracket-classes.ced9bd70e4.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/like-malformed-patterns/r-sql-13b.like-malformed-patterns.d5808645fc.a1.json`.
**Open.** ANSI-92 counterparts through ADO are covered only for PAT-01.

### R-EXPR-1 PAT-03: class ranges compare lowercased code units
**Claim.** A class range `[x-y]` and the unit tested against it are both mapped to lowercase, unit by unit, and then compared as UTF-16 code units; collation plays no part. So `[a-c]` and `[A-C]` match a, A, b, B, c, C and not ä, á, à or æ; `[z-à]` (7A..E0) matches z, Z, {, ½, ×, ß, à and À but not á or ä; `[à-æ]` and `[À-Æ]` match à, á, ä, æ in both cases and not a; `[×-÷]` matches ×, ß, à..æ, ÷ and not ø or ÿ; `[ß-ÿ]` matches Ÿ (U+0178, lowercase ÿ); `[_-a]` matches _, ` and a; `[Z-a]` is a reversed range after the mapping (z..a) and fails 93. The mapping is one-to-one and leaves some units alone: İ (U+0130) does not match `[h-j]` or `i`, the Kelvin sign (U+212A) not `[j-l]` or `k`, ſ (U+017F) not `[r-t]`, the Ohm sign (U+2126) not `[ω]`, the micro sign (U+00B5) not `[μ]` (which matches Μ, U+039C), and ǅ (U+01C5) not `[ǆ]` (which matches Ǆ, U+01C4); Cyrillic ж and Ж match each other, as do Ā and ā. A single literal unit uses the same mapping (`'ä'` and `'Ä'` match ä and Ä).
**Conditions.** DAO SQL `LIKE` over a Text(1) column, ACCDB, General sort, en-ZA.
**Records.** `evidence/r-q4-values/like-ranges/`, `evidence/r-q4-values/like-null-fold/`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/like-collation-ranges/`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/like-bracket-classes/`, `evidence/r-expr-1/pat-03-ranges/`. Discriminators: ranges whose code-unit, case-folded and collation readings differ ([z-à], [×-÷], [ß-ÿ], [Z-a], [_-a]); upper against lower bounds ([à-æ] against [À-Æ]); units without a simple Latin-1 case pair.
**Open.** Which Windows mapping it is (the units above decide between candidates); ranges under a Spanish or Turkish sort; ALIKE and ADO routes.

### R-EXPR-1 PAT-06: Null operands and row patterns
**Claim.** `Null LIKE 'q['` is Null with no error (the invalid pattern is not visited); `'' LIKE '*'` is -1 and `'' LIKE '?'` is 0; undeclared parameters holding Empty or "" arrive as Null. `t LIKE t` over a table without bracket rows matches every non-Null row and is Null for a Null row; `CStr(dt) LIKE` over a Null row fails 94 "Invalid use of Null". A Null subject against a pattern whose value is exactly `*` is False, not Null: `Null LIKE '*'` is 0 and `NOT (Null LIKE '*')` is -1, so `c NOT LIKE '*'` and `NOT (c LIKE '*')` keep only the Null row; the same for a computed operand (`(c + '') LIKE '*'`) and a computed pattern (`'*' & ''`), so it is a rule of the operator, not a rewrite of a column predicate. Every other pattern over Null is Null (`'**'`, `'?*'`, `'a*'`, `'*a'`, `'a'`, and `c LIKE Null`), so `NOT LIKE` keeps no Null row there.
**Conditions.** DAO SQL, ACCDB.
**Records.** `evidence/r-expr-1/pat-06-null-and-operand-order/r-expr-1.pat-06-null-and-operand-order.30f6a5a062.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/like-clean-operands/r-fix-expr-sql.like-clean-operands.713010bb35.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/like-null-pattern-edges/r-sql-13b.like-null-pattern-edges.891651cea6.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q4-values/like-ranges/`, `evidence/r-q4-values/like-null-fold/`. Discriminators for the `*` rule: a column against a computed subject and a computed pattern; `*` against `**` and `?*`; the constant against a row.

### R-EXPR-1 PAT-07, PAT-08, PAT-09, PAT-18: letters, expansions and composition
