### R-EXPR-1 PAT-11, PAT-16: counted U+0000 and pattern reuse
**Claim.** Counted U+0000 is preserved in stored subjects and in parameter values: Len of S = 'A', 'A'+NUL, 'A'+NUL+'B', 'A'+NUL+'C' is 1, 2, 3, 3. For pattern 'A', `S = pp` is true for 'A' and 'A'+NUL (a trailing NUL is ignored by `=`) and false for the longer values, `S LIKE pp` only for 'A'. For 'A'+NUL, `=` is true for 'A' and 'A'+NUL and LIKE only for 'A'+NUL. 'A'+NUL+'B' matches only row 3 and 'A'+NUL+'C' only row 4 under both. 'A'+NUL+'*' matches rows 2, 3 and 4 under LIKE and none under `=`. One QueryDef whose pp is assigned 'A'+NUL+'B', then 'A'+NUL+'C', then 'A', with a newly opened recordset after each assignment, returns each value's own result: no stale reuse of an earlier pattern.
**Conditions.** DAO QueryDef with `PARAMETERS pp Text(255)`, snapshot, ACCDB; a NUL inside the statement text truncates it (3075, `r-fix-expr-sql/nul-pattern-requery-no-argument`).
**Records.** `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/pat-11-16-counted-nul/r-q-expr-sql.pat-11-16-counted-nul.027f707001.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/nul-pattern-requery-no-argument/r-fix-expr-sql.nul-pattern-requery-no-argument.e8629e506b.a1.json`.
**Open.** Reuse through Requery cannot be asked (crashes ACE); the cache identity of a pattern is not observable through DAO.

### R-EXPR-1 EXPR-BIND: parameter and schema changes seen by a fresh open
**Claim.** A fresh OpenRecordset of a saved QueryDef reads the current parameter value and the current SQL, and a recordset opened earlier keeps its own values. QBp (`PARAMETERS pl Long; SELECT pl + 1`): pl = 1 gives 2, pl = 2 and a new open gives 3, the first recordset re-read after MoveFirst still gives 2; after QBp.SQL is replaced by `pl + 10` a new open gives 12 and both held recordsets still read 2 and 3. Saved queries re-resolve at every open: ALTER TABLE ADD COLUMN fails 3211 (cannot lock) while a recordset of the table is open and succeeds after it is closed; then `SELECT *` opens with 3 fields (the new column Null) while `SELECT ID, A` keeps 2; after DROP COLUMN A `SELECT *` opens with 2 fields and `SELECT ID, A` fails 3061 (A becomes an unresolved parameter); after DROP TABLE and a recreate with A as Text both open again.
**Conditions.** DAO, ACCDB, snapshots; no Requery.
**Records.** `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/bind-param-fresh-open/r-q-expr-sql.bind-param-fresh-open.e496cff6f2.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/bind-schema-change/r-q-expr-sql.bind-schema-change.50a1c085f0.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/parameter-lifecycle-no-requery-argument/r-fix-expr-sql.parameter-lifecycle-no-requery-argument.b096e7d778.a1.json`.
**Open.** Cache hit and miss are not observable; Requery (crash); the declared parameter's identity after SQL replacement; the II-04 Row A decoder differential is CalcDb's work.

### R-EXPR-1 EXPR-BIND, EXPR-INVENTORY (Find criteria): grammar, names, functions, literals and acceptance of a DAO Find criteria string

- **Claim.** A Find criteria is an Access expression over the recordset's fields, parsed and bound before any row is tested. Syntax errors are 3077 with a message by shape: "Syntax error (missing operator) in expression." for an empty or blank string, a trailing operand (`N = 40 N`), an operand missing (`N = &H14`: `&H` is not a literal here), a comment (`/* c */`) or a quote that ends a string early (`'O'Brien'`); "Missing ), ], or Item in expression." for `N = (40`; "Syntax error in expression." for a trailing `;`; "Syntax error in string in expression." for an unterminated string (`'beta`, `'''`); "Syntax error in date in expression." for `#1/32/2020#`. A name that is not a field is 3070 "The Microsoft Access database engine does not recognize '<name>' as a valid field name or expression.", including a table-qualified `F.N` and the `c` of `N = 20 -- c` (two minus signs); names are case-insensitive and may be bracketed. A function the standalone service does not know is 3072 "Unknown function name" (`Nz`, `CVErr`); a domain aggregate is 3072 "Function is not available in expressions" (`DMax`); the VBA functions asked work (`UCase`, `Len`, `Chr`, `Val`, `Int`, `CInt`, `CDate`, `DateSerial`, `IsNull`, `Left`). Strings take either quote with the delimiter doubled inside (`"O'Brien"`, `'a"b'`, `'O''Brien'` on an unindexed column); `+` of two strings concatenates. Date literals read with the US convention whatever the locale: `#1/15/2020#`, `#15 Jan 2020#`, `#2020-01-15#`, `#1/15/20#` (2020) and `#15/1/2020#` (day and month swapped when the first part cannot be a month) are one date; `#2/20/2020 1:30:00 PM#` equals `#2/20/2020 13:30#`. Numeric literals `20.0`, `2E1`, `43845` and `43845.0` compare equal to their integer values; a Date field compares with a number as a date serial. The criteria must be Boolean or Null: a bare field (`N`), a numeric or text literal (`0`, `1`, `-1`, `2.5`, `''`, `'1'`, `'True'`) fails 3001 "Invalid argument."; `Null` finds nothing; comparisons, `Like`, `Is [Not] Null`, `In`, `Between`, `Not`/`And`/`Or` and `IsNull(…)` are admitted. A VT_BOOL True argument reads as `True` and finds every row, so DAO turns it into the text `True`, not VariantChangeType's default `-1` (which fails 3001). Text compares under the database sort order: `S = 'ALPHA'` and `S = 'alpha '` find "alpha". `Like` uses the DAO grammar of PAT-01 (`%`, `_`, `#c` literal or digit as PAT-01/PAT-13 say) case-insensitively; a number or date subject matches through its text (`N Like '4*'` finds 40, `D Like '*2020*'` the 2020 dates); `Q Like '*'` finds every non-Null row and `Like ''` none; `S Like Null` finds nothing.
- **Conditions.** DAO dynaset over a local ACCDB table (Long ID and N, Text S, P and Q, Date D), lcid 7177 (en-ZA), raw IDispatch; the 3001 forms on a SELECT dynaset.
- **Records.** `evidence/r-cursor-1b/find-expression-forms/r-cursor-1b.find-expression-forms.fb106aaff6.a1.json`, `find-text-quotes/r-cursor-1b.find-text-quotes.48a175800c.a1.json`, `find-like-patterns/r-cursor-1b.find-like-patterns.933b207186.a1.json`, `find-null-forms/r-cursor-1b.find-null-forms.8ba762ea70.a1.json`, `find-date-forms/r-cursor-1b.find-date-forms.ee7428cf5c.a1.json`; `evidence/r-fix-cursor-dao/find-text-quotes-unindexed/r-fix-cursor-dao.find-text-quotes-unindexed.568398af80.a1.json`, `find-like-patterns-unindexed/r-fix-cursor-dao.find-like-patterns-unindexed.2112df40bc.a1.json`; `evidence/r-def-cursor-dao/err-badcol-findfirst/r-def-cursor-dao.err-badcol-findfirst.ad515ad4c4.a1.json`; `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/findfirst-fresh-recordset/r-fix-expr-sql.findfirst-fresh-recordset.eae4a4c21a.a1.json` (discriminating: `-1`, `0` and `'True'` fail 3001 where a value rule would admit them, `Null` is admitted). Binding is static: the 3001 does not depend on the row's value.
- **Open.** The message of syntax error shapes not asked (an unexpected `)`, a dangling operator at the end); the static type of a function result other than `IsNull` (`Len(S)`, `IIf`) as a whole criteria; arithmetic or a date literal as a whole criteria; whether a criteria error precedes the cursor's refusals; `Not Like` with a Null subject; functions not asked here (`Mid`, `Trim`, `StrComp`, …: in the inventory of the SELECT note, not yet bound by CalcDb).

### R-EXPR-1 EXPR-DOMAIN: lookup order, Null handling and visibility inside one Workspace
**Claim.** DLookup and DFirst return the first matching row in scan (insertion) order and DLast the last, not the key order: rows (id, g, v) inserted (3,1,30), (1,1,10), (2,1,20), (4,2,40), (5,1,50), (6,2,60) give for g = 1 DLookup "30", DFirst "30", DLast "50", DMin "10", DMax "50" and DLookup without criteria "30", identically for Dd (primary key on id), DdI (also an index on g) and DdN (no key): the results are Text. DSum ignores Null: an all-Null group and a group with no rows give Null, (5, Null) gives 5, the whole table 9, `DSum('v * 2', ...)` 8; DCount('v') counts non-Null values (0 for the all-Null group and for no rows); DAvg of an all-Null group is Null; `DSum('1 / 0', ...)` fails 11; an absent criteria field fails 3061 "Too few parameters". Inside one Workspace a domain function sees an uncommitted INSERT (DCount 1), the row disappears after Rollback (0) and stays after CommitTrans; an AddNew edit buffer is invisible until Update (1, then 2).
**Conditions.** DAO, ACCDB, one process; domain calls inside a snapshot SELECT.
**Records.** `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/domain-lookup-order/r-q-expr-sql.domain-lookup-order.45d0c945db.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/domain-dsum-null-empty/r-q-expr-sql.domain-dsum-null-empty.2579b92f64.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/domain-txn-visibility/r-q-expr-sql.domain-txn-visibility.90af78bc65.a1.json`, `evidence/r-def-expr-sql/expr-domain-aggregate-values/r-def-expr-sql.expr-domain-aggregate-values.1f10ca6730.a1.json`, `evidence/r-def-expr-sql/expr-domain-criteria-and-failures/r-def-expr-sql.expr-domain-criteria-and-failures.6cc34da748.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/domain-per-row-no-nz/r-fix-expr-sql.domain-per-row-no-nz.993dcfba68.a1.json`.
**Claim (round 2, other Workspace).** A second Workspace's uncommitted INSERT is invisible to DCount through the first Database (0), visible through its own (1); after Rollback it is 0 everywhere; after CommitTrans the first Database sees it (1); a dynaset AddNew buffer in the second Workspace is invisible to both Databases until Update (1, 1, then 2); the first Workspace's uncommitted INSERT is invisible to the second Database (2) before and after Rollback. No read raised a lock error.
**Claim (round 2, other process).** In a peer process the domain functions read 0 and Null (DCount 0, DLookup Null) at baseline and after every step of the writer: its uncommitted INSERT, Rollback, a committed INSERT (CommitTrans), its AddNew buffer and its Update, although the writer itself reads 2 afterwards. The reads came within milliseconds of each step, so this is the reader's cache or the writer's write-behind (implicit and committed writes land after the call returns, `evidence/r-sample-rec`), not evidence about visibility rules.
**Records (round 2).** `evidence/r-q2-expr-sql/domain-xworkspace/r-q2-expr-sql.domain-xworkspace.993baef642.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q2-expr-sql/domain-xprocess/r-q2-expr-sql.domain-xprocess.ff37899ced.a1.json`; write timing: `evidence/r-sample-rec/insert-implicit/r-sample-rec.insert-implicit.ec0e0d5e13.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sample-rec/insert-explicit/r-sample-rec.insert-explicit.988b9b9b26.a1.json`.
**Open.** Cross-process visibility of committed rows needs a reader that waits out the write-behind window or refreshes its cache (status F); the Access form namespace stays T.

**Claim (round 3, `r-q52-ddl-make-domain` Part D, ACCDB & Jet 4 MDB, DAO & ADO).** All 8 domain functions (`DLookup`, `DCount`, `DSum`, `DMin`, `DMax`, `DAvg`, `DFirst`, `DLast`) run inside SQL `SELECT` across both routes: `DCount` projects `dbLong`/`adInteger` (`0` on empty/unmatched); `DSum`/`DAvg` project `dbDouble`/`adDouble` (`VT_R8` even on Long/Currency inputs, `Null` on empty/all-Null/unmatched); `DLookup`/`DMin`/`DMax`/`DFirst`/`DLast` project `dbText`/`adLongVarWChar` (`VT_BSTR`, `Null` on empty/all-Null/unmatched). `''` criteria succeeds as no criteria; `Null` expr/domain/criteria arg fails `3464`; missing table fails `3078`/`0x80040E37`; missing field fails `3061`/`0x80040E10`; bad criteria syntax & wrong arity fail `3075`/`0x80040E14`; `DSum`/`DAvg` on Text fails `3464`; `DLookup` on DateTime formats with the active thread locale (`en-ZA` `2026/01/10`).
**Records (round 3).** `evidence/r-q52-ddl-make-domain/domain-{all-scalar-dao,criteria-perrow-dao,null-empty-errors-dao,ado}-{accdb,mdb}/`.


### R-SQL-2 ACT-SEL-03, ACT-SEL-04, SQL-ACT-FAIL: a peer changes rows and schema while an UPDATE is running
**Claim.** A statement frozen inside ACE does not block a peer's row changes: while participant a's `UPDATE Big SET V = V + 1, Pad = String(100, 'q')` over 65,536 rows was suspended (40 ms and 400 ms after its start; a had already updated ID 0 in the second case, not in the first), b's `UPDATE Big SET V = -1 WHERE ID = 60000`, `INSERT` of ID 100000 (V 7) and `DELETE` of ID 65000 each succeeded (RecordsAffected 1) with no lock error. After resume a's statement used the peer's value (ID 60000 ends V = 0, i.e. -1 + 1: the assignment source is read when the row is reached), stopped at the row the peer had deleted with 3197 ("stopped the process because you and another user are attempting to change the same data at the same time") and reported RecordsAffected 0 although about 65,000 rows were changed (64,999 to V = 1 and ID 60000 to 0; Sum(V) 65006 includes the inserted row's 7): IDs 65001..65535 stayed at V = 0, the inserted row was not updated, and earlier changes were not reversed. The two delays gave the same final state. A peer's schema change is refused while the statement runs: CREATE INDEX, ALTER TABLE ADD COLUMN, DROP INDEX and DROP TABLE each failed 3262 "Could not lock table 'Big'; currently in use by user 'admin'", and the frozen UPDATE then completed normally (RecordsAffected 65536).
**Conditions.** Two DAO processes on one shared ACCDB, writer suspended by the peer (SuspendParticipant) and resumed; dbFailOnError on every statement. A peer stands in for the callback of the book's ACT-SEL-03/04; it does not change a predicate field.
**Records.** `evidence/r-q2-expr-sql/act-frozen-update-dml-early/r-q2-expr-sql.act-frozen-update-dml-early.68f8aeb358.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q2-expr-sql/act-frozen-update-dml-late/r-q2-expr-sql.act-frozen-update-dml-late.955c863160.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q2-expr-sql/act-frozen-update-ddl/r-q2-expr-sql.act-frozen-update-ddl.7a16d67779.a1.json`.
**Open.** A peer changing the predicate field or key of an unseen row, and an effectful callback (T: needs a host route); whether the candidate list is fixed before the loop (the 3197 on a deleted row is consistent with it) is not proved.

### R-UDF-1 UDF-CONTEXT and R-EXPR-1 EXPR-SANDBOX: what the Access host admits that standalone DAO does not, and what its expression service refuses
**Claim.** In an owned Access host, `Application.Eval` and CurrentDb SQL resolve Nz, the domain functions and public functions of a standard module; standalone DAO on the same file refuses all of them with 3085 (Undefined function). Under the machine's existing sandbox mode, Environ and CurDir are refused by name (Eval 0x800A0979, SQL 3085), yet both run when generated VBA calls them through Run, Eval or SQL, so the refusal is the expression service's. `CDec` is refused in Eval (0x800A0987, wrong number of arguments). The Eval literal `1.5` reaches VBA as Decimal (VarType 14), while a Double column value reaches it as Double. A Variant result of Nz or a UDF in CurrentDb SQL projects as text. A VBA Boolean returns as VT_BOOL through Run and as VT_I2 -1 through Eval and SQL. A UDF in WHERE or with a row-dependent argument runs once per row in key order; with a constant argument it runs once per query. A projected UDF runs when its field value is read.
**Conditions.** Access.Application 16.0 Build 20527 as a claimed local server, main desktop by owner instruction, AutomationSecurity low on the instance, a new database made by NewCurrentDatabase, the generated module `CalcDbGen` loaded by LoadFromText; standalone DAO opened after Access quit.
**Records.** `evidence/r-access-host/eval-context/`, `generated-module/`, `sandbox-control/` (decides that the sandbox, not absence, refuses Environ and CurDir), `udf-error-query/` (the field-read timing).
**Open.** ADO lanes and a host control reference (UDF-CONTEXT); other sandbox modes need another provisioned machine; DLookup and DSum inside CurrentDb SQL against EXPR-DOMAIN's standalone results.

### R-UDF-1 UDF-NAME, UDF-SIGNATURE: name resolution and argument mapping of host functions
**Claim.** Through `Application.Run`, an omitted Optional Variant is missing (TypeName Error) and an omitted typed Optional takes its default; Null and Empty arrive as themselves; text converts into a Double parameter when it can and otherwise fails with DISP_E_TYPEMISMATCH (0x80020005); a missing procedure fails with 2517. Through `Application.Eval` the same calls give the same values, a failed conversion is 0x800A000D, and a missing name is 0x800A0979. The module-qualified name `CalcDbGen.CalcDbEcho(5)` is not resolved (2517), and its description repeats the previous failure's text, so EXCEPINFO text after a 2517 can be stale.
**Conditions.** As for UDF-CONTEXT.
**Records.** `evidence/r-access-host/generated-module/`.
**Open.** Ambiguous names, a name shadowing a field or builtin, provider remove and reload under the same name; named arguments through Run.

### R-UDF-1 UDF-ERROR: a VBA error in a host function goes to a modal dialog; once it is ended, the caller gets 40040 with no description, and a query field gets 3072
**Claim.** An unhandled error raised in a fixture function, through Run, Eval or a CurrentDb query, opens the modal run-time error dialog (`#32770` "Microsoft Visual Basic": "Run-time error '<number> (<hex>)': <description>", buttons Continue disabled, End, Debug, Help) and blocks the caller; the query reaches it at the failing row's field read. A compile error (syntax, or an undeclared variable under Option Explicit) opens "Microsoft Visual Basic for Applications" ("Compile error: <reason>", OK and Help); after OK the VBA editor enters break mode and the call stays blocked until the project is reset. Once the dialog is ended (End, or OK then Reset), Run and Eval fail with 0x80020009, EXCEPINFO scode 0x800A9C68 (40040) and no source or description: the raised number and text reach the caller only through the dialog. A query's failing field reads 3072 (source DAO.Field, empty description, one DBEngine.Errors entry), the scan goes on, and later rows read normally. The module and the host stay usable; a second call to a function that does not compile shows the compile dialog again. MsgBox in generated code shows its own dialog, and its answer (Cancel) is the value Run returns (2).
**Conditions.** Visible Access host, main desktop by owner instruction; VBA error trapping as configured; dialogs described by UI Automation and answered by the question's dialog policy (InvokePattern, no input), with no owner input during the attempt.
**Records.** `evidence/r-access-dialog/repro-*` (the dialogs, unanswered); `runtime-run-end/`, `runtime-eval-end/`, `runtime-query-end/`, `syntax-eval-ok/`, `undeclared-run-ok/`, `msgbox-run-cancel/`; `evidence/r-access-host/udf-error*` (blocked until the deadline without a policy).
**Open.** Nested errors and errors during cleanup; Debug and Continue are not asked; the private desktop.

### R-SQL-1 SQL-DEMAND, R-PAR-1 PAR-ERROR: the ADO lane and its recordset events
**Claim.** On ADO (ACE OLE DB provider) a static read-only recordset reports its full population at Open for every query shape (RecordCount 256 for a plain scan and ORDER BY, 7 for GROUP BY and DISTINCT, 10 for TOP 10, 3 for the crosstab), and its recordset events do not tell the shapes apart: Open raises WillMove and MoveComplete (reason Move, adStatus cant-deny then OK), MoveLast, MoveFirst and each MoveNext raise WillMove/MoveComplete with their reasons (15, 12, 13), Close raises RecordsetChangeComplete (reason Close); no FetchProgress or FetchComplete arrives and every event is delivered on the caller's thread. A forward-only recordset raises, for each MoveNext, WillMove, a WillChangeRecordset/RecordsetChangeComplete pair (two pairs on the first move) and MoveComplete, and EndOfRecordset at the end. A projected failing expression (`1 / z` at id 3) does not fail on ADO: the field reads Empty with no error and no event, static or forward-only. A failing WHERE term fails the MoveNext onto the bad row with 0x80004005 "Division by zero" and MoveComplete adStatus 2, and the row is skipped (ids 1, 2, 4, 5); `Sum(1 / z)` fails at Open (0x80040E14), after which the recordset is closed.
**Conditions.** ADODB.Recordset (static 3 and forward-only 0, read-only, adCmdText), sink on RecordsetEvents connected before Open; event names are the documented DISPID and reason tables (13 WillChangeRecordset, 14 RecordsetChangeComplete, 15 WillMove, 16 MoveComplete, 17 EndOfRecordset; reasons 9 Close, 10 Move, 12 MoveFirst, 13 MoveNext, 15 MoveLast), not read from the captured type library.
**Records.** `evidence/r-q2-expr-sql/demand-ado-events-shapes/r-q2-expr-sql.demand-ado-events-shapes.28618e250b.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q2-expr-sql/demand-ado-events-failing-row/r-q2-expr-sql.demand-ado-events-failing-row.bd544953d9.a1.json`.
**Open.** Asynchronous fetch (adAsyncFetch) and FetchProgress; native page and TEMP I/O and a host call counter stay T.

## R-ADO-1, R-ENC-1, R-LINK-1, R-COMPLEX-1, R-REBUILD-1, R-OLEDB-1 and R-ODBC-1

### R-ADO-1 ADO-CONN: provider resolution, construction orders, open and post-open rules
- **Claim**: A new ADO Connection reports Provider "MSDASQL"; ACE is used only if `Provider` (property or connection-string key) names `Microsoft.ACE.OLEDB.12.0` before Open, and a Data Source is required (none gives 0x80040E4D "Authentication failed"). A bare path without Provider is read as an ODBC DSN (MSDASQL, "Data source name too long"). Reading `Properties` on a fresh Connection pins MSDASQL (a later Provider assignment gives 0x800A0C94); assigning Provider after nothing, State, ConnectionString or Mode does not. Accepted orders: Provider then Open(path), ConnectionString then Open, Data Source via `Properties("Data Source")`, a duplicated Provider key, whitespace around `Mode = Read`, quoted values (the Provider getter keeps the quotes until Open). An unknown key (`CalcDbUnknownOption`) gives 0x80004005 "Could not find installable ISAM". After Open, ConnectionString is expanded (`User ID=Admin;Data Source=...;Jet OLEDB:Engine Type=6;...`); while open, ConnectionString, Provider and Mode assignments give 0x800A0E79, CursorLocation/CommandTimeout/IsolationLevel/Attributes are accepted, and Data Source or Mode as Properties give 0x80040E21. After Close ConnectionString reads "" and assigned values persist, and Open with no new settings gives 0x80040E4D. UID "Admin"/"" opens; another user or any password gives 0x80040E4D (SQLState 3028).
- **Conditions**: ACCDB and MDB fixtures, read and read/write modes, relative, upper-case, forward-slash and `.\` Data Source spellings open; `\\?\inputs\...` (relative behind the prefix) gives 3043.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-str-ado-act/ado-open-alt-orders/r-str-ado-act.ado-open-alt-orders.0648fd8b02.a1.json`, `evidence/r-str-ado-act/ado-connstring-keys/r-str-ado-act.ado-connstring-keys.b6c2e4bd38.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/conn-cs-bare-path/r-ado-1b.conn-cs-bare-path.683780018e.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/conn-datasource-spellings/r-ado-1b.conn-datasource-spellings.5a2c81638f.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/conn-open-args/r-ado-1b.conn-open-args.f9c16cb1f2.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/conn-set-while-open/r-ado-1b.conn-set-while-open.5efbd0c7f5.a1.json`. Discriminators: `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/ado-provider-pin-isolated/r-fix-wire-ado.ado-provider-pin-isolated.aa5101744f.a1.json` (the Properties read, not Provider/State/ConnectionString/Mode, pins MSDASQL; this removes the order confound of `ado-open-provider-property`), `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/ado-open-provider-property-ace-first/r-fix-wire-ado.ado-open-provider-property-ace-first.220390f412.a1.json` (ACE inventoried: 31 properties closed, 101 open, 31 after Close).
- **Open**: `File Name=` (UDL) and an absolute `\\?\C:\...` spelling (the builder cannot concatenate or absolutize a path); empty-valued and aliased keys; ADO-CONN on a `.NET Framework 4.8` caller.

### R-ADO-1 ADO-PROVIDER: effective provider routes (ACE, default, MSPersist)
- **Claim**: Three routes were asked and each is stable: ACE (explicit, or selected through the Provider property) opens the file; an omitted or default provider is MSDASQL and reaches the ODBC Driver Manager, which fails (IM002 "Data source name not found", or IM010 for a bare path); `Provider="MSPersist"` reads back "MSPersist.1" and serves persisted files (`Recordset.Open(file, conn)` and `Connection.Execute(file)` return the 4 saved rows). Each failed non-ACE open leaves State 0 and one Errors entry (Number -2147467259, SQLState IM002).
- **Conditions**: ADO only; no DSN or driver exists on the machine, so every MSDASQL attempt fails at the Driver Manager.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-str-ado-act/ado-provider-non-ace/r-str-ado-act.ado-provider-non-ace.9a0ec48d46.a1.json`, `evidence/r-str-ado-act/ado-persist/r-str-ado-act.ado-persist.4ad2e7d2b5.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/conn-cs-bare-path/r-ado-1b.conn-cs-bare-path.683780018e.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/ado-provider-pin-isolated/r-fix-wire-ado.ado-provider-pin-isolated.aa5101744f.a1.json`.
- **Open**: named non-ACE providers (Jet 4, unknown, empty names) and conflicting duplicate Provider keys are not asked; new probes may only open ACE connections (prompt rule), and `ado-provider-missing-names` stays off pending the owner (ACT-MISSING). One earlier ODBC attempt without a Dbq returned "Operation was canceled" and the record does not say whether a prompt appeared (`evidence/r-oledb-odbc/odbc-path-free-connections/r-oledb-odbc.odbc-path-free-connections.5e580a45cb.a1.json`).

### R-ADO-1 ADO-SESSION: sessions follow the Connection object, not the string
- **Claim**: Recordsets and Commands opened on one Connection return that same Connection object from `ActiveConnection`; `Connection.Close` leaves them at State 0 and `Command.ActiveConnection` returns a null interface pointer; a second Connection to the same file stays open and records its own errors (SQLState 3000 from its own bad statement). Rollback scope is the Connection: ADO changes made inside a transaction revert for Connection.Execute, Command.Execute, keyset Update and client UpdateBatch, and a released (not closed) transaction connection leaves a second ADO connection and DAO reading the old values.
- **Conditions**: ACCDB, writable ACE connection; same-process connections.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/ado-session-writable/r-fix-wire-ado.ado-session-writable.99362e12b0.a1.json`, `evidence/r-str-ado-act/ado-session/r-str-ado-act.ado-session.f1e2e94cae.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/disconnect-close-connection/r-ado-1b.disconnect-close-connection.ea2b9594c3.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/txn-rollback-scope-objects/r-ado-1b.txn-rollback-scope-objects.64de5c2b49.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/txn-release-without-close/r-ado-1b.txn-release-without-close.efe977c816.a1.json`.
- **Open**: an implicit Connection created from a connection string (needs a concatenated string, T); equal strings on two Connection objects against each other's errors and transactions.
- **Built (`w14-ado-session-book-meta`, `5ff0eb36`, 2026-11-27)**: `CommandObject.OnConnectionClosed` releases `_activeConnection` -> null interface pointer (`r-str-ado-act/ado-session`, `r-fix-wire-ado/ado-session-writable`).


### R-ADO-1 ADO-ASYNC (part): async flags on this provider; blocked statements; async fetch
- **Claim**: With the ACE provider ADO returns before completion for `adAsyncExecute` and `adAsyncConnect`. `Connection.Execute` of a seven-way cross join (10^7 rows) with `adAsyncExecute` returns a Recordset at once with Connection State 5 and Recordset State 4; both stay pending at 100 ms in every run (three runs over two cards), are still pending at 1000 ms in two of three and are open (State 1, value 10000000) by 3000 ms in all. An UPDATE with `adAsyncExecute|adExecuteNoRecords` returns a null recordset with State 5 and State 1 at 200 ms; `Recordset.Open("K", adCmdTable|adAsyncExecute)` returns State 4 and State 1 at 200 ms; `adCmdTableDirect|adAsyncExecute` is refused 0x800A0BB9. `Connection.Open(..., adAsyncConnect)` returns State 2 and State 1 at 50 ms, repeated on two connections; for an absent file State 2, then 0x80004005 (3024) on the State read at 50 ms, State 0 with one Errors entry at 500 ms. A statement blocked by another process's open transaction (row 1 locked): the async UPDATE returns with State 5, still 5 at 500 ms, the State read at 2000 ms fails 0x80004005 "Could not update; currently locked" (3218, native -72156238) and State is 1 at 5000 ms: the pending state tracks the lock wait and the failure surfaces on the next call. A second blocked async UPDATE cancelled at once: `Cancel` returns, State is 1, no error (the row was not read afterwards). Client static recordsets over a 10^6-row source: with `adAsyncFetch` State is 9 (open and fetching) right after Open with RecordCount already 1000000 and State 1 by 200 ms; with `adAsyncFetchNonBlocking` State is 9 with RecordCount growing (5840 at return, 62705 at 200 ms, 340175 at 1200 ms) and State 1 with 1000000 at 3200 ms. Cancel of a running query (earlier record) returned 0 and did not abort it.
- **Conditions**: ADO over ACE, ACCDB; STA worker; the timing results were observed more than once (execute: three runs, connect: two connections, blocked UPDATE: one). A sink on the Connection saw WillExecute (Options 17) before Execute returned and Disconnect at Close but no ExecuteComplete.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q2-ado-misc/ado-events-async-execute/r-q2-ado-misc.ado-events-async-execute.e1d912d59c.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q2-ado-misc/ado-async-fetch-rows/r-q2-ado-misc.ado-async-fetch-rows.0703f487b8.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q2-ado-misc/async-lock-wait-cancel/r-q2-ado-misc.async-lock-wait-cancel.fbc13f5702.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/async-execute-polled/r-q-ado-misc.async-execute-polled.98bd8802aa.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/async-open-connect-polled/r-q-ado-misc.async-open-connect-polled.a2ea66ba74.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/async-flag-matrix/r-q-ado-misc.async-flag-matrix.6df9172641.a1.json`, `evidence/r-str-ado-act/ado-async/r-str-ado-act.ado-async.2bd4dbabf6.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/conn-open-async-connect/r-ado-1b.conn-open-async-connect.dfc04d7265.a1.json`. Competing explanation not separated: an ACE async path versus ADO's own worker thread (ACE and its OLE DB provider expose no event).
- **Decision flag**: by the card text a proven early return is an initial-scope/architecture conflict with D-CALL-1, for the owner; this note records the observation only.
- **Still T (one line)**: delivery of the async completion events (ExecuteComplete, FetchProgress, FetchComplete): none arrived on the non-pumping STA worker, so raised-versus-undelivered is undecided; needs a sink that runs while the call is pending.

### R-ADO-1 ADO-EVENT (part): ConnectionEvents and RecordsetEvents as delivered to recording sinks
- **Claim**: Events arrive synchronously on the calling thread, inside the call that causes them. ConnectionEvents: `Open` raises WillConnect (ConnectionString "", UserID "", Password "", Options -1, adStatus 1) then ConnectComplete (error null, adStatus 1) before it returns; `Execute` raises WillExecute (Source text, CursorType -1, LockType -1, Options, adStatus 1) then ExecuteComplete (RecordsAffected 1 for a one-row UPDATE, error null, adStatus 1); a failing SELECT of a missing table raises WillExecute and then ExecuteComplete with RecordsAffected 0, a non-null error object, adStatus 2 and a non-null Recordset, and the call fails 0x80040E37; BeginTrans raises BeginTransComplete (TransactionLevel 1), CommitTrans CommitTransComplete, RollbackTrans RollbackTransComplete; `Close` raises Disconnect. Two sinks advised on one connection point (cookies 1 and 2) both receive every event, ev1 then ev2 (registration order); after ev1 is unadvised ev2 still receives. Object identity in the events: for `Command.Execute` WillExecute and ExecuteComplete carry the caller's Command (same identity) and ExecuteComplete the returned Recordset; for `Connection.Execute` and `Recordset.Open` WillExecute carries a null Command (for Recordset.Open the caller's Recordset, with CursorType 1, LockType 1, Options 1) and ExecuteComplete a new synthetic Command object. RecordsetEvents: Open raises WillMove/MoveComplete (reason Move, adStatus cantDeny 3); MoveNext, MovePrevious, MoveLast, MoveFirst and Move raise WillMove/MoveComplete with reasons 13, 14, 15, 12 and 10; a Move past the end adds EndOfRecordset (more data False); a MoveNext at EOF still raises WillMove, EndOfRecordset and MoveComplete although the call fails 0x800A0BCD; Requery raises WillMove and MoveComplete (reason 7) then RecordsetChangeComplete (reason Close 9) and a new move to the first row; Close raises RecordsetChangeComplete (reason 9) (in one record a move pair came first). No FetchProgress or FetchComplete arrived. Edits (server keyset optimistic): the first field assignment raises WillChangeRecord (reason FirstChange 11, 1 record), WillChangeField and FieldChangeComplete (1 field) and RecordChangeComplete (11); further assignments only the field pair; `Update` raises WillChangeRecord/RecordChangeComplete (reason 3); `CancelUpdate` reason 4; a MoveNext with a pending edit raises WillMove, then the Update pair (reason 3), then MoveComplete (13); AddNew raises WillMove, the pair with reason 1 and MoveComplete (10); `Delete` raised two RecordChangeComplete (reason 2) and no WillChangeRecord. Client batch (3 staged edits, third conflicting): `UpdateBatch` raises three WillChangeRecord (reason 3) then three RecordChangeComplete, the third with a non-null error and adStatus 2 and the call fails 0x80040E38; `Resync(adAffectAll, ...)` raises four pairs with reason 8 (one per row); the next UpdateBatch one pair. A Clone has its own connection point: moves on the clone raise events only on the clone's sink, not on the original's; Close of the clone raises none; Close of the original raises RecordsetChangeComplete (9).
- **Conditions**: recording sinks on an STA worker; `r-ops/ado-connection-events` (a smoke record) shows the same ConnectionEvents.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q2-ado-misc/ado-events-connection-lifecycle/r-q2-ado-misc.ado-events-connection-lifecycle.aae7f613fb.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q2-ado-misc/ado-events-command-identity/r-q2-ado-misc.ado-events-command-identity.35a2e57613.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q2-ado-misc/ado-events-recordset-moves/r-q2-ado-misc.ado-events-recordset-moves.20f6a17c9a.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q2-ado-misc/ado-events-recordset-edit/r-q2-ado-misc.ado-events-recordset-edit.25df46ebc8.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q2-ado-misc/ado-events-recordset-batch/r-q2-ado-misc.ado-events-recordset-batch.4ac27bdfaf.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q2-ado-misc/ado-events-clone-propagation/r-q2-ado-misc.ado-events-clone-propagation.a420418293.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ops/ado-connection-events/r-ops.ado-connection-events.91b58e84fe.a1.json`.
- **Open (T)**: cancelling or suppressing denyable events, changing Advise membership, throwing or re-entering inside a callback (a recording sink cannot act: ADO-REENTRY and ADO-PARAM-EVENT stay T), and the async completion events (see ADO-ASYNC).
### R-ADO-1 ADO-PROPS: dynamic Property inventory by connection phase
- **Claim**: With Provider set to ACE the Connection's Properties collection holds 31 entries before Open (data-source initialisation group: Cache Authentication, Password, User ID, Data Source, Mode, Locale Identifier, OLE DB Services, the `Jet OLEDB:` options, ...), 101 after Open (the 70 provider-information entries such as Active Sessions, DBMS Name, Provider Name, Isolation Levels, Transaction DDL, `Autocommit Isolation Levels` and the runtime `Jet OLEDB:` timing options are added), and 31 again after Close, in the same order. Between closed and open only `Jet OLEDB:Engine Type` changes value (0 to 6 for ACCDB); after Close the Data Source entry reads "" (and the other 30 are unchanged). After a failed Open (absent file) the collection has 31 entries, State 0, ConnectionString "", Data Source "", Engine Type 0, Mode 16, one Errors entry (3024). Lookup by name (`Item("Jet OLEDB:Engine Type")`: Value 6, Type 3, Attributes 1537) and by ordinal (`Item(5)`: "Column Definition", 1) works on an open connection; `Properties.Refresh` leaves retained wrappers and their Values readable. A Recordset and a Command each expose 88 properties (Command: before any Execute). A wrapper retained across Open or Close fails 0x800A0D5C (draft record `ado-open-provider-property-ace-first`): wrappers are not reattached by name.
- **Conditions**: ADO Connection over ACE, ACCDB, Provider assigned before any Properties read (a Properties read on a fresh Connection pins MSDASQL, see ADO-CONN).
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/props-conn-inventory/r-q-ado-misc.props-conn-inventory.ecdc06bcba.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/props-failed-open/r-q-ado-misc.props-failed-open.df6a44df10.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/props-lookup-refresh/r-q-ado-misc.props-lookup-refresh.0b8d3b783c.a1.json`; discriminator for the retained-wrapper rule: `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/ado-open-provider-property-ace-first/r-fix-wire-ado.ado-open-provider-property-ace-first.220390f412.a1.json`; context `evidence/r-oledb-odbc/prop-connection-inventory/r-oledb-odbc.prop-connection-inventory.56c9930ca5.a1.json`.
- **Open**: Type/Attributes of every entry was captured in the record but not tabulated here; property inventories of Recordset/Command after Execute.

### R-ADO-1 ADO-PARAM: CreateParameter, derived metadata, value coercion and positional binding
- **Claim**: Binding is by position in every form asked. A saved query `QPair(pA, pB)` as adCmdStoredProc exposes no derived parameters (Count 0, `Item(0)` 0x800A0CC1); appending `pA`=2 then `pB`=4 returns rows 2..4; appending `pB`=4 first and `pA`=2 second returns no rows (names do not matter, position does); parameters named `x` and `y` in declared order return rows 2..4; one parameter only fails 0x80040E10 "Too few parameters. Expected 2." (SQLState 3061); a third surplus parameter is accepted and ignored. A text command with two `?` markers bound `hi`=4 then `lo`=2 returns no rows with NamedParameters False and with True (names cannot select). Parameter creation: `Append` of an untyped parameter, or of Type 202 with Size 0 or omitted, fails 0x800A0E7C; Size -1 and Size 100 (Type 203) append; Type 9999 and Direction 99 fail 0x800A0BB9. A `?` text derives Name "?", Type 202, Size 510, Precision 255 (Count 2 on first access, 0 after Refresh in one record); changing CommandText re-derives. For a Long parameter in `L = ?` the tags I2, UI1, R8, R4, CY, DATE, "20", "2e1" and " 20 " select ids 2 and 4; R8 20.5 reads as 20; BOOL -1 and Null select nothing; "0x14", "" and "abc" and a byte array fail 0x800A0D5D and keep the old value; out-of-range numerics fail 0x8002000A at the put; Name changes after Append fail 0x800A0C93; Output/InputOutput/ReturnValue on a `?` command fail 0x80040E21; a Command with no connection fails 0x800A0E7D. Decimal parameters: String and Double 3.125 read back DECIMAL scale 3, Currency scale 4, Long scale 0.
- **Conditions**: ADO Command, ACCDB, ACE provider; values bound at Append time.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/param-saved-declared-order/r-q-ado-misc.param-saved-declared-order.23811df417.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/param-saved-names-swapped/r-q-ado-misc.param-saved-names-swapped.575ec38ae1.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/param-saved-wrong-names/r-q-ado-misc.param-saved-wrong-names.7eaad60c99.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/param-markers-names/r-q-ado-misc.param-markers-names.5f7d196265.a1.json`; earlier `evidence/r-ado-1b/param-create-forms/r-ado-1b.param-create-forms.0492011378.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/param-refresh-after-text-change/r-ado-1b.param-refresh-after-text-change.f4ed5d8f03.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/param-value-tag-matrix/r-ado-1b.param-value-tag-matrix.1ba10384f9.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/param-mutate-after-append/r-ado-1b.param-mutate-after-append.a7826b61ff.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/ado-params-saved-query/r-fix-wire-ado.ado-params-saved-query.597c15efa9.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/param-decimal-scale-fresh/r-fix-wire-ado.param-decimal-scale-fresh.d0d5c4e4ec.a1.json`, `evidence/r-oledb-odbc/param-edge/r-oledb-odbc.param-edge.e84aa08f05.a1.json`. Discriminator: the swapped-order run, whose values (4 then 2) make position give an empty set and name give 2..4. The earlier "appending pA before pB fails Unspecified error" in `evidence/r-oledb-odbc/param-order-binding/r-oledb-odbc.param-order-binding.92ef1f32bf.a1.json` is explained by position: the first appended parameter there was a Text where the query's first parameter is Long.
- **Open**: arrays with gaps or non-zero bounds and Output directions through a real output parameter (builder has no array argument, T); ADO-PARAM-EVENT (T).
- **Built (`w14-ado-params-errinfo`, `d8d78c87`, 2026-11-27)**: `CalcDb.Com.Core` (`NullStringsErrorInfo` unmanaged `IErrorInfo` with `GetGUID` `S_OK` and `GetSource`/`GetDescription`/`GetHelpFile`/`GetHelpContext` `0x80004005` `E_FAIL`, plus `Outcome.ToExcepInfo` dedup) and `CalcDb.Com.Ado` (`Parameters` 3-state `?` derivation/`Refresh()` machine, `Parameter` empty `Name` null BSTR + mutators + `DISP_E_OVERFLOW` `0x8002000A` Provider, prepared saved-query `3748`, `Command.Execute` `Options=12345` -> `0x800A0BB9`, integer-string BSTR `WHERE` coercion, and `GetRows(-1)` BOF/EOF -> `0x800A0BCD`); `r-ado-1b` `31/62` (`+9`), `r-fix-wire-ado` `19/51` (`+5`).

### R-ADO-1 ADO-RESULTS: row, empty, closed and refused result forms, RecordsAffected, timeouts, named arguments
- **Claim**: A row SELECT through Connection.Execute returns an open forward-only recordset (State 1, CursorType 0, LockType 1, RecordCount -1); an empty result is open with BOF and EOF true and the full field list (also `QEmpty`); an action statement returns a recordset with State 0 (closed); `adExecuteNoRecords` (129) returns a null interface pointer. Two statements in one text are refused 0x80040E14 "Characters found after end of SQL statement" (SQLState 3000) and the tail is not executed. `Recordset.NextRecordset` fails 0x800A0CB3 "Current provider does not support returning multiple recordsets from a single execution", open or closed. `Recordset.Requery()` with no argument works. `RecordsAffected` passed by reference (VT_BYREF|VT_VARIANT holding Empty and typed VT_BYREF|VT_I4 holding -5) is written back: UPDATE of three rows 3, of none 0, INSERT 1, DELETE of two rows 2, SELECT 0, CREATE TABLE 0, DROP TABLE 0, a `Command.Execute` UPDATE of two rows 2; a failing statement (table exists, table missing, unknown column) leaves the by-reference value untouched (-5 or Empty). `Execute` slots: a value in the RecordsAffected slot or a String Options is accepted, Options 12345 gives 0x800A0BB9; `Open "T"` with adCmdText fails 0x80040E14, `"EXEC QAll"` works and `{call QAll}` fails; a Command with an explicit ActiveConnection refuses `Recordset.Open` with 0x800A0E7B. Named arguments bind to ADO's documented dispids: `Execute` CommandText 0 and Options 2 (given in reverse order they work), `Recordset.Open` Source 0, ActiveConnection 1, CursorType 2, LockType 3, `CreateParameter` Name 0, Type 1, Direction 2, Size 3, Value 4 (Size before Name works); an unknown name fails GetIDsOfNames 0x80020006 (DISP_E_UNKNOWNNAME) and a name repeating a positional argument 0x80020004. `Connection.CommandTimeout` defaults to 30 and accepts 0, 1, 99999, an I2 and the string "5", and refuses -1 (0x800A0BB9); a Command takes 30 whatever the Connection had and the two do not follow each other. **CommandTimeout does not decide a lock wait**: with another process holding row 1, an ADO UPDATE waited 2.02 to 2.04 s and failed 0x80004005 "Could not update; currently locked" (3218) with Connection.CommandTimeout 1, 4 and 0 and with a Command timeout of 2 (the 6 s release of the holder came later); the seven-way join under timeout 1 returned its full value (earlier record).
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q2-ado-misc/results-timeout-lock-wait/r-q2-ado-misc.results-timeout-lock-wait.2e260b57a7.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q2-ado-misc/ado-recordsaffected-byref/r-q2-ado-misc.ado-recordsaffected-byref.8b9bdd9784.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q2-ado-misc/ado-named-arguments/r-q2-ado-misc.ado-named-arguments.9e5ebb1356.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/results-nextrecordset-requery/r-q-ado-misc.results-nextrecordset-requery.d383798dae.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/results-timeout-values/r-q-ado-misc.results-timeout-values.5c3656f4e3.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/ado-execute-results-rows/r-fix-wire-ado.ado-execute-results-rows.00bec5d592.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/ado-execute-update-compound/r-fix-wire-ado.ado-execute-update-compound.b25c1ab63f.a1.json`, `evidence/r-str-ado-act/ado-execute-action/r-str-ado-act.ado-execute-action.f337c92ed7.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/cmd-execute-args/r-ado-1b.cmd-execute-args.77951ef251.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/rs-source-kinds/r-ado-1b.rs-source-kinds.8238995ad3.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ops/byref-ado-execute-records-affected/r-ops.byref-ado-execute-records-affected.fdfa8ae1e0.a1.json`.
- **Open**: CommandTimeout against a delayed phase other than a lock wait (needs a source that is slow without being locked, with elapsed times).
- **Built (`w22-com-ado-dao-core`, `68cda61d`, 2026-11-28)**: `AdoObject` dual dispatch repeated/positional+named arguments return `DISP_E_PARAMNOTFOUND` (`0x80020004`) without `EXCEPINFO`/`IErrorInfo` (`r-q2-ado-misc/ado-named-arguments`). **(`w23-cat-sql-idx-ddl`, `a33d5b53`, 2026-11-28):** `ActionBinder.BindUpdate` emits `3061` (`"Too few parameters. Expected 1."`) for single-assignment unqualified unknown `SET` columns, mapped via `AdoSqlErrors.TryMap` to `0x80040E10` `"No value given for one or more required parameters."` (`r-q2-ado-misc/ado-recordsaffected-byref` #110/#117).

### R-ADO-1 ADO-CURSOR: requested versus effective tuple, inheritance, visibility, CacheSize
- **Claim**: On a writable ACE connection the requested (location, type, lock) becomes: server forward-only read-only 0,1,2 (RecordCount -1, `MoveLast` 0x80040E24); server keyset optimistic 1,3,2 (RecordCount 4); server dynamic pessimistic is delivered as keyset (1,2,2); server static read-only 3,1,2; client static batch-optimistic 3,4,3; client forward-only 3,1,3; `adCmdTable` and `adCmdTableDirect` keyset 1,1,2. Client lock 2 reads back as 4; a server static request with an updatable lock reads back as keyset; on a read-mode connection updatable locks are delivered as LockType 1 and Update/AddNew/Delete fail 0x800A0CB3. A Recordset inherits the Connection's CursorLocation at Open (3 turns forward-only and keyset requests into static, RecordCount 7). Paging: PageSize 10 by default, AbsolutePage 99 gives position -3 with EOF, 0/-1 and PageSize 0 fail 0x800A0BB9, MaxRecords is ignored. Changes through the same Connection: a keyset shows an update at once, including rows inside its cache window (CacheSize 4 and 1 give the same reads, the unvisited rows 2, 3, 8 and 9 all read their new values) and a deleted row errors 0x80040E23; inserts are not visible; static recordsets show nothing; `Requery` shows all. Changes by another connection (update ID, insert, delete ID, in two rounds): a server keyset and a dynamic recordset (RecordCount 10 and -1) show the old values at once, show the update after 8 s and read the deleted row as an error (hr 0x80020009 on its fields), and do not show the insert; a server static and a client static recordset show nothing until `Requery`, which after 8 s shows update, delete and insert in all four. Both rounds gave the same result at both time points.
- **Conditions**: ACCDB; the 0 s and 8 s observations repeat in two rounds; the exact lag between 0 and 8 s is not bracketed (the earlier DAO records put it near 7 s).
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/cursor-external-visibility/r-q-ado-misc.cursor-external-visibility.2f25fa0c3d.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/cursor-cachesize-window/r-q-ado-misc.cursor-cachesize-window.50e555f741.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/ado-rs-cursors-writable/r-fix-wire-ado.ado-rs-cursors-writable.f73a063b5c.a1.json`, `evidence/r-str-ado-act/ado-rs-cursors/r-str-ado-act.ado-rs-cursors.002e5fa5c5.a1.json`, `evidence/r-oledb-odbc/prop-rowset-requests/r-oledb-odbc.prop-rowset-requests.2862dd72da.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/rs-lock-matrix/r-ado-1b.rs-lock-matrix.66f89c7e78.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/conn-cursorlocation-inherit/r-ado-1b.conn-cursorlocation-inherit.30d21b33cb.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/rs-readonly-mode-edit/r-ado-1b.rs-readonly-mode-edit.79357bf4a7.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/rs-paging/r-ado-1b.rs-paging.726129e683.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/rs-cache-same-connection/r-ado-1b.rs-cache-same-connection.24054dcd7f.a1.json`. Discriminator for the earlier "others' writes invisible even after Requery" (`evidence/r-oledb-odbc/update-others-visible/r-oledb-odbc.update-others-visible.f1d10465be.a1.json`): a Requery issued after 8 s shows them, so that record is a lag, not a permanent invisibility (it issued its Requery without a pause).
- **Open**: the precise lag and whether Requery before 7 s shows the change (one-line: a pause sweep between 0 and 8 s); re-reading a visited cached row after a same-connection update.

### R-ADO-1 ADO-EDIT: edit states, automatic posting and cancel
- **Claim**: On a server keyset (optimistic) recordset an assigned Field.Value puts EditMode 1 and `Value` shows the proposal while `OriginalValue` keeps the stored value; `Update` returns EditMode 0 and OriginalValue becomes the new value; Move (MoveNext, MovePrevious) posts the edit; `CancelUpdate` restores; `AddNew`+`Update` yields the generated ID. On a client batch-optimistic recordset Find, Filter, Sort, Resync, MoveNext, MoveFirst, AddNew and Delete commit a pending edit locally, while `Requery`, `Close` and Clone-then-Close fail 0x800A0C93 and leave EditMode 1. `Update(name, value)` and `AddNew(name, value)` work; an unknown name or Empty gives 0x800A0CC1, a Missing name 0x800A0BB9, "abc" into a Long 0x80020005. A failed put (odd-byte text 0x80040E21, 256-character text, out-of-range numerics 0x8002000A) leaves the edit open; the following `Update` then fails ("Multiple-step OLE DB operation generated errors") without ending the recordset, and later rows write normally.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/ado-edit-fresh/r-fix-wire-ado.ado-edit-fresh.d0143e1f67.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/ado-write-values-numeric/r-fix-wire-ado.ado-write-values-numeric.2dd7d13095.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/ado-write-values-text-binary/r-fix-wire-ado.ado-write-values-text-binary.b6e3456443.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/edit-pending-ops/r-ado-1b.edit-pending-ops.b69e9de040.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/edit-update-args/r-ado-1b.edit-update-args.645de455f0.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/fields-assign-conversion/r-ado-1b.fields-assign-conversion.8d73c73cc5.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/rs-readonly-mode-edit/r-ado-1b.rs-readonly-mode-edit.79357bf4a7.a1.json`.
- **Open**: `Update(array, array)` forms (builder has no array argument, T); edits during events.
- **Built (`w15-ado-batch-err-schema`, `d2b21142`, 2026-11-27):** client immediate `(3,3,3)` `Resync` flushes unposted edit before `TryResync` (`r-ado-1b/edit-pending-ops`), and server keyset `(2,1,3)` `adCmdText` omitted-arg `CancelBatch()` clears pending edit and `Connection.Errors` (`r-ado-1b/batch-cancel`).


### R-ADO-1 ADO-VALUES: value channels, conflict reporting and Update Criteria
- **Claim**: Server keyset optimistic, ID 1 set to 100, edited to 120 and changed to 110 by a second connection: before the conflict `Value` 120 and `OriginalValue` 100; `UnderlyingValue` is refused 0x800A0CB3 ("Current provider does not support refreshing underlying values") before and after Update and after CancelUpdate; `Update` fails 0x80040E21 (SQLState 3197, "you and another user are attempting to change the same data at the same time") and EditMode stays 1; after the failure `OriginalValue` reads 110 (the external value) while `Value` stays 120; `CancelUpdate` gives EditMode 0 with Value and OriginalValue 110; a following edit to 130 and `Update` succeeds (EditMode 0, Value and OriginalValue 130). Client static batch-optimistic, same sequence: `Update` is local (EditMode 1, Value 120, OriginalValue 100, UnderlyingValue 100); `UpdateBatch` fails 0x80040E38 (Microsoft Cursor Engine, "Row cannot be located for updating", native 32) with the channels unchanged; `Resync(adAffectAll, adResyncUnderlyingValues)` makes UnderlyingValue 110 (Value 120, OriginalValue 100); the second `UpdateBatch` then succeeds with Value, OriginalValue and UnderlyingValue 120; a later edit to 130 and `UpdateBatch` succeeds (all three 130). Update Criteria (read 2 = adCriteriaUpdCols by default, set 0 to 3 per recordset), with the same column changed externally (100 to 120 against external 110): 0 (key) overwrites without error (Value and OriginalValue 120); 1 (all columns), 2 (updated columns) and 3 (timestamp) fail 0x80040E38 and leave Value 120, OriginalValue 100. With a different column changed externally criteria 0 and 2 apply and 1 and 3 fail (earlier record). A row deleted externally fails 0x80004005 "Record is deleted" with Status 2 and Value/OriginalValue/UnderlyingValue as read.
- **Conditions**: ACCDB; the server and client runs are separate probes; the criteria values were asked one per fresh recordset and row.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/values-witness-keyset/r-q-ado-misc.values-witness-keyset.d13e93ae91.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/values-witness-client-batch/r-q-ado-misc.values-witness-client-batch.226dc9e511.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/values-witness-criteria/r-q-ado-misc.values-witness-criteria.93d8b10f55.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/batch-update-criteria/r-ado-1b.batch-update-criteria.8b6dda4158.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/values-keyset-conflict/r-ado-1b.values-keyset-conflict.38ab9f531d.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/batch-conflict-delete/r-ado-1b.batch-conflict-delete.1d4d89d6ea.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/batch-conflict-delete-readback/r-fix-wire-ado.batch-conflict-delete-readback.5b4b34d50a.a1.json`. Not used as evidence: the stored-value reads at the end of the new records (defect a, no pause) and the Field `Status` reads (defect b).
- **Open**: row-level `Recordset.Status` flags during these steps; stored values confirmed after a pause.

### R-ADO-1 ADO-BATCH: staged changes are all attempted; conflicts at first, middle and last row
- **Claim**: `UpdateBatch(adAffectAll)` attempts every staged row, in staging order, and reports a failure without stopping: with the first staged row conflicting (external change) rows 2 and 3 are stored (OriginalValue updated) and row 1 keeps Status 2050; a duplicate key in the middle row leaves rows 1 and 3 stored and row 2 unchanged (statuses: failing row 2, others 8); with the last staged row conflicting, rows 1 and 2 are stored (read by an independent connection) and row 3 fails 0x80040E38 (native 32). A deleted row fails 0x80004005 "Record is deleted". After `Resync` (underlying) the second UpdateBatch completes without error in each case (earlier records read the stored values through the same recordset; for the last-row case the independent reader still showed the external value, no pause, defect a). Referential failures give "related record is required" / "includes related records"; a duplicate resubmission gives -2147467259 and does not resend accepted rows; generated inserts and mixed staging orders ran without failing calls.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/batch-conflict-last/r-q-ado-misc.batch-conflict-last.db26b48c2f.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/batch-conflict-first/r-ado-1b.batch-conflict-first.b4ff3fae6a.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/batch-conflict-middle/r-ado-1b.batch-conflict-middle.7702a784fb.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/batch-fk-failure/r-ado-1b.batch-fk-failure.9933a7a86b.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/batch-resubmit/r-ado-1b.batch-resubmit.9f9796ac6b.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/batch-status-staging/r-ado-1b.batch-status-staging.28795ef6df.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/batch-mixed-generated/r-ado-1b.batch-mixed-generated.52031cc21f.a1.json`, `evidence/r-str-ado-act/ado-batch/r-str-ado-act.ado-batch.47c0bca9d0.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/batch-conflict-delete-readback/r-fix-wire-ado.batch-conflict-delete-readback.5b4b34d50a.a1.json`. Correction: the draft said "stops at the first failure"; `batch-conflict-first` (rows 2 and 3 stored after row 1 failed) and `ado-batch` (row 3 stored after row 2 failed) contradict that. Discriminator for "the failure is the reading connection": a fresh third connection and DAO read the data (`batch-conflict-delete-readback`).
- **Open**: row `Recordset.Status` per row for the last-row case (defect b); stored value of the resubmitted last row after a pause; I/O failure and cancellation (T).

### R-ADO-1 ADO-BATCH-SCOPE: adAffectCurrent, group by constant filter, Update Criteria, clones
- **Claim**: `UpdateBatch(adAffectCurrent)` submits only the current row: with rows 1 to 3 staged and the cursor on row 2 the pending-filter count afterwards is 2. `Filter = adFilterPendingRecords (1)` over a pending Delete, a pending update and a pending AddNew gives RecordCount 3 and `UpdateBatch(adAffectGroup)` then stores all three (row 4 gone, row 5 updated, new row present). A Clone shares the original's batch: an edit made through a clone (row 6, `Recordset.Status` 2 adRecModified on the clone) shows on the original (Value changed, OriginalValue the old value, Status 2, pending-filter count 1); `UpdateBatch(adAffectAll)` on the original stores it (an independent reader 8 s later reads it), and a later `UpdateBatch` on the clone raises no error; submitted from the clone first, the clone's Status becomes 8 (adRecUnmodified), the original shows Value = OriginalValue, Status 8, pending count 0, and the reader reads the stored value; the original's `UpdateBatch` afterwards raises no error. The empty-group and string-filter refusals (0x800A0C93) and the Update Criteria matrix (0 key overwrites, 1, 2, 3 fail 0x80040E38 on the same column) are in the earlier records and ADO-VALUES.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q2-ado-misc/batch-scope-clone-submit-original/r-q2-ado-misc.batch-scope-clone-submit-original.d3f4145afa.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q2-ado-misc/batch-scope-clone-submit-clone/r-q2-ado-misc.batch-scope-clone-submit-clone.66f5de3324.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/batch-scope-current/r-q-ado-misc.batch-scope-current.0644b593d8.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/batch-scope-pending-deletes/r-q-ado-misc.batch-scope-pending-deletes.8b3beda486.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/values-witness-criteria/r-q-ado-misc.values-witness-criteria.93d8b10f55.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/batch-scope-group-pending-constant/r-fix-wire-ado.batch-scope-group-pending-constant.edad1c330c.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/batch-scope-group-string-filter/r-fix-wire-ado.batch-scope-group-string-filter.a8203914f2.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/batch-scope-group-unposted-edit/r-fix-wire-ado.batch-scope-group-unposted-edit.390bbf0159.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/batch-update-criteria/r-ado-1b.batch-update-criteria.8b6dda4158.a1.json`. ### R-ADO-1 (`r-q48-ado-batch-edits`, `W-09/edit-client-batch`): client batch properties, MarshalOptions, page-0 cadence, scopes, Resync, Update Criteria & transaction interactions
- **Claim**: Client `(3,3,4)` `Properties.Count` is `103`; `Update Criteria` defaults to `2` (`0..3` valid; `-1`/`4` refuse `0x80040E21`, `"bad"` refuses `0x800A0D5D`); `Update Resync` defaults to `1` (`0..15` bitmask valid; `-1`/`16` refuse `0x80040E21`); `Status` at BOF/EOF refuses `0x800A0BCD`. `MarshalOptions` defaults to `0` (`adMarshalAll`), closed Put refuses `0x800A0E78`, open `(3,3,4)`/`(3,3,1)` accept `0`/`1` (`2`/`-1` refuse `0x800A0BB9`, `"bad"` `0x80020005`), `Clone` inherits independently, and the property survives `Close`; server `(2,1,3)` Get=`0` and Put refuses `0x800A0CB3`. Deferred `(3,3,4)` writes `0` bytes locally during `Update`/`AddNew`/`Delete`, then `UpdateBatch` of 3 staged operations steps `0xE02` (`page 0 offset 3586`) `00 -> 02` at return, `-> 03` after a 1500 ms pause, and `0` at `Close` on both ACCDB and Jet 4 MDB (while `(3,3,3)` commits per operation `03 -> 04 -> 05 -> 06` after pause). `EditMode` remains `1` (`Status=2`) after local `Update` + navigation (`CancelUpdate` restores `EditMode=0, Status=8`), and AutoNumber `ID` populates at `UpdateBatch`. `Delete()`/`Delete(1)` sets `Status=4` (`RecordCount -1`) or `262144` on pending `AddNew`, while `Delete(2)`/`Delete(3)` refuse `0x800A0C93`. `CancelBatch()` defaults to `3`; `CancelBatch(1)` restores deleted rows via `adFilterPendingRecords` (`4 -> 8`, `RecordCount +1`), drops `AddNew` (`262144`), and no-ops clean rows; `CancelBatch(2)` succeeds on non-empty `adFilterConflictingRecords` and refuses none/empty/string filters (`0x800A0C93`). `Filter`+`Sort`+`Find` compose and live edits to sorted columns reposition rows; `adResyncUnderlyingValues (1)` refreshes `UnderlyingValue` on pending-modified rows (`Status=2`) without needing a prior failed `UpdateBatch` while skipping clean rows (`Status=8`). Multi-row conflicts populate one `Connection.Errors` entry per failing row, switching `Filter` to `0/1/2/3` clears `adFilterConflictingRecords`, local `Delete` under `Update Criteria=2` conflicts only on external `DELETE`, and Jet 4 MDB `Update Criteria` `0..3` matches ACCDB. Inside `BeginTrans`, `UpdateBatch` writes `0` bytes; `CommitTrans` coalesces into `+1` `0xE02` on ACCDB and Jet 4 MDB; `RollbackTrans` writes `0` bytes and leaves client cache until `Requery` (invalidating held `Field` `0x800A0D5C`, and `Resync(3,2)` fails `0x80040E23` on rolled-back insert). (`Update(array, array)` and bookmark-array `Filter` remain tooling-gated for SAFEARRAY args; explicit `Set rs.ActiveConnection = Nothing` awaits `NullObjectArg` support in `Arg`.)
- **Records**: `tools/CalcDb.Probes/Cards/RQ48AdoBatchEdits.cs`, `probes/r-q48-ado-batch-edits/*.question.json` (30 Part A probes + `.a2` repeats in `evidence/r-q48-ado-batch-edits/`, incl. `client-props-inventory-accdb`, `client-marshal-options-{accdb,mdb}`, `client-deferred-vs-immediate-{accdb,mdb}`, `client-addnew-channels-status-accdb`, `client-batch-pending-ops-accdb`, `batch-delete-scopes-{accdb,mdb}`, `batch-cancel-scopes-accdb`, `conflict-update-delete-mixed-accdb`, `conflict-update-criteria-mdb`, `txn-updatebatch-{commit,rollback}-*`, `txn-updatebatch-partial-rollback-accdb`).
- **Open**: ready gate `wzq4` closed.
- **Built (`wzq4`, `6ebdcbac`, 2026-11-26)**: `CalcDb.Com.Ado` client-cursor batch `(3,3,4)` and immediate `(3,3,3)` parity implemented on ACCDB & Jet 4 MDB (`AdoClientBatch.cs`, `AceProperties.cs`, `Recordset.cs`, `AdoSql.cs`, `AdoView.cs`, `AdoClientBatchEditTests.cs`); verified `319/319` Com tests and candidate compares (`r-q48-ado-batch-edits` `4/32` full + `26/28` COM-call match, `r-ado-1b` `20/62`, `r-q-ado-misc` `4/40`, `r-q2-ado-misc` `3/24`). Mid-open `nativeRead` `w32=32` diffs remain under `gp86` (`SessionFile` Step-1 exclusive share); unrecorded shapes (`Clone(LockType != -1)`, client `Delete`/failed `Resync` with event sinks, client batch `3314`/`3316`/`3218`, `Resync(..., 1)` on externally deleted row) stay refused by name.

### R-ADO-1 ADO-TXN: connection-level nesting, rollback scope, retaining
- **Claim**: `BeginTrans` returns the nesting level (1, 2, 3); an inner commit is undone by an outer rollback; a final Commit/Rollback with no transaction fails 0x8004D00E; Connection.Execute, Command.Execute, keyset Update and client UpdateBatch all revert on RollbackTrans, DDL (CREATE/ALTER/INSERT) included; `IsolationLevel = 1048576` is accepted but the next BeginTrans fails 0x8004D008 and later work autocommits; held server and client recordsets stay State 1 across commit and rollback (Preserve on Commit true; Preserve on Abort false for the two server cursors, true for client static); with Attributes 393216 the transaction stays open after commit or rollback and `Close` fails 0x800A0CAE; after rollback a keyset shows the stored row and `Requery` restores client caches; `Resync` is refused 0x800A0CB3 on keyset and server static recordsets and works on client ones.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-ado-1b/txn-nesting-three/r-ado-1b.txn-nesting-three.00ca433b23.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/txn-rollback-scope-objects/r-ado-1b.txn-rollback-scope-objects.64de5c2b49.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/txn-client-cache-rollback/r-ado-1b.txn-client-cache-rollback.ed5b011515.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/txn-client-cache-rollback-distinct-rows/r-fix-wire-ado.txn-client-cache-rollback-distinct-rows.ce457accdf.a1.json`, `evidence/r-str-ado-act/ado-txn/r-str-ado-act.ado-txn.eb75f1792d.a1.json`, `evidence/r-oledb-odbc/txn-held-rows/r-oledb-odbc.txn-held-rows.91a0129038.a1.json`, `evidence/r-oledb-odbc/txn-ddl-rollback/r-oledb-odbc.txn-ddl-rollback.b6e0df0330.a1.json`.
- **Open**: failed successor creation on a retaining route; clone closure.
- **Built (`w15-ado-batch-err-schema`, `d2b21142`, 2026-11-27):** server keyset `(2,1,3)` at EOF during outer `RollbackTrans` truncates after `MoveFirst()` until `Requery()`, and server static `(2,3,1)` `Requery()` materializes a `MemoryRowSource` snapshot (`r-fix-wire-ado/txn-client-cache-rollback-distinct-rows`, `r-ado-1b/txn-client-cache-rollback`).


### R-ADO-1 ADO-VIEW: Filter and Sort grammar, LIKE wildcards, Sort clearing
- **Claim**: Filter constants 0, 1, 2 and 3 work (RecordCount 7, 3, 0 and 1 for the fixture), "" matches all, and 99, -1, "1", Empty, Null, R8 1 and BOOL -1 fail 0x800A0BB9. String filters accept `>`, AND, OR, `<>`, `[S]`, case-insensitive equality and `LIKE`; NOT, parentheses, IN, BETWEEN, `==`, `!=`, UPPER() and arithmetic are rejected 0x800A0BB9; `L = NULL` matches Null rows and `L IS NULL` fails. LIKE: `*` and `%` are both wildcards and behave the same (`T LIKE 'a*'` and `'a%'` give 2 rows, `'*a*'` and `'%a%'` give 9), and a wildcard must be trailing or on both ends: a leading-only pattern (`'%a'`, `'%1'`, `'*1'`) is rejected 0x800A0BB9 whichever wildcard it uses. This resolves the draft's contradiction: `ado-view` used a trailing `%`, `view-filter-operators` a leading-only `'%a'`. Other pattern characters: `'alph?'` matched no row and `'alph_'` matched 2 rows (alpha and alpha2; its mechanism was not separated). Sort: unknown field 0x800A0CC1, "L DESC DESC" 0x800A0BB9, Memo and Binary fields 0x80040E60, trailing comma, lower case and `[S]` accepted; `Sort "L DESC, ID"` returns IDs 4, 8, 3, 7, 2, 6, 10, 1, 5, 9 and `Sort ""` restores the original order 1 to 10; `Sort` fails 0x800A0CB3 on every server cursor and on Execute results while Filter works on all; `Find` with `ID = = 3` fails and leaves the position.
- **Conditions**: client static recordset of K (10 rows) for the new records, ACCDB.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/view-like-percent-sort-clear/r-q-ado-misc.view-like-percent-sort-clear.6c886a25ff.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/view-filter-constants/r-ado-1b.view-filter-constants.68a67ea2d6.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/view-filter-operators/r-ado-1b.view-filter-operators.1e0ea81dbc.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/view-filter-literals/r-ado-1b.view-filter-literals.f7f5edb1f8.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/view-sort-grammar/r-ado-1b.view-sort-grammar.5a49f6f4bf.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/view-sort-server/r-ado-1b.view-sort-server.09ed08dce8.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/view-find-client/r-ado-1b.view-find-client.d7c719d9b9.a1.json`, `evidence/r-str-ado-act/ado-view/r-str-ado-act.ado-view.d257b3e165.a1.json`.
- **Open**: why `'alph_'` matches two rows; bookmark-array filters (array argument: T).

### R-ADO-1 ADO-BOOK (part): bookmark forms, client-static exchange, positions
- **Claim**: Forward-only recordsets do not support bookmarks (`Supports(adBookmark)` 0, Bookmark 0x800A0CB3, `MoveLast` 0x80040E24, AbsolutePosition and RecordCount -1). Keyset Bookmark is VT_R8 (512, 513, ... 518 at rows 1, 2, 7 of the earlier fixture); static Bookmarks were VT_R8 1, 2, 7 equal to the row position in the earlier fixture. Past the end AbsolutePosition is -3 and Bookmark 0x800A0BCD; before the start -2. Assigning a Long, String, Empty or Null bookmark fails 0x800A0BB9, a 4-byte array and R8 2 fail 0x80040E0E "Not a valid bookmark" on keyset. A Clone starts at row 1 with Filter 0 and Sort ""; after the parent is filtered to one row the clone still has RecordCount 4. **Client static runtime exchange** (K, `ID <= 6 ORDER BY ID`): Bookmark R8 3 selects ID 3 on the recordset, on its Clone and on an independent recordset of the same text; `CompareBookmarks(R8 1, R8 3)` is 0 (less) and `(R8 3, R8 3)` is 1 (equal); after a pending Delete of row 2 and UpdateBatch, R8 3 still selects ID 3 (the bookmark follows the row); after `Requery` R8 3 selects ID 4 (positions are renumbered). On a **server static** read-only recordset of the same query the constants R8 1 to 3 are refused 0x80040E0E on the recordset, its Clone and an independent recordset, and `CompareBookmarks(R8 1, R8 3)` returns 3 (not comparable): server static bookmark values are not the row position here.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q2-ado-misc/book-static-exchange/r-q2-ado-misc.book-static-exchange.76665fef5d.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/book-position-readback/r-fix-wire-ado.book-position-readback.80fff259e9.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/book-raw-forms/r-ado-1b.book-raw-forms.30a727ca1b.a1.json`, `evidence/r-str-ado-act/ado-view/r-str-ado-act.ado-view.d257b3e165.a1.json`. The correction in item (6) above: the draft statement that server static bookmarks equal the position holds for the earlier fixture only.
- **Still T (one line)**: server static and keyset bookmark exchange needs the value read from a recordset passed back as a runtime argument (constants from another fixture are refused), and bookmark-array filters need an array argument.
- **Built (`w14-ado-session-book-meta`, `5ff0eb36`, 2026-11-27)**: `Recordset.Bookmark` get/put (`0x800A0BCD` at BOF/EOF, `0x800A0BB9` invalid arg types, `0x80040E0E` invalid server bookmarks), `CompareBookmarks`, and server-static `Clone` in `CalcDb.Com.Ado` (`r-ado-1b/book-raw-forms`, `r-fix-wire-ado/book-position-readback`, `r-str-ado-act/ado-view`, `r-q2-ado-misc/book-static-exchange`); mutated/filtered server keyset `Bookmark` and unrecorded valid keyset `Put("Bookmark", 512+i)` stay refused by name (`Still T`).
- **Built (`w22-com-ado-dao-core`, `68cda61d`, 2026-11-28)**: Server-static (`CursorType=3`) failed `Bookmark` put (`3159`/`0x80040E0E`) makes `Field.Value` read `VT_EMPTY` without altering `ActualSize` until cleared (`r-q2-ado-misc/book-static-exchange` #125..#190).

### R-ADO-1 ADO-RESYNC: Resync scope and values on a client batch recordset
- **Claim**: On a client static batch-optimistic recordset, after another connection updated ID 1, deleted ID 2 and inserted a row (8 s earlier): the recordset still shows the old rows; `Resync(adAffectAll, adResyncAllValues)` fails 0x80040E23 (Microsoft Cursor Engine, "Key value for this row was changed or deleted at the data store. The local row is now deleted"), yet the existing rows are refreshed (ID 1 reads 777) and the deleted row is dropped (RecordCount 9); the inserted row is not discovered; `Requery` discovers it (RecordCount 10) and `Fields.Refresh` leaves Count 4. Scope matrix (external update of IDs 1 to 4, 8 s, cursor on row 2): `Resync(adAffectCurrent, adResyncUnderlyingValues)` changes no Value; `Resync(adAffectCurrent, adResyncAllValues)` refreshes row 2 only; `Resync(adAffectGroup, ...)` without a Filter fails 0x800A0C93 and changes nothing; `Resync(adAffectAll, adResyncAllValues)` refreshes all rows. **adResyncUnderlyingValues refreshes nothing observable on its own**: on a clean row (`Recordset.Status` 8) after an external update (701, then 702) Value, OriginalValue and UnderlyingValue stay 10 after `Resync(adAffectAll, 1)` and `Resync(adAffectCurrent, 1)`, with an 8 s pause and with none (four observations); on a row with a pending edit (Status 2, value 555, external 703) the three channels stay 555/20/20 after `Resync(adAffectAll, 1)`. The one record where it moved UnderlyingValue (to the external 110) had a failed `UpdateBatch` before the Resync (values-witness-client-batch): the difference between the records is that conflict state; that it is the trigger was not tested directly. Earlier: on keyset and server static recordsets Resync fails 0x800A0CB3 and on a client static read-only recordset 0x80004005 "Insufficient base table information".
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q2-ado-misc/resync-underlying-clean-row/r-q2-ado-misc.resync-underlying-clean-row.8865e92bc7.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/resync-client-external/r-q-ado-misc.resync-client-external.48ce593a95.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/resync-affect-matrix/r-q-ado-misc.resync-affect-matrix.807bb2db9e.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/values-witness-client-batch/r-q-ado-misc.values-witness-client-batch.226dc9e511.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/rs-cache-same-connection/r-ado-1b.rs-cache-same-connection.24054dcd7f.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/txn-client-cache-rollback-distinct-rows/r-fix-wire-ado.txn-client-cache-rollback-distinct-rows.ce457accdf.a1.json`.
- **Open**: whether a prior failed UpdateBatch is what lets adResyncUnderlyingValues refresh UnderlyingValue (one-line follow-up).

### R-ADO-1 ADO-SCHEMA: field metadata, refresh after DDL and schema routes
- **Claim**: ADO publishes ACE field types as: Long 3, Text 202 (size 10/255), Double 5, Currency 6, Date 7, Memo 203 (536870910), Binary 205 / 204, Yes/No 11, Short 2, Byte 17, Single 4, GUID 72, Decimal 131 (precision 38 for DAO-made fields, as declared for DDL columns). Decimal expressions: N, N+1, N+N, N/2, -N are adNumeric 131 (precision 18, scale 4); `N*1.5` is adDouble; Sum(N) precision 28 scale 4; Count(N) adInteger. After `ALTER TABLE ADD COLUMN` with the table closed, a retained Fields collection reports the old count until Requery (client cursor: 5 after Refresh, 6 after Requery; on a keyset the ALTER itself fails 0x80040E14 "could not lock table" while the recordset is open); a new recordset sees the column. `Connection.OpenSchema(id)` with the restrictions argument omitted succeeds for 16 SchemaEnum values: 4, 5, 6, 8, 9, 10, 12, 16, 19, 20, 22, 23, 27, 28, 30 (67 rows) and 31 (16 rows), all forward-only (RecordCount -1) except those two; 0, 1, 2, 3, 7, 11, 13, 14, 15, 17, 18, 21, 24, 25, 26, 29 and 32 to 41 fail 0x800A0CB3 and 9999 fails 0x800A0BB9.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/schema-ids-no-restrictions/r-q-ado-misc.schema-ids-no-restrictions.f1f79a0408.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/fields-meta-mdb/r-ado-1b.fields-meta-mdb.b93c565d13.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/fields-meta-decimal-expressions/r-fix-wire-ado.fields-meta-decimal-expressions.a7d2aad72e.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/fields-refresh-ddl-closed/r-fix-wire-ado.fields-refresh-ddl-closed.2b8ac47651.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/fields-refresh-ddl-client-only/r-fix-wire-ado.fields-refresh-ddl-client-only.27000fbbd5.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/fields-refresh-ddl-keyset-only/r-fix-wire-ado.fields-refresh-ddl-keyset-only.fdd76b6a40.a1.json`, `evidence/r-str-ado-act/ado-schema/r-str-ado-act.ado-schema.a2208b3039.a1.json`. Discriminator: the earlier "every other id refused" (`evidence/r-oledb-odbc/schema-others/r-oledb-odbc.schema-others.4ef914e3e7.a1.json`) passed scalar restrictions; without them ids 5, 8, 9, 10, 28, 30 and 31 are supported, so that earlier list is superseded.
- **Open**: restriction arrays and restriction positions (array argument: T); SchemaEnum names are from the ADO type library, not tabulated in these records.
- **Built (`w14-ado-session-book-meta`, `5ff0eb36`, 2026-11-27)**: `AdoFieldValues.FromNativeDecimal` preserves stored `-0` sign (`0x80`) on Precision-38/Scale-0 server cursors and normalizes `-0` to `+0` (`0x00`) on client cursors (`r-ado-1b/fields-meta-mdb`, `r-str-ado-act/ado-rs-read-close`).
- **Built (`w22-com-ado-dao-core`, `68cda61d`, 2026-11-28)**: `AdoFieldDescriptor.FromSelectColumn` maps `Now()` to `adDate(7)` (`DefinedSize=8`, `Prec=255`, `Scale=255`, `Attr=114`) while execution remains refused in `CalcDb.Expressions` (`r-ado-1b/fields-meta-expressions`); `Connection.Execute` held ACCDB depth-0 single-column `TEXT` `ALTER TABLE ADD COLUMN` returns `3211`/`0x80040E14` (`fields-refresh-ddl`, `fields-refresh-ddl-keyset-only`); client static `LockType=1` `Resync(3,2)` returns `0x80004005` `Microsoft Cursor Engine` without `IErrorInfo` (`rs-cache-same-connection`).
- **Built (`w23-com-dao-ado-near`, `369144e0`, 2026-11-28):** ADO `UpdateBatch` `ApplyUpdate` on externally deleted row returns `0x80004005` (`"Record is deleted."`, `5003167`, `SQLState="3167"`, `IidCommand`) preserving `Status=2` and allowing non-failing rows to commit (`r-ado-1b/batch-conflict-delete`, `r-fix-wire-ado/batch-conflict-delete-readback`); ADO `Recordset.Requery` supports server keyset `(2,1,1)` & client static `(3,3,1)` (`r-ado-1b/{rs-cache-same-connection,fields-refresh-ddl}`, `r-fix-wire-ado/fields-refresh-ddl-{keyset,client}-only`); and closed non-clone `adCmdText` `Recordset.Fields` refreshes after `Connection` DDL (`r-fix-wire-ado/fields-refresh-ddl-closed`), cleared on `Open()` / `ActiveConnection` change.

### R-ADO-1 ADO-RECORD: Record.Open forms on the ACE provider

No URL, file or network source was asked (scope).
